# Expansion set. Same format as words.txt:
# word | partOfSpeech | English definition | Tagalog translation | Tagalog definition | English example

# ---------------- Verb Tenses ----------------
present simple|noun|The tense used for habits, routines and general facts|pangkasalukuyang simple|Panahunang ginagamit sa ugali, gawi at katotohanan|We use the present simple in "She walks to work."
present continuous|noun|The tense for something happening right now|kasalukuyang nagaganap|Panahunang ginagamit sa kasalukuyang ginagawa|"She is walking" is present continuous.
present perfect|noun|The tense linking a past action to the present|perpektong kasalukuyan|Panahunang nag-uugnay sa nakaraan at kasalukuyan|"I have finished" is present perfect.
present perfect continuous|noun|The tense for an action that started in the past and still continues|tuloy-tuloy na perpektong kasalukuyan|Panahunan para sa kilos na nagsimula noon at tuloy pa rin|"She has been studying for hours."
past simple|noun|The tense for a finished action at a definite past time|pangnakaraang simple|Panahunan para sa tapos nang kilos sa nakaraan|"He walked home" is past simple.
past continuous|noun|The tense for an action in progress at a past moment|nagaganap sa nakaraan|Panahunan para sa kilos na nagaganap noon|"She was reading when I called."
past perfect|noun|The tense for an action completed before another past action|perpektong nakaraan|Panahunan para sa kilos na natapos bago ang iba pang nakaraan|"He had left before we arrived."
past perfect continuous|noun|The tense for a continuing action that ended before another past event|tuloy-tuloy na perpektong nakaraan|Panahunan para sa tuloy-tuloy na kilos bago ang ibang nakaraan|"They had been waiting for an hour."
future simple|noun|The tense for a decision or prediction about the future|pangkinabukasang simple|Panahunan para sa hinaharap na kilos|"I will call you tomorrow."
future continuous|noun|The tense for an action that will be in progress later|magaganap sa hinaharap|Panahunan para sa kilos na gaganap sa hinaharap|"This time tomorrow I will be flying."
future perfect|noun|The tense for something that will be finished by a future time|perpektong hinaharap|Panahunan para sa matatapos bago dumating ang takdang oras|"By June she will have graduated."
going to future|noun|A form used for plans and intentions already decided|balak na kinabukasan|Anyo para sa napagpasyahang balak|"We are going to move house."
used to|verb|A form describing a past habit that has stopped|dati|Anyo para sa dating gawi na tapos na|"I used to live in Cebu."
would|verb|A form for repeated past actions or polite requests|dati o pakiusap|Anyo para sa paulit-ulit na nakaraan o magalang na hiling|"Every summer we would visit the farm."
auxiliary verb|noun|A helping verb such as be, do or have|pantulong na pandiwa|Pandiwang tumutulong sa pangunahing pandiwa|In "she is running", is is an auxiliary verb.
modal verb|noun|A verb such as can, must or should that shows possibility or obligation|modal na pandiwa|Pandiwang nagpapakita ng posibilidad o obligasyon|Must is a modal verb in "You must go."
base form|noun|The simplest form of a verb, with no ending|payak na anyo|Pinakasimpleng anyo ng pandiwa|The base form of "walked" is "walk".
past participle|noun|The verb form used in perfect tenses and the passive|pandiwaring nakaraan|Anyong ginagamit sa perpekto at balintiyak|The past participle of "eat" is "eaten".
present participle|noun|The -ing form of a verb|pandiwaring kasalukuyan|Anyong -ing ng pandiwa|"Running" is the present participle of "run".
transitive verb|noun|A verb that takes a direct object|palipat na pandiwa|Pandiwang may tuwirang layon|"She kicked the ball" uses a transitive verb.
intransitive verb|noun|A verb that takes no direct object|katawaning pandiwa|Pandiwang walang tuwirang layon|"He slept" uses an intransitive verb.
linking verb|noun|A verb that joins a subject to a description|pandiwang pang-ugnay|Pandiwang nag-uugnay ng simuno at paglalarawan|"She seems tired" uses a linking verb.
imperative mood|noun|The verb form used to give orders or instructions|pautos na modo|Anyong ginagamit sa pag-uutos|"Close the door" is in the imperative mood.
subjunctive|noun|A verb form for wishes, suggestions or unreal situations|panubhunhitibo|Anyo para sa hiling o di-tunay na sitwasyon|"If I were you" uses the subjunctive.
conditional|noun|A form describing what would happen if something else were true|pasakali|Anyong naglalarawan ng mangyayari kung may kondisyon|"I would help if I could" is conditional.
first conditional|noun|A pattern for a likely future result|unang pasakali|Balangkas para sa malamang na kinalabasan|"If it rains, we will stay in."
second conditional|noun|A pattern for an unlikely or imaginary situation|ikalawang pasakali|Balangkas para sa di-malamang na sitwasyon|"If I won the lottery, I would travel."
third conditional|noun|A pattern for an imagined change to the past|ikatlong pasakali|Balangkas para sa haka-hakang pagbabago ng nakaraan|"If she had studied, she would have passed."
tag question|noun|A short question added to the end of a statement|tanong na pandugtong|Maikling tanong na idinudugtong sa pahayag|"You're coming, aren't you?"
question tag|noun|The short phrase that turns a statement into a question|pandugtong na tanong|Maikling parirala na ginagawang tanong ang pahayag|"It's cold, isn't it?" ends with a question tag.
reported speech|noun|Telling what somebody said without quoting them exactly|salaysay na pahayag|Pagsasabi ng sinabi ng iba nang hindi tuwiran|"She said she was tired" is reported speech.
aspect|noun|Whether an action is finished, repeated or ongoing|aspekto|Kung ang kilos ay tapos, paulit-ulit o nagpapatuloy|The perfect aspect shows a completed action.
finite verb|noun|A verb that changes with the subject and tense|pandiwang may banghay|Pandiwang nagbabago ayon sa simuno at panahunan|"Walks" is a finite verb; "to walk" is not.
regular verb|noun|A verb that forms its past tense with -ed|regular na pandiwa|Pandiwang gumagamit ng -ed sa nakaraan|"Play" is a regular verb: played.
irregular verb|noun|A verb whose past forms do not follow the usual pattern|di-regular na pandiwa|Pandiwang hindi sumusunod sa karaniwang anyo|"Go" is an irregular verb: went, gone.
phrasal verb|noun|A verb plus a particle whose meaning is not literal|pandiwang parirala|Pandiwa at kasamang salita na may bagong kahulugan|"Give up" is a phrasal verb.
stative verb|noun|A verb describing a state rather than an action|pandiwang pang-estado|Pandiwang naglalarawan ng kalagayan, hindi kilos|"Know" is a stative verb.
causative|noun|A structure showing that somebody arranges for something to be done|pasanhi|Balangkas na nagpapakita ng pinagawa|"I had my hair cut" is causative.
inversion|noun|Putting the verb before the subject for emphasis or questions|pagbabaligtad|Paglalagay ng pandiwa bago ang simuno|"Never have I seen such a thing."
ellipsis|noun|Leaving out words that the listener can easily supply|pag-aalis ng salita|Pag-alis ng salitang madaling maunawaan|"Want some?" uses ellipsis for "Do you want some?"
concord|noun|The matching of subject and verb forms|pagkakatugma|Pagtutugma ng simuno at pandiwa|Concord requires "she walks", not "she walk".
zero article|noun|Using no article before a noun|walang pantukoy|Paggamit ng walang pantukoy|"I like music" has a zero article.
double negative|noun|Using two negatives where one is expected|dobleng pagtanggi|Paggamit ng dalawang pagtanggi|"I don't know nothing" is a double negative.
split infinitive|noun|Putting a word between "to" and a verb|hating pawatas|Paglalagay ng salita sa pagitan ng to at pandiwa|"To boldly go" is a split infinitive.

# ---------------- Irregular Verbs ----------------
arise|verb|To happen or come into being (arose, arisen)|bumangon|Mangyari o lumitaw|Problems arose during the meeting.
awake|verb|To stop sleeping (awoke, awoken)|gumising|Magising sa tulog|She awoke before dawn.
bear|verb|To carry or endure something (bore, borne)|magtiis|Magdala o magtiis|He could not bear the noise.
beat|verb|To hit repeatedly or to win against (beat, beaten)|talunin|Hampasin o manalo laban sa iba|Our team beat theirs by two goals.
become|verb|To start to be something (became, become)|maging|Magsimulang maging isang bagay|She became a teacher last year.
begin|verb|To start (began, begun)|magsimula|Umpisahan ang isang bagay|The film begins at eight.
bend|verb|To curve something (bent, bent)|baluktutin|Gawing hindi tuwid|He bent the wire into a hook.
bet|verb|To risk money on a result (bet, bet)|pumusta|Magtaya ng pera sa kinalabasan|I bet ten pounds on the race.
bind|verb|To tie or fasten together (bound, bound)|itali|Igapos o pagsamahin|The book was bound in leather.
bite|verb|To cut with the teeth (bit, bitten)|kagatin|Putulin gamit ang ngipin|The dog bit the postman.
bleed|verb|To lose blood (bled, bled)|dumugo|Mawalan ng dugo|The cut bled for a minute.
blow|verb|To move air from the mouth or of wind (blew, blown)|hipan|Magpalabas ng hangin|The wind blew all night.
breed|verb|To keep animals to produce young (bred, bred)|magpalahi|Mag-alaga upang magparami|They breed horses on the farm.
bring|verb|To carry something to a place (brought, brought)|dalhin|Magdala patungo sa isang lugar|Please bring your notes tomorrow.
build|verb|To make by putting parts together (built, built)|magtayo|Gumawa sa pamamagitan ng pagbuo|They built the house in a year.
burn|verb|To be on fire or damage with fire (burnt, burnt)|masunog|Magliyab o masira sa apoy|The toast burnt in the pan.
burst|verb|To break open suddenly (burst, burst)|pumutok|Sumabog nang biglaan|The pipe burst in the cold.
cast|verb|To throw or to choose actors (cast, cast)|ihagis|Ihagis o pumili ng artista|She was cast as the lead.
catch|verb|To take hold of something moving (caught, caught)|salohin|Hulihin ang gumagalaw|He caught the ball easily.
choose|verb|To pick one from several (chose, chosen)|pumili|Kumuha ng isa sa marami|Choose whichever you prefer.
cling|verb|To hold on tightly (clung, clung)|kumapit|Humawak nang mahigpit|The child clung to her mother.
creep|verb|To move slowly and quietly (crept, crept)|gumapang|Gumalaw nang dahan-dahan at tahimik|He crept up the stairs.
deal|verb|To handle or trade in something (dealt, dealt)|harapin|Asikasuhin o magkalakal|She dealt with the complaint calmly.
dig|verb|To make a hole in the ground (dug, dug)|humukay|Gumawa ng butas sa lupa|They dug a well behind the house.
draw|verb|To make a picture with lines (drew, drawn)|gumuhit|Gumawa ng larawan gamit ang linya|He drew a map for us.
drive|verb|To operate a vehicle (drove, driven)|magmaneho|Magpatakbo ng sasakyan|She drives to work every day.
dwell|verb|To live in a place (dwelt, dwelt)|manirahan|Tumira sa isang lugar|They dwelt in the valley for years.
feed|verb|To give food to (fed, fed)|pakainin|Magbigay ng pagkain|We feed the chickens at dawn.
flee|verb|To run away from danger (fled, fled)|tumakas|Tumakbo palayo sa panganib|The family fled the flood.
fling|verb|To throw with force (flung, flung)|ipukol|Ihagis nang malakas|He flung his bag on the bed.
forbid|verb|To order somebody not to do something (forbade, forbidden)|ipagbawal|Utusang huwag gawin|The rules forbid smoking here.
forgive|verb|To stop being angry with somebody (forgave, forgiven)|magpatawad|Tigilan ang galit sa iba|She forgave him at once.
freeze|verb|To turn to ice or stop moving (froze, frozen)|magyelo|Maging yelo o huminto|The lake froze overnight.
grind|verb|To crush into powder (ground, ground)|gilingin|Durugin hanggang maging pulbos|They grind the beans fresh.
hang|verb|To fix something at the top so it hangs down (hung, hung)|isabit|Ilagay upang nakalawit|Hang your coat by the door.
hide|verb|To put out of sight (hid, hidden)|magtago|Ilagay sa hindi makikita|He hid the key under a stone.
hold|verb|To have in your hands (held, held)|hawakan|Panatilihin sa kamay|Hold this for a moment.
hurt|verb|To cause pain (hurt, hurt)|masaktan|Magdulot ng sakit|My knee hurts when I run.
kneel|verb|To go down on your knees (knelt, knelt)|lumuhod|Ibaba ang tuhod sa lupa|She knelt to tie her shoe.
lead|verb|To guide or be in charge (led, led)|pamunuan|Manguna o mamahala|She led the team to victory.
lean|verb|To bend or rest against something (leant, leant)|sumandal|Ikiling o sumandig|He leant against the wall.
leap|verb|To jump a long way (leapt, leapt)|lumundag|Tumalon nang malayo|The cat leapt onto the roof.
lend|verb|To give something for a short time (lent, lent)|magpahiram|Ipagamit pansamantala|Can you lend me a pen?
light|verb|To make something burn or give light (lit, lit)|sindihan|Pagliyabin o bigyan ng liwanag|She lit the candles.
mean|verb|To have a particular sense (meant, meant)|ibig sabihin|Magkaroon ng tiyak na kahulugan|What does this word mean?
mislead|verb|To give somebody a wrong idea (misled, misled)|iligaw|Magbigay ng maling ideya|The advert misled customers.
pay|verb|To give money for something (paid, paid)|magbayad|Magbigay ng pera bilang kabayaran|I paid for the tickets.
prove|verb|To show that something is true (proved, proven)|patunayan|Ipakitang totoo|The results proved him right.
quit|verb|To leave a job or stop doing something (quit, quit)|umalis|Iwanan ang trabaho o itigil|He quit smoking last year.
rid|verb|To free from something unwanted (rid, rid)|alisin|Palayain sa bagay na ayaw|We rid the garden of weeds.
ring|verb|To make a bell sound or to telephone (rang, rung)|tumunog|Magpatunog o tumawag|The phone rang twice.
seek|verb|To try to find or obtain (sought, sought)|hanapin|Subukang makita o makuha|They sought advice from a lawyer.
sew|verb|To join cloth with a needle (sewed, sewn)|manahi|Pagdugtungin ang tela gamit ang karayom|She sewed a button on.
shake|verb|To move quickly from side to side (shook, shaken)|kalugin|Igalaw nang mabilis|Shake the bottle before use.
shine|verb|To give out light (shone, shone)|magningning|Maglabas ng liwanag|The sun shone all day.
shoot|verb|To fire a weapon (shot, shot)|barilin|Magpaputok ng sandata|He shot at the target.
shrink|verb|To become smaller (shrank, shrunk)|umurong|Maging mas maliit|The shirt shrank in the wash.
sink|verb|To go down below the surface (sank, sunk)|lumubog|Bumaba sa ilalim|The boat sank in the storm.
slide|verb|To move smoothly over a surface (slid, slid)|dumausdos|Gumalaw nang madulas|The book slid off the desk.
sow|verb|To plant seeds (sowed, sown)|maghasik|Magtanim ng buto|They sow wheat in autumn.
spill|verb|To let liquid fall out (spilt, spilt)|matapon|Mabuhos ang likido|I spilt coffee on my notes.
spin|verb|To turn round quickly (spun, spun)|umikot|Umikot nang mabilis|The wheel spun freely.
split|verb|To divide into parts (split, split)|hatiin|Bahagihin|We split the bill evenly.
spread|verb|To cover a wider area (spread, spread)|kumalat|Sumakop ng mas malawak|The news spread quickly.
steal|verb|To take without permission (stole, stolen)|magnakaw|Kumuha nang walang pahintulot|Someone stole my bike.
stick|verb|To fix with glue or become fixed (stuck, stuck)|idikit|Ikabit gamit ang pandikit|Stick the label on the box.
sting|verb|To hurt with a sharp point (stung, stung)|kagatin|Manakit gamit ang tulis|A bee stung my arm.
strike|verb|To hit or to stop work in protest (struck, struck)|hampasin|Sumuntok o magwelga|Lightning struck the tower.
swear|verb|To promise seriously or use rude words (swore, sworn)|manumpa|Mangakong taimtim o magmura|He swore to tell the truth.
sweep|verb|To clean with a brush (swept, swept)|magwalis|Maglinis gamit ang walis|She swept the floor.
swell|verb|To become larger (swelled, swollen)|mamaga|Lumaki ang sukat|His ankle swelled after the fall.
swing|verb|To move back and forth (swung, swung)|umugoy|Gumalaw pabalik-balik|The gate swung open.
tear|verb|To pull apart into pieces (tore, torn)|punitin|Sirain sa paghila|She tore the letter in half.
throw|verb|To send through the air with your hand (threw, thrown)|ihagis|Ipadala sa hangin gamit ang kamay|Throw me the keys.
thrust|verb|To push suddenly and hard (thrust, thrust)|isaksak|Itulak nang bigla at malakas|He thrust the door open.
tread|verb|To walk or step on something (trod, trodden)|tapakan|Maglakad o yapakan|Do not tread on the grass.
undergo|verb|To experience something difficult (underwent, undergone)|sumailalim|Danasin ang mahirap na bagay|She underwent surgery last week.
uphold|verb|To support or maintain a decision (upheld, upheld)|panatilihin|Suportahan o ipagpatuloy|The court upheld the ruling.
weave|verb|To make cloth by crossing threads (wove, woven)|maghabi|Gumawa ng tela sa paghahabi|They weave baskets by hand.
weep|verb|To cry (wept, wept)|umiyak|Lumuha|She wept with relief.
wind|verb|To turn or twist something (wound, wound)|iikot|Pilipitin o paikutin|Wind the string round the post.

# ---------------- Prefixes & Suffixes ----------------
un-|prefix|Not, or the opposite of|hindi|Panlaping nangangahulugang hindi|Unhappy means not happy.
re-|prefix|Again, or back|muli|Panlaping nangangahulugang muli|To rewrite is to write again.
dis-|prefix|Not, or the reverse of|di-|Panlaping nagpapahiwatig ng kabaligtaran|Disagree means not to agree.
mis-|prefix|Wrongly or badly|mali|Panlaping nangangahulugang mali|To misspell is to spell wrongly.
pre-|prefix|Before|bago|Panlaping nangangahulugang bago|A preview happens before the main event.
post-|prefix|After|pagkatapos|Panlaping nangangahulugang pagkatapos|Postwar means after the war.
sub-|prefix|Under or below|ilalim|Panlaping nangangahulugang sa ilalim|A submarine travels under the sea.
super-|prefix|Above, beyond or very large|higit|Panlaping nangangahulugang higit|A supermarket is a very large market.
inter-|prefix|Between or among|pagitan|Panlaping nangangahulugang sa pagitan|International means between nations.
intra-|prefix|Within|loob|Panlaping nangangahulugang sa loob|Intracity travel stays within the city.
trans-|prefix|Across or through|tawid|Panlaping nangangahulugang patawid|To transport is to carry across.
anti-|prefix|Against or opposed to|laban|Panlaping nangangahulugang laban|An antiwar protest opposes war.
pro-|prefix|In favour of|panig|Panlaping nangangahulugang panig sa|Pro-reform groups support change.
non-|prefix|Not|hindi|Panlaping nangangahulugang hindi|A nonsmoker does not smoke.
over-|prefix|Too much or above|sobra|Panlaping nangangahulugang labis|To overeat is to eat too much.
under-|prefix|Too little or beneath|kulang|Panlaping nangangahulugang kulang|Underpaid means paid too little.
co-|prefix|Together or jointly|kasama|Panlaping nangangahulugang magkasama|A coauthor writes together with another.
ex-|prefix|Former, or out of|dating|Panlaping nangangahulugang dati|An ex-president used to be president.
semi-|prefix|Half or partly|kalahati|Panlaping nangangahulugang kalahati|A semicircle is half a circle.
multi-|prefix|Many|marami|Panlaping nangangahulugang marami|Multicoloured means having many colours.
mono-|prefix|One or single|isa|Panlaping nangangahulugang isa|A monologue is spoken by one person.
bi-|prefix|Two or twice|dalawa|Panlaping nangangahulugang dalawa|Bilingual means speaking two languages.
tri-|prefix|Three|tatlo|Panlaping nangangahulugang tatlo|A triangle has three sides.
auto-|prefix|Self or by itself|sarili|Panlaping nangangahulugang sarili|An autobiography is written about oneself.
micro-|prefix|Very small|napakaliit|Panlaping nangangahulugang napakaliit|A microchip is extremely small.
macro-|prefix|Large scale|malawak|Panlaping nangangahulugang malawakan|Macroeconomics studies the whole economy.
mega-|prefix|Very large|napakalaki|Panlaping nangangahulugang napakalaki|A megacity holds millions of people.
mini-|prefix|Small|maliit|Panlaping nangangahulugang maliit|A minibus carries few passengers.
tele-|prefix|At a distance|malayo|Panlaping nangangahulugang malayuan|Television sends pictures over a distance.
fore-|prefix|Before or in front|una|Panlaping nangangahulugang nauuna|To foresee is to see beforehand.
out-|prefix|Beyond or more than|higit|Panlaping nangangahulugang higit pa|To outrun is to run faster than another.
de-|prefix|To remove or reverse|alis|Panlaping nangangahulugang pag-alis|To defrost is to remove frost.
en-|prefix|To cause to be|maging|Panlaping nangangahulugang gawing|To enlarge is to make larger.
il-|prefix|Not, used before l|hindi|Panlaping hindi na ginagamit bago ang l|Illegal means not legal.
im-|prefix|Not, used before m and p|hindi|Panlaping hindi na ginagamit bago ang m at p|Impossible means not possible.
ir-|prefix|Not, used before r|hindi|Panlaping hindi na ginagamit bago ang r|Irregular means not regular.
in-|prefix|Not, or into|hindi|Panlaping nangangahulugang hindi o papasok|Incomplete means not complete.
-able|suffix|Able to be, forming adjectives|kaya|Hulaping nangangahulugang maaaring|Readable means able to be read.
-ible|suffix|Able to be, a variant of -able|kaya|Hulaping katulad ng -able|Visible means able to be seen.
-ful|suffix|Full of|puno|Hulaping nangangahulugang puno ng|Careful means full of care.
-less|suffix|Without|walang|Hulaping nangangahulugang wala|Hopeless means without hope.
-ness|suffix|The state of being, forming nouns|kalagayan|Hulaping bumubuo ng pangngalan|Kindness is the state of being kind.
-ment|suffix|The result or act of, forming nouns|pag-|Hulaping bumubuo ng pangngalan|Movement is the act of moving.
-tion|suffix|The action or result of, forming nouns|pag-|Hulaping bumubuo ng pangngalan|Creation is the act of creating.
-ity|suffix|The quality of, forming nouns|kalidad|Hulaping nagpapahayag ng katangian|Clarity is the quality of being clear.
-ly|suffix|In a certain manner, forming adverbs|nang|Hulaping bumubuo ng pang-abay|Quickly means in a quick manner.
-er|suffix|A person or thing that does something|tagapag-|Hulaping tumutukoy sa gumagawa|A teacher is one who teaches.
-or|suffix|A person or thing that does something|tagapag-|Hulaping katulad ng -er|An actor is one who acts.
-ist|suffix|A person who practises something|tagapag-|Hulaping tumutukoy sa nagsasagawa|A pianist plays the piano.
-ism|suffix|A belief or practice|-ismo|Hulaping tumutukoy sa paniniwala|Realism values things as they are.
-ish|suffix|Somewhat, or resembling|medyo|Hulaping nangangahulugang medyo|Greenish means somewhat green.
-ous|suffix|Having the quality of|may|Hulaping nangangahulugang may katangian|Dangerous means full of danger.
-ive|suffix|Having a tendency to|may hilig|Hulaping nagpapakita ng hilig|Creative means tending to create.
-al|suffix|Relating to, forming adjectives|may kaugnayan|Hulaping nangangahulugang may kinalaman|Musical relates to music.
-ic|suffix|Relating to, forming adjectives|may kaugnayan|Hulaping nangangahulugang may kinalaman|Historic relates to history.
-y|suffix|Full of or characterised by|puno ng|Hulaping nangangahulugang puno ng|Rainy means full of rain.
-en|suffix|To make, or made of|gawin|Hulaping nangangahulugang gawing|To darken is to make dark.
-ify|suffix|To make or become|gawing|Hulaping nangangahulugang gawing|To simplify is to make simple.
-ise|suffix|To make or become, British spelling|gawing|Hulaping nangangahulugang gawing|To modernise is to make modern.
-ship|suffix|A state or skill|kalagayan|Hulaping tumutukoy sa kalagayan|Friendship is the state of being friends.
-hood|suffix|A state or period of life|panahon|Hulaping tumutukoy sa yugto ng buhay|Childhood is the period of being a child.
-dom|suffix|A state or domain|kaharian|Hulaping tumutukoy sa kalagayan o nasasakupan|Freedom is the state of being free.
-ward|suffix|In the direction of|patungo|Hulaping nangangahulugang patungo|Homeward means towards home.
-wise|suffix|In the manner or direction of|paraan|Hulaping nangangahulugang sa paraang|Clockwise means in the direction of a clock.
-proof|suffix|Protected against|hindi tinatablan|Hulaping nangangahulugang protektado laban sa|Waterproof means protected against water.
-logy|suffix|The study of|pag-aaral|Hulaping tumutukoy sa pag-aaral|Biology is the study of life.
-graphy|suffix|Writing or recording about|pagsulat|Hulaping tumutukoy sa pagsulat|Geography describes the earth.
-phobia|suffix|An extreme fear of|takot|Hulaping tumutukoy sa matinding takot|Claustrophobia is fear of small spaces.

# ---------------- Word Roots ----------------
aud|noun|A root meaning to hear|dinig|Ugat na nangangahulugang pandinig|Audible and audience share the root aud.
bene|noun|A root meaning good or well|mabuti|Ugat na nangangahulugang mabuti|Benefit and benevolent share the root bene.
biblio|noun|A root meaning book|aklat|Ugat na nangangahulugang aklat|A bibliography lists books.
bio|noun|A root meaning life|buhay|Ugat na nangangahulugang buhay|Biology is the study of life.
chron|noun|A root meaning time|panahon|Ugat na nangangahulugang panahon|Chronological order follows time.
cred|noun|A root meaning to believe|paniwala|Ugat na nangangahulugang maniwala|Credible means believable.
dict|noun|A root meaning to say|sabi|Ugat na nangangahulugang magsabi|To dictate is to say aloud.
duc|noun|A root meaning to lead|akay|Ugat na nangangahulugang mangunahan|To conduct is to lead together.
fac|noun|A root meaning to make or do|gawa|Ugat na nangangahulugang gumawa|A factory is where things are made.
geo|noun|A root meaning earth|lupa|Ugat na nangangahulugang lupa|Geology studies the earth.
graph|noun|A root meaning to write|sulat|Ugat na nangangahulugang sumulat|Autograph means self-written.
ject|noun|A root meaning to throw|hagis|Ugat na nangangahulugang ihagis|To eject is to throw out.
jud|noun|A root meaning to judge|hatol|Ugat na nangangahulugang humatol|Judicial relates to judgement.
luc|noun|A root meaning light|liwanag|Ugat na nangangahulugang liwanag|Lucid means clear and bright.
man|noun|A root meaning hand|kamay|Ugat na nangangahulugang kamay|Manual work is done by hand.
mit|noun|A root meaning to send|padala|Ugat na nangangahulugang magpadala|To transmit is to send across.
mort|noun|A root meaning death|kamatayan|Ugat na nangangahulugang kamatayan|Mortal beings eventually die.
path|noun|A root meaning feeling or suffering|damdamin|Ugat na nangangahulugang damdamin|Sympathy is feeling with another.
ped|noun|A root meaning foot|paa|Ugat na nangangahulugang paa|A pedestrian travels on foot.
phon|noun|A root meaning sound|tunog|Ugat na nangangahulugang tunog|A telephone carries sound.
port|noun|A root meaning to carry|dala|Ugat na nangangahulugang magdala|To transport is to carry across.
scrib|noun|A root meaning to write|sulat|Ugat na nangangahulugang sumulat|To describe is to write about.
spec|noun|A root meaning to look|tingin|Ugat na nangangahulugang tumingin|To inspect is to look closely.
struct|noun|A root meaning to build|tayo|Ugat na nangangahulugang magtayo|To construct is to build.
tract|noun|A root meaning to pull or drag|hila|Ugat na nangangahulugang hilahin|A tractor pulls heavy loads.
vac|noun|A root meaning empty|walang laman|Ugat na nangangahulugang walang laman|A vacuum is an empty space.
ven|noun|A root meaning to come|dating|Ugat na nangangahulugang dumating|To convene is to come together.
vid|noun|A root meaning to see|kita|Ugat na nangangahulugang makita|Evidence is what can be seen.
voc|noun|A root meaning voice or call|tinig|Ugat na nangangahulugang tinig|To vocalise is to use the voice.
volv|noun|A root meaning to roll or turn|ikot|Ugat na nangangahulugang umikot|To revolve is to turn around.

# ---------------- Prepositions ----------------
above|preposition|In a higher position than something|sa itaas|Nasa mas mataas na posisyon|The lamp hangs above the table.
across|preposition|From one side to the other|patawid|Mula sa isang gilid patungo sa kabila|They walked across the bridge.
against|preposition|Touching, or in opposition to|laban sa|Nakadikit o salungat sa|He leaned against the wall.
along|preposition|Following the length of something|sa tabi|Sumusunod sa haba ng isang bagay|We strolled along the river.
amid|preposition|In the middle of|sa gitna|Nasa kalagitnaan ng|She stayed calm amid the noise.
among|preposition|Surrounded by, or in the group of|kabilang sa|Napapaligiran o kasama sa grupo|Divide the sweets among the children.
around|preposition|On every side of, or approximately|sa paligid|Sa palibot o humigit-kumulang|They sat around the fire.
behind|preposition|At the back of|sa likod|Nasa likuran ng|The garden is behind the house.
below|preposition|At a lower level than|sa ibaba|Nasa mas mababang antas|Temperatures fell below zero.
beneath|preposition|Directly under|sa ilalim|Nasa direktang ilalim|Treasure lay beneath the floor.
beside|preposition|Next to|sa tabi|Katabi ng|Sit beside me.
besides|preposition|In addition to|bukod sa|Karagdagan sa|Besides English, she speaks French.
between|preposition|In the space separating two things|sa pagitan|Nasa espasyo ng dalawang bagay|The shop is between the bank and the café.
beyond|preposition|On the far side of, or more than|lampas|Sa kabila o higit pa sa|The hills lie beyond the river.
despite|preposition|In spite of|sa kabila ng|Kahit na may hadlang|Despite the rain, we went out.
during|preposition|Throughout the time of|habang|Sa buong tagal ng|Stay quiet during the exam.
following|preposition|After|kasunod ng|Pagkatapos ng|Following the talk, there were questions.
inside|preposition|Within the limits of|sa loob|Sa loob ng hangganan|Wait inside the building.
into|preposition|Moving to the inside of|papasok sa|Papunta sa loob|She walked into the room.
near|preposition|Close to|malapit|Nasa malapit sa|We live near the station.
onto|preposition|Moving to a position on|papunta sa ibabaw|Papunta sa ibabaw ng|The cat jumped onto the shelf.
outside|preposition|Beyond the limits of|sa labas|Sa labas ng hangganan|Please wait outside the office.
over|preposition|Above, or across the top of|sa ibabaw|Sa itaas o patawid sa ibabaw|A bridge runs over the motorway.
past|preposition|Going by, or later than|lampas|Dumaraan o lampas sa|It is half past three.
regarding|preposition|About or concerning|hinggil sa|Tungkol sa|I wrote regarding your letter.
since|preposition|From a point in the past until now|mula noong|Mula sa nakaraan hanggang ngayon|She has lived here since 2010.
through|preposition|From one end or side to the other|sa pamamagitan|Mula sa isang dulo patungo sa kabila|We drove through the tunnel.
throughout|preposition|In every part of, or for the whole time|sa buong|Sa lahat ng bahagi o buong panahon|It rained throughout the night.
toward|preposition|In the direction of|patungo|Sa direksyon ng|He walked toward the gate.
towards|preposition|In the direction of, British spelling|patungo|Sa direksyon ng|She ran towards the exit.
underneath|preposition|Below or beneath|sa ilalim|Sa ibaba ng|The keys were underneath the mat.
unlike|preposition|Different from|hindi katulad|Iba sa|Unlike her brother, she enjoys maths.
until|preposition|Up to a particular time|hanggang|Hanggang sa takdang oras|Wait until Friday.
upon|preposition|On, in a formal sense|sa ibabaw|Sa ibabaw, sa pormal na paraan|Upon arrival, please sign in.
via|preposition|By way of|sa pamamagitan ng|Sa daan o paraang|We flew to Rome via Dubai.
within|preposition|Inside the limits of|sa loob ng|Sa loob ng hangganan o panahon|Reply within seven days.
without|preposition|Not having|walang|Kulang o walang|He left without his coat.

# ---------------- Articles & Determiners ----------------
definite article|noun|The word "the", pointing to something specific|tiyak na pantukoy|Pantukoy na tumutukoy sa tiyak na bagay|"The book" uses the definite article.
indefinite article|noun|The words "a" and "an", used for something general|di-tiyak na pantukoy|Pantukoy para sa pangkalahatang bagay|"A book" uses the indefinite article.
quantifier|noun|A word showing how much or how many|pananda ng dami|Salitang nagpapakita ng dami|"Many" and "some" are quantifiers.
demonstrative|noun|A word such as this or those that points something out|pamatlig|Salitang nagtuturo sa bagay|"This" and "that" are demonstratives.
possessive|noun|A form showing that something belongs to somebody|paari|Anyong nagpapakita ng pagmamay-ari|"Her book" uses a possessive.
each|determiner|Every one of two or more, considered separately|bawat isa|Bawat isa, isa-isang tinitingnan|Each student received a copy.
either|determiner|One or the other of two|alinman sa dalawa|Isa sa dalawang pagpipilian|Either answer is acceptable.
neither|determiner|Not one and not the other|wala sa dalawa|Hindi ang isa at hindi rin ang isa|Neither option suited us.
several|determiner|More than two but not many|ilan|Higit sa dalawa ngunit hindi marami|Several people complained.
enough|determiner|As much as is needed|sapat|Kasing dami ng kailangan|We have enough chairs.
plenty|noun|A large enough amount|marami|Sapat at higit pa ang dami|There is plenty of time.
few|determiner|A small number, suggesting not many|kakaunti|Maliit na bilang|Few students failed.
little|determiner|A small amount of something uncountable|kaunti|Maliit na dami ng di-mabilang|There is little milk left.
much|determiner|A large amount of something uncountable|marami|Malaking dami ng di-mabilang|How much water do we need?
whole|determiner|Complete, with no part missing|buo|Kumpleto at walang kulang|She read the whole book.

# ---------------- Food & Drink ----------------
appetite|noun|The desire to eat|gana|Pagnanais na kumain|Exercise gave him a good appetite.
appetiser|noun|A small dish served before the main course|pampagana|Maliit na putahe bago ang pangunahing pagkain|We shared an appetiser of olives.
beverage|noun|A drink of any kind|inumin|Anumang uri ng inumin|Hot beverages are served all day.
breakfast|noun|The first meal of the day|almusal|Unang pagkain sa maghapon|She eats breakfast at seven.
lunch|noun|The meal eaten in the middle of the day|tanghalian|Pagkain sa katanghalian|We had lunch in the park.
dinner|noun|The main meal of the evening|hapunan|Pangunahing pagkain sa gabi|Dinner is at eight tonight.
supper|noun|A light evening meal|meryenda sa gabi|Magaang pagkain sa gabi|They had soup for supper.
snack|noun|A small amount of food between meals|meryenda|Maliit na pagkain sa pagitan ng oras|Fruit makes a healthy snack.
meal|noun|An occasion when food is eaten|pagkain|Pagkakataon ng pagkain|We share three meals a day.
dish|noun|Food prepared in a particular way|putahe|Pagkaing inihanda sa tiyak na paraan|Adobo is a popular dish.
recipe|noun|A set of instructions for cooking something|resipe|Panuto sa pagluluto|The recipe calls for garlic.
ingredient|noun|One of the foods used to make a dish|sangkap|Isa sa mga bagay na ginagamit sa pagluluto|Salt is the simplest ingredient.
flavour|noun|The taste of a food|lasa|Panlasa ng pagkain|The soup has a rich flavour.
seasoning|noun|Salt, pepper or herbs added to food|pampalasa|Asin o pampalasa na idinaragdag|Adjust the seasoning to taste.
spice|noun|A strong-tasting plant part used in cooking|pampalasa|Bahagi ng halamang pampalasa|Cinnamon is a warm spice.
herb|noun|A plant used to flavour food|damong pampalasa|Halamang pampalasa sa pagkain|Basil is a fragrant herb.
grain|noun|The seed of a cereal plant|butil|Buto ng halamang cereal|Rice is the most eaten grain.
cereal|noun|A grain crop, or breakfast food made from it|butil|Pananim na butil o pang-almusal|Wheat is an important cereal.
dairy|noun|Milk and foods made from it|gatasan|Gatas at mga produktong gawa rito|She avoids dairy products.
poultry|noun|Birds kept for meat and eggs|manukan|Mga ibong inaalagaan para sa karne at itlog|The farm raises poultry.
seafood|noun|Fish and shellfish eaten as food|pagkaing-dagat|Isda at kabibe bilang pagkain|The coast is famous for seafood.
produce|noun|Fresh fruit and vegetables|ani|Sariwang prutas at gulay|The market sells local produce.
protein|noun|A nutrient needed to build the body|protina|Sustansyang kailangan sa paglaki|Beans are a good source of protein.
carbohydrate|noun|A nutrient that gives energy|karbohidrat|Sustansyang nagbibigay ng lakas|Rice is high in carbohydrate.
vitamin|noun|A substance in food needed in small amounts|bitamina|Sustansyang kailangan sa maliit na dami|Oranges contain vitamin C.
fibre|noun|The part of plant food that aids digestion|hibla|Bahagi ng pagkaing tumutulong sa panunaw|Wholemeal bread is high in fibre.
calorie|noun|A unit measuring the energy in food|kaloriya|Panukat ng enerhiya sa pagkain|This meal has 600 calories.
diet|noun|The food a person usually eats|diyeta|Karaniwang kinakain ng isang tao|A balanced diet keeps you well.
vegetarian|noun|Somebody who does not eat meat|behetaryan|Taong hindi kumakain ng karne|She has been vegetarian for years.
vegan|noun|Somebody who eats no animal products at all|bigan|Taong hindi kumakain ng anumang galing sa hayop|Vegan meals use no dairy.
edible|adjective|Safe or suitable to eat|nakakain|Ligtas o angkop kainin|Not every mushroom is edible.
stale|adjective|No longer fresh|panis|Hindi na sariwa|The bread went stale.
ripe|adjective|Fully grown and ready to eat|hinog|Ganap na at handa nang kainin|Choose ripe mangoes.
raw|adjective|Not cooked|hilaw|Hindi luto|Sushi uses raw fish.
savoury|adjective|Salty or spicy rather than sweet|maalat|Maalat o maanghang, hindi matamis|He prefers savoury snacks.
bland|adjective|Having very little flavour|walang lasa|Kulang sa lasa|The soup was rather bland.
tender|adjective|Soft and easy to cut or chew|malambot|Madaling hiwain o nguyain|The beef was beautifully tender.
crisp|adjective|Firm and pleasantly brittle|malutong|Matigas at masarap kagatin|The apples were crisp and sweet.
juicy|adjective|Full of juice|makatas|Puno ng katas|These peaches are very juicy.
greasy|adjective|Covered in or containing a lot of fat|malangis|May labis na mantika|Greasy food upsets my stomach.
sour|adjective|Having a sharp acid taste|maasim|May matalas na asim|The lemon tasted sour.
bitter|adjective|Having a sharp unpleasant taste|mapait|May matalas at di-kaaya-ayang lasa|Dark chocolate is slightly bitter.
salty|adjective|Containing a lot of salt|maalat|Maraming asin|The broth was too salty.
spicy|adjective|Strongly flavoured with spices|maanghang|Malakas ang pampalasa|I love spicy noodles.
sweet|adjective|Tasting like sugar|matamis|May lasang asukal|The mangoes were very sweet.
starving|adjective|Extremely hungry|gutom na gutom|Matinding gutom|We were starving after the hike.
thirsty|adjective|Needing to drink|nauuhaw|Nangangailangang uminom|Running makes me thirsty.
full|adjective|Having eaten enough|busog|Sapat na ang nakain|No more, thank you, I am full.

# ---------------- Cooking & Kitchen ----------------
bake|verb|To cook in an oven using dry heat|maghurno|Magluto sa hurno|She bakes bread every Sunday.
boil|verb|To heat liquid until it bubbles|pakuluan|Painitin hanggang kumulo|Boil the water first.
simmer|verb|To cook gently just below boiling|pakuluan nang mahina|Lutuin sa mahinang apoy|Let the sauce simmer for an hour.
fry|verb|To cook in hot oil|magprito|Magluto sa mainit na mantika|Fry the onions until golden.
grill|verb|To cook under or over direct heat|mag-ihaw|Magluto sa direktang init|We grilled fish on the beach.
roast|verb|To cook in an oven with fat|mag-ihaw sa hurno|Lutuin sa hurno na may taba|Roast the chicken for an hour.
steam|verb|To cook using hot vapour|pasingawan|Lutuin gamit ang singaw|Steam the vegetables lightly.
stir|verb|To move food round with a spoon|haluin|Igalaw gamit ang kutsara|Stir the soup occasionally.
whisk|verb|To beat quickly to add air|batihin|Batihin upang magkahangin|Whisk the eggs until foamy.
knead|verb|To press and fold dough with your hands|masahin|Pisilin at tiklupin ang masa|Knead the dough for ten minutes.
chop|verb|To cut into pieces|hiwain|Putulin sa maliliit na piraso|Chop the carrots finely.
slice|verb|To cut into thin flat pieces|hiwain nang manipis|Putulin sa manipis na piraso|Slice the bread thickly.
dice|verb|To cut into small cubes|kubuhin|Hiwain sa maliliit na kubo|Dice the potatoes evenly.
peel|verb|To remove the skin of a fruit or vegetable|balatan|Alisin ang balat|Peel the apples first.
grate|verb|To shred food using a rough surface|kudkurin|Gadgarin gamit ang magaspang na kasangkapan|Grate the cheese over the top.
marinate|verb|To soak food in a sauce before cooking|imarinate|Ibabad sa sarsa bago lutuin|Marinate the pork overnight.
garnish|verb|To decorate a dish before serving|palamutian|Gayakan ang putahe bago ihain|Garnish with fresh parsley.
serve|verb|To give food to somebody|ihain|Ibigay ang pagkain|Serve the dish while hot.
season|verb|To add salt, pepper or spices|timplahan|Lagyan ng pampalasa|Season the soup to taste.
saucepan|noun|A deep cooking pot with a handle|kaserola|Malalim na kaldero na may hawakan|Heat the milk in a saucepan.
frying pan|noun|A shallow pan for frying|kawali|Mababaw na kawali para sa pagprito|Use a heavy frying pan.
kettle|noun|A container for boiling water|takure|Lalagyan para sa pagpapakulo ng tubig|Put the kettle on.
oven|noun|An enclosed space for baking and roasting|hurno|Saradong lugar para sa paghurno|Preheat the oven to 200 degrees.
stove|noun|An appliance for cooking on|kalan|Kasangkapan para sa pagluluto|The stove has four burners.
utensil|noun|A tool used in the kitchen|kagamitan|Kasangkapang panluto|Wooden utensils are gentle on pans.
cutlery|noun|Knives, forks and spoons|kubyertos|Kutsilyo, tinidor at kutsara|Set the cutlery on the right.
crockery|noun|Plates, cups and bowls|pinggan|Mga plato, tasa at mangkok|Wash the crockery after dinner.
tray|noun|A flat board for carrying food|bandehado|Patag na sisidlan para sa pagdadala|She carried the tea on a tray.
lid|noun|A cover for a pot or jar|takip|Pantakip sa kaldero o garapon|Keep the lid on while it cooks.
ladle|noun|A large deep spoon for serving liquid|sandok|Malaking kutsara para sa sabaw|Use a ladle for the broth.
sieve|noun|A tool with holes for straining|salaan|Kagamitang may butas para sa pagsala|Pass the sauce through a sieve.
leftovers|noun|Food remaining after a meal|tira|Natirang pagkain|We ate the leftovers cold.
portion|noun|An amount of food for one person|bahagi|Dami ng pagkain para sa isang tao|Serve modest portions.
helping|noun|An amount of food served at one time|serbing|Dami ng ihaing pagkain|He asked for a second helping.

# ---------------- Family & Relationships ----------------
relative|noun|A member of your family|kamag-anak|Kasapi ng pamilya|Many relatives came to the wedding.
sibling|noun|A brother or sister|kapatid|Kapatid na lalaki o babae|She has three siblings.
offspring|noun|A person's children|supling|Anak ng isang tao|The couple has two offspring.
ancestor|noun|A person from whom you are descended|ninuno|Taong pinagmulan ng lahi|Our ancestors farmed this land.
descendant|noun|A person descended from another|inapo|Taong nagmula sa isang ninuno|She is a descendant of settlers.
heir|noun|A person who will inherit property|tagapagmana|Taong magmamana ng ari-arian|He is the sole heir.
guardian|noun|A person legally responsible for a child|tagapag-alaga|Taong legal na nangangalaga sa bata|Her aunt became her guardian.
spouse|noun|A husband or wife|asawa|Asawang lalaki o babae|Bring your spouse to the dinner.
in-law|noun|A relative by marriage|biyenan|Kamag-anak dahil sa kasal|My in-laws live nearby.
godparent|noun|A person who promises to help raise a child|ninong o ninang|Taong nangakong tumulong sa pagpapalaki|Her godparent gave her a watch.
household|noun|All the people living in one home|sambahayan|Lahat ng nakatira sa isang bahay|The household includes five people.
upbringing|noun|The way a child is raised|pagpapalaki|Paraan ng pagpapalaki sa bata|He had a strict upbringing.
acquaintance|noun|Somebody you know slightly|kakilala|Taong bahagyang kilala|He is an acquaintance, not a friend.
companion|noun|A person who spends time with you|kasama|Taong kasama mo|A dog makes a loyal companion.
peer|noun|Somebody of the same age or status|kapantay|Taong kapareho ng edad o antas|Teenagers value their peers' views.
neighbour|noun|A person living next to you|kapitbahay|Taong nakatira sa katabi|Our neighbours are very kind.
bond|noun|A strong feeling of connection|ugnayan|Matibay na damdamin ng koneksyon|Sisters often share a close bond.
intimacy|noun|Close personal connection|lapit|Malapit na personal na ugnayan|Trust grows with intimacy.
loyalty|noun|Faithful support for somebody|katapatan|Tapat na suporta sa iba|Loyalty matters in friendship.
commitment|noun|A promise to keep doing something|pangako|Pangakong ipagpatuloy ang isang bagay|Marriage is a lifelong commitment.
engagement|noun|An agreement to marry|pagpapakasal|Kasunduang magpakasal|They announced their engagement.
wedding|noun|A marriage ceremony|kasal|Seremonya ng pag-iisang dibdib|The wedding took place in June.
anniversary|noun|The yearly return of a special date|anibersaryo|Taunang pagbabalik ng natatanging araw|They celebrated their tenth anniversary.
divorce|noun|The legal ending of a marriage|diborsyo|Legal na pagtatapos ng kasal|The divorce was settled quietly.
reunion|noun|A meeting of people after a long time apart|muling pagkikita|Pagkikita matapos ang mahabang panahon|The family reunion drew fifty people.
courtship|noun|The period of getting to know a partner|panliligaw|Panahon ng pagkilala sa magiging kapareha|Their courtship lasted two years.
quarrel|noun|An angry disagreement|away|Galit na di-pagkakasundo|The quarrel was soon forgotten.
reconciliation|noun|The restoring of a friendly relationship|pagkakasundo|Pagbabalik ng magandang ugnayan|Reconciliation took months.
estranged|adjective|No longer close or in contact|hiwalay|Hindi na malapit o nagkakausap|He is estranged from his brother.
supportive|adjective|Giving help and encouragement|maalalay|Nagbibigay ng tulong at lakas ng loob|Her family was very supportive.
protective|adjective|Wanting to keep somebody safe|mapangalaga|Nais panatilihing ligtas ang iba|Parents are naturally protective.

# ---------------- Home & Household ----------------
apartment|noun|A set of rooms on one floor|apartment|Grupo ng silid sa isang palapag|They rent a small apartment.
flat|noun|A set of rooms on one floor, British usage|plat|Tirahan sa isang palapag|She lives in a one-bedroom flat.
bungalow|noun|A house on a single level|bungalow|Bahay na iisa ang palapag|The bungalow has a wide porch.
cottage|noun|A small house in the country|kubo|Maliit na bahay sa bukid|They own a cottage by the lake.
mansion|noun|A very large impressive house|mansyon|Napakalaki at kahanga-hangang bahay|The mansion has twenty rooms.
terrace|noun|A row of joined houses, or a flat outdoor area|terasa|Hanay ng magkakadikit na bahay|We ate on the terrace.
attic|noun|The space just under the roof|atiko|Espasyo sa ilalim ng bubong|Old boxes fill the attic.
basement|noun|A room below ground level|silong|Silid sa ilalim ng lupa|The basement floods in winter.
hallway|noun|A passage inside a house|pasilyo|Daanan sa loob ng bahay|Leave your shoes in the hallway.
landing|noun|The flat area at the top of stairs|palapag|Patag na bahagi sa itaas ng hagdan|The bathroom is off the landing.
staircase|noun|A set of stairs and its supports|hagdanan|Hanay ng baitang at suporta nito|A wooden staircase leads upstairs.
ceiling|noun|The upper surface of a room|kisame|Itaas na bahagi ng silid|The ceiling needs painting.
wall|noun|A vertical side of a room or building|dingding|Patayong bahagi ng silid|Hang the picture on that wall.
floor|noun|The surface you walk on indoors|sahig|Bahaging nilalakaran sa loob|The floor is wooden.
roof|noun|The covering on top of a building|bubong|Panakip sa itaas ng gusali|The roof leaks in heavy rain.
doorway|noun|The opening where a door is|pinto|Bukana kung saan naroon ang pinto|She stood in the doorway.
window sill|noun|The shelf at the bottom of a window|pasimano|Istante sa ilalim ng bintana|Plants line the window sill.
curtain|noun|Cloth hung to cover a window|kurtina|Telang nakasabit sa bintana|Draw the curtains at night.
blind|noun|A window covering that rolls or folds|blaynds|Pantakip sa bintana na nakarolyo|Lower the blind to block the sun.
carpet|noun|A thick floor covering|karpet|Makapal na pantakip sa sahig|The carpet muffles footsteps.
rug|noun|A small carpet|banig|Maliit na karpet|A rug warms the room.

mattress|noun|The soft part of a bed you lie on|kutson|Malambot na bahagi ng kama|The mattress is very firm.

chore|noun|A routine household task|gawaing bahay|Karaniwang gawain sa bahay|Washing up is my least favourite chore.
tidy|verb|To put things in order|maglinis|Ayusin ang mga bagay|Please tidy your room.

scrub|verb|To rub hard to clean|kuskusin|Kuskusin nang malakas upang linisin|She scrubbed the pan clean.

declutter|verb|To remove things you do not need|magbawas|Alisin ang hindi kailangan|We decluttered the garage.
rent|noun|Money paid regularly to use a property|upa|Perang binabayad para sa tirahan|The rent is due on the first.

utilities|noun|Services such as water and electricity|serbisyong pambahay|Serbisyo tulad ng tubig at kuryente|Utilities are not included.

# ---------------- Clothing & Fashion ----------------

leather|noun|Animal skin treated for use|katad|Balat ng hayop na ginawang materyal|Leather shoes last for years.

casual|adjective|Informal and relaxed in style|kaswal|Impormal at maluwag ang istilo|Casual dress is fine.

worn|adjective|Damaged by long use|luma|Sira dahil sa matagal na paggamit|The carpet is badly worn.

# ---------------- Body & Appearance ----------------

# ---------------- Time & Calendar ----------------

# ---------------- Weather & Seasons ----------------

# ---------------- Animals & Plants ----------------
mammal|noun|A warm-blooded animal that feeds its young milk|mamalya|Hayop na nagpapasuso sa anak|Whales are the largest mammals.
reptile|noun|A cold-blooded animal with scales|reptilya|Hayop na may kaliskis at malamig ang dugo|Lizards are reptiles.
amphibian|noun|An animal living both in water and on land|ampibyan|Hayop na nabubuhay sa tubig at lupa|Frogs are amphibians.
insect|noun|A small animal with six legs|insekto|Maliit na hayop na anim ang paa|Bees are useful insects.
prey|noun|An animal hunted by another|biktima|Hayop na hinuhuli ng iba|Rabbits are common prey.
herbivore|noun|An animal that eats only plants|halamanin|Hayop na halaman lamang ang kinakain|Cows are herbivores.
carnivore|noun|An animal that eats meat|karnihan|Hayop na kumakain ng karne|Lions are carnivores.
hibernate|verb|To sleep through the winter|maghibernate|Matulog sa buong taglamig|Bears hibernate in caves.
extinct|adjective|No longer existing as a species|lipol na|Wala nang natitira sa lahi|The dodo is extinct.
endangered|adjective|At risk of dying out|nanganganib|Nanganganib mawala ang lahi|Tigers remain endangered.
domesticated|adjective|Tamed and kept by people|maamo|Inamo at inalagaan ng tao|Dogs were domesticated long ago.
wild|adjective|Living naturally, not tamed|ligaw|Namumuhay nang malaya|Wild horses roam the plain.
livestock|noun|Farm animals kept for use|alagang hayop|Hayop sa bukid na inaalagaan|Livestock grazed on the hill.
poultry farm|noun|A place where birds are raised for food|manukan|Lugar ng pag-aalaga ng manok|The poultry farm supplies the market.
herd|noun|A group of large animals together|kawan|Grupo ng malalaking hayop|A herd of cattle blocked the road.
flock|noun|A group of birds or sheep|kawan|Grupo ng ibon o tupa|A flock of geese flew overhead.
swarm|noun|A large moving group of insects|kuyog|Malaking grupo ng gumagalaw na insekto|A swarm of bees settled on the branch.
nest|noun|A structure built by a bird for its eggs|pugad|Bahay na itinayo ng ibon para sa itlog|The birds built a nest in the hedge.
burrow|noun|A hole dug by an animal for shelter|lungga|Butas na hinukay ng hayop|Rabbits sleep in a burrow.
foliage|noun|The leaves of a plant|dahon|Mga dahon ng halaman|The foliage turns red in autumn.
blossom|noun|A flower on a tree|bulaklak|Bulaklak ng puno|Cherry blossom lasts a week.
seedling|noun|A very young plant grown from seed|punla|Napakabatang halaman mula sa buto|Water the seedlings daily.
root|noun|The part of a plant that grows underground|ugat|Bahagi ng halaman sa ilalim ng lupa|Deep roots find water.
stem|noun|The main upright part of a plant|tangkay|Pangunahing patayong bahagi ng halaman|Cut the stem at an angle.
shrub|noun|A low woody plant smaller than a tree|palumpong|Mababang halamang mas maliit sa puno|Shrubs line the garden path.
weed|noun|A wild plant growing where it is not wanted|damo|Ligaw na halaman sa maling lugar|Pull the weeds before they spread.
germinate|verb|To begin to grow from a seed|umusbong|Magsimulang tumubo mula sa buto|The beans germinate in a week.
wither|verb|To dry up and die|malanta|Matuyo at mamatay|The flowers withered in the heat.
prune|verb|To cut branches to help a plant grow|magbungkal|Putulin ang sanga upang lumago|Prune the roses in winter.

# ---------------- Transport & Vehicles ----------------
vehicle|noun|A machine used for transporting people or goods|sasakyan|Makinang panghatid ng tao o kargamento|Every vehicle must be insured.
transport|noun|A system for carrying people or goods|transportasyon|Sistema ng paghahatid ng tao o kalakal|Public transport is cheap here.
carriage|noun|A vehicle pulled by horses, or a train coach|karwahe|Sasakyang hila ng kabayo|The first carriage is quiet.
coach|noun|A long-distance bus|bus|Bus na pangmalayuang biyahe|We took the coach to Manchester.
tram|noun|A vehicle running on rails in a street|tranvia|Sasakyang tumatakbo sa riles sa kalye|Trams cross the city centre.
underground|noun|A railway system beneath a city|tren sa ilalim ng lupa|Riles ng tren sa ilalim ng lungsod|Take the underground to the museum.
ferry|noun|A boat carrying people across water|lantsa|Bangkang naghahatid ng tao sa tubig|The ferry runs every hour.
freight|noun|Goods carried by transport|kargamento|Mga bagay na dinadala ng sasakyan|Freight moves by rail at night.
cargo|noun|Goods carried by ship or plane|kargamento|Kalakal na dala ng barko o eroplano|The cargo was unloaded at dawn.
timetable|noun|A list of times when things happen|talaorasan|Listahan ng oras ng mga pangyayari|Check the timetable online.
junction|noun|A place where roads or railways meet|sangandaan|Lugar kung saan nagtatagpo ang daan|Turn left at the junction.
roundabout|noun|A circular road junction|rotonda|Bilog na sangandaan|Take the second exit at the roundabout.
lane|noun|A narrow road, or a marked strip of a road|daan|Makitid na daan o gilid ng kalsada|Stay in the left lane.
pedestrian|noun|A person walking rather than driving|naglalakad|Taong naglalakad, hindi nagmamaneho|Pedestrians have right of way.
congestion|noun|Too much traffic in one place|siksikan|Sobrang dami ng trapiko|Congestion doubles the journey time.
breakdown|noun|A mechanical failure of a vehicle|sira|Pagkasira ng makina ng sasakyan|We had a breakdown on the motorway.
puncture|noun|A small hole in a tyre|butas|Maliit na butas sa gulong|A puncture delayed us an hour.
fuel|noun|Material burnt to produce power|gasolina|Materyal na sinusunog para sa lakas|Fuel prices keep rising.
mileage|noun|The distance a vehicle travels|milya|Layong nalalakbay ng sasakyan|The car has low mileage.
overtake|verb|To pass a slower vehicle|lumampas|Lampasan ang mas mabagal na sasakyan|Never overtake on a bend.
reverse|verb|To drive backwards|umatras|Magmaneho paatras|Reverse slowly into the space.
board|verb|To get onto a vehicle|sumakay|Pumasok sa sasakyan|Passengers may now board.
disembark|verb|To get off a ship or plane|bumaba|Umalis mula sa barko o eroplano|We disembarked at noon.

# ---------------- Sports & Leisure ----------------
athlete|noun|A person trained in a sport|atleta|Taong sanay sa palakasan|The athlete broke the record.
opponent|noun|A person you compete against|kalaban|Taong kinakalaban mo|He respects every opponent.
referee|noun|An official who enforces the rules|reperi|Opisyal na nagpapatupad ng batas ng laro|The referee blew the whistle.
umpire|noun|An official in games such as cricket or tennis|umpire|Opisyal sa laro tulad ng tennis|The umpire called it out.
spectator|noun|A person watching an event|manonood|Taong nanonood ng palabas|Spectators filled the stands.
tournament|noun|A competition with many matches|torneo|Paligsahang may maraming laban|The tournament lasts a fortnight.
championship|noun|A competition to decide the best|kampeonato|Paligsahan upang tukuyin ang pinakamahusay|They won the championship twice.
fixture|noun|A scheduled sporting match|nakatakdang laban|Nakaiskedyul na laban|Saturday's fixture was postponed.
squad|noun|A group of players in a team|pangkat|Grupo ng manlalaro sa koponan|The squad trains every morning.
tactic|noun|A plan for gaining an advantage|taktika|Plano para makakuha ng bentahe|Their tactic was to defend deep.
stamina|noun|The energy to keep going for a long time|tibay|Lakas na magtagal sa gawain|Long runs build stamina.
agility|noun|The ability to move quickly and easily|liksi|Kakayahang gumalaw nang mabilis|Agility matters more than size.
endurance|noun|The ability to keep going under strain|tatag|Kakayahang magtiis sa hirap|Marathons test endurance.
warm-up|noun|Gentle exercise before activity|pag-iinit|Magaang ehersisyo bago maglaro|Never skip the warm-up.
fixture list|noun|The schedule of upcoming matches|talaan ng laban|Iskedyul ng mga darating na laban|The fixture list is published in July.
defeat|noun|A loss in a game or contest|pagkatalo|Pagkatalo sa laro o paligsahan|It was their first defeat.
victory|noun|A win in a game or contest|tagumpay|Panalo sa laro o paligsahan|The victory was well deserved.
hobby|noun|An activity done for pleasure|libangan|Gawaing ginagawa para sa kasiyahan|Photography is her main hobby.
pastime|noun|Something done to pass time enjoyably|libangan|Bagay na ginagawa upang lumipas ang oras|Chess is a quiet pastime.
recreation|noun|Activity done for enjoyment and rest|paglilibang|Gawain para sa aliw at pahinga|The park is used for recreation.
leisure|noun|Free time when you are not working|paglilibang|Oras na hindi nagtatrabaho|Use your leisure time well.
craft|noun|An activity involving skill with the hands|sining|Gawaing nangangailangan ng galing ng kamay|Pottery is a demanding craft.
gathering|noun|A meeting of people for a purpose|pagtitipon|Pagpupulong ng mga tao|A small gathering marked the day.
outing|noun|A short trip for pleasure|lakad|Maikling lakad para sa aliw|The class went on an outing.
picnic|noun|A meal eaten outdoors|piknik|Pagkain sa labas ng bahay|We had a picnic by the river.
camping|noun|Staying outdoors in a tent|kamping|Pananatili sa labas gamit ang tolda|Camping teaches self-reliance.
hiking|noun|Walking long distances in the countryside|paglalakad sa bundok|Mahabang paglalakad sa kabundukan|Hiking clears my head.

# ---------------- School & Education ----------------
curriculum|noun|The subjects taught in a school|kurikulum|Mga aralin sa paaralan|The curriculum changed this year.
syllabus|noun|The topics covered in one course|silabus|Mga paksa sa isang kurso|Read the syllabus carefully.
lesson|noun|A period of teaching on one topic|aralin|Panahon ng pagtuturo sa isang paksa|The lesson lasted an hour.
seminar|noun|A small class for discussion|seminar|Maliit na klase para sa talakayan|We debated the text in the seminar.
tutorial|noun|A small teaching session|tutorial|Maliit na sesyon ng pagtuturo|Tutorials are held weekly.
enrol|verb|To officially join a course|magpatala|Pormal na sumali sa kurso|She enrolled in the evening class.
graduate|verb|To complete a course of study|magtapos|Tapusin ang pag-aaral|He graduated with honours.
qualification|noun|An official record of passing a course|kwalipikasyon|Opisyal na patunay ng pagpasa|The job needs a teaching qualification.
diploma|noun|A certificate awarded after a course|diploma|Sertipiko pagkatapos ng kurso|She earned a diploma in design.
degree|noun|A qualification from a university|digri|Kwalipikasyon mula sa unibersidad|He holds a degree in history.
scholarship|noun|Money given to help somebody study|iskolarsip|Perang tulong sa pag-aaral|The scholarship covers her fees.
tuition|noun|Teaching, or the fee paid for it|matrikula|Pagtuturo o bayad dito|Tuition has risen sharply.
assignment|noun|A task given to students|takdang-aralin|Gawaing ibinigay sa mga mag-aaral|Hand in the assignment by Friday.
coursework|noun|Work done during a course and marked|gawaing pampaaralan|Gawaing tinatasa habang nag-aaral|Coursework counts for forty percent.
revision|noun|Study done before an examination|pagbabalik-aral|Pag-aaral bago ang pagsusulit|Start your revision early.
examination|noun|A formal test of knowledge|pagsusulit|Pormal na pagsubok sa kaalaman|The examination lasts three hours.
grade|noun|A mark showing the standard of work|marka|Marka na nagpapakita ng antas ng gawa|She got the top grade.
transcript|noun|An official record of a student's grades|transcript|Opisyal na talaan ng marka|Request your transcript online.
attendance|noun|The number of times you are present|pagdalo|Bilang ng pagdalo|Attendance affects your grade.
truancy|noun|Staying away from school without permission|pagliban|Pagliban sa paaralan nang walang paalam|Truancy has fallen this term.
discipline|noun|Training that produces obedience|disiplina|Pagsasanay na nagdudulot ng pagsunod|The school is known for its discipline.
headteacher|noun|The teacher in charge of a school|punong-guro|Gurong namumuno sa paaralan|The headteacher addressed the parents.
faculty|noun|The teaching staff of a school|guro|Mga gurong nagtuturo|The faculty met on Monday.
pupil|noun|A child at school|mag-aaral|Batang nasa paaralan|Each pupil received a book.
undergraduate|noun|A student working for a first degree|undergraduate|Mag-aaral sa unang digri|Undergraduates live on campus.
postgraduate|noun|A student studying after a first degree|postgraduate|Mag-aaral pagkatapos ng unang digri|She is a postgraduate in law.
semester|noun|Half of an academic year|semestre|Kalahati ng taong akademiko|Exams fall at the end of the semester.
campus|noun|The grounds of a school or university|kampus|Bakuran ng paaralan o unibersidad|The campus has three libraries.

# ---------------- Study Skills ----------------
comprehension|noun|The ability to understand what you read|pag-unawa|Kakayahang maunawaan ang binabasa|Reading daily improves comprehension.
concentration|noun|The ability to focus attention|konsentrasyon|Kakayahang mag-ukol ng pansin|Noise breaks my concentration.
memorise|verb|To learn something so you can repeat it|isaulo|Matutuhan upang maulit|He memorised the whole poem.
recall|verb|To bring something back to mind|maalala|Maibalik sa isip|I cannot recall her name.
summarise|verb|To give the main points briefly|buodin|Ibigay ang pangunahing punto nang maikli|Summarise the chapter in ten lines.
annotate|verb|To add notes to a text|lagyan ng nota|Magdagdag ng tala sa teksto|Annotate the margins as you read.
highlight|verb|To mark important text with colour|kulayan|Markahan ang mahalagang teksto|Highlight the key definitions.
cite|verb|To name a source you have used|banggitin|Sabihin ang pinagkunan|Cite every source you quote.
reference|noun|A mention of a source of information|sanggunian|Pagbanggit sa pinagkunan|Add a reference for each claim.
footnote|noun|A note printed at the bottom of a page|talababa|Notang nasa ibaba ng pahina|The footnote explains the term.
appendix|noun|Extra material added at the end of a text|apendise|Karagdagang bahagi sa dulo ng teksto|The data appears in the appendix.
mnemonic|noun|A pattern that helps you remember|panandang pang-alaala|Paraang tumutulong sa pag-alala|A mnemonic helps with spelling.
flashcard|noun|A card with a question on one side|flashcard|Kartang may tanong sa isang gilid|Flashcards suit vocabulary work.
procrastinate|verb|To delay doing something you should do|magpaliban|Ipagpaliban ang dapat gawin|Do not procrastinate before exams.

# ---------------- Arts & Literature ----------------
novel|noun|A long written story|nobela|Mahabang kuwentong isinulat|She published her first novel.
novella|noun|A short novel|maikling nobela|Maikling nobela|The novella runs to 90 pages.
anthology|noun|A collection of writings in one book|antolohiya|Koleksyon ng sulatin sa isang aklat|The anthology includes twenty poets.
memoir|noun|An account of a person's own life|memoir|Salaysay ng sariling buhay|Her memoir describes the war years.
biography|noun|The story of a person's life written by another|talambuhay|Kuwento ng buhay na isinulat ng iba|The biography took ten years to write.
autobiography|noun|The story of your own life|sariling talambuhay|Kuwento ng sariling buhay|He wrote his autobiography at eighty.
non-fiction|noun|Writing about real facts and events|di-kathang-isip|Sulatin tungkol sa totoong pangyayari|The non-fiction section is upstairs.
narrative|noun|An account of connected events|salaysay|Salaysay ng magkakaugnay na pangyayari|The narrative moves quickly.
protagonist|noun|The main character in a story|pangunahing tauhan|Punong tauhan sa kuwento|The protagonist changes greatly.
antagonist|noun|The character who opposes the main character|kontrabida|Tauhang kumakalaban sa bida|The antagonist is well drawn.
imagery|noun|Descriptive language that creates pictures|imahen|Paglalarawang lumilikha ng larawan sa isip|The imagery is unusually vivid.
symbolism|noun|The use of symbols to suggest meaning|simbolismo|Paggamit ng simbolo upang magpahiwatig|The river is full of symbolism.
tragedy|noun|A serious play with an unhappy ending|trahedya|Seryosong dula na malungkot ang wakas|The tragedy ends with three deaths.
comedy|noun|A light amusing play or film|komedya|Magaan at nakakatawang dula|The comedy played to full houses.
manuscript|noun|A text written before it is published|manuskrito|Sulatin bago ilathala|The manuscript ran to 500 pages.
publisher|noun|A company that produces books|tagapaglathala|Kumpanyang naglalathala ng aklat|The publisher accepted her book.
edition|noun|A version of a book published at one time|edisyon|Bersyon ng aklat na inilathala|This is the second edition.
critic|noun|A person who judges works of art|kritiko|Taong humahatol sa akda|The critic praised the acting.
review|noun|A written judgement of a work|repaso|Sulating paghatol sa akda|The review was very positive.
sculpture|noun|Art made by shaping stone, wood or metal|iskultura|Sining na hinuhubog mula sa bato o kahoy|The sculpture stands in the square.
portrait|noun|A picture of a person|larawan|Larawan ng isang tao|She painted a portrait of her father.
landscape|noun|A picture of natural scenery|tanawin|Larawan ng likas na tanawin|The landscape hangs in the hall.
exhibition|noun|A public showing of works of art|eksibit|Pampublikong pagtatanghal ng sining|The exhibition runs until May.
gallery|noun|A place where art is displayed|galeriya|Lugar na pinagtatanghalan ng sining|The gallery is free on Sundays.
canvas|noun|The cloth an artist paints on|kanbas|Telang pinipintahan ng pintor|He stretched a fresh canvas.
masterpiece|noun|An artist's greatest work|obra maestra|Pinakamahusay na gawa ng artista|The painting is his masterpiece.

# ---------------- Music & Performance ----------------
melody|noun|A tune made of a series of notes|melodiya|Himig na binubuo ng mga nota|The melody stays in your head.
harmony|noun|Notes sounded together pleasingly|harmoniya|Magkasabay na notang kaaya-aya|Their harmony is beautiful.
tempo|noun|The speed of a piece of music|tempo|Bilis ng isang piyesa ng musika|Increase the tempo slightly.
lyrics|noun|The words of a song|liriko|Mga salita ng awit|The lyrics tell a sad story.
chorus|noun|The repeated part of a song|koro|Inuulit na bahagi ng awit|Everyone sang the chorus.
composer|noun|A person who writes music|kompositor|Taong sumusulat ng musika|The composer conducted the piece.
conductor|noun|A person who directs an orchestra|konduktor|Taong nangunguna sa orkestra|The conductor raised her baton.
orchestra|noun|A large group of musicians|orkestra|Malaking grupo ng musikero|The orchestra tuned up.
choir|noun|A group of singers|koro|Grupo ng mang-aawit|The choir rehearses on Tuesdays.
soloist|noun|A performer who plays or sings alone|solista|Tagaganap na nag-iisa|The soloist received an ovation.
rehearsal|noun|A practice session before a performance|ensayo|Pagsasanay bago ang pagtatanghal|The final rehearsal is tonight.
performance|noun|An occasion when a work is presented|pagtatanghal|Pagkakataon ng pagpapakita ng akda|The performance sold out.
stage|noun|The raised area where performers appear|entablado|Nakataas na bahagi ng pagtatanghal|She walked onto the stage.
backstage|noun|The area behind the stage|likod-entablado|Bahagi sa likod ng entablado|We waited backstage.
costume|noun|Clothes worn by a performer|kasuotan|Damit ng tagaganap|The costumes were hand sewn.
script|noun|The written text of a play or film|iskrip|Nakasulat na teksto ng dula o pelikula|He learned the whole script.
improvise|verb|To perform without preparation|mag-improvise|Gumanap nang walang paghahanda|The band improvised for ten minutes.
applaud|verb|To clap to show approval|pumalakpak|Pumalakpak bilang papuri|The crowd applauded loudly.
encore|noun|An extra performance after applause|encore|Dagdag na pagtatanghal|They played two encores.
debut|noun|A first public appearance|debut|Unang pagharap sa publiko|She made her debut at twenty.
acoustic|adjective|Not using electrical amplification|akustiko|Hindi gumagamit ng elektrikong lakas|He played an acoustic set.

# ---------------- Business & Marketing ----------------
enterprise|noun|A business, or bold activity|negosyo|Negosyo o matapang na gawain|The enterprise employs fifty people.
startup|noun|A newly formed company|startup|Bagong tatag na kumpanya|The startup raised funding quickly.
merger|noun|The joining of two companies|pagsasanib|Pagsasama ng dalawang kumpanya|The merger was approved.
acquisition|noun|The buying of one company by another|pagkuha|Pagbili ng isang kumpanya sa iba|The acquisition cost millions.
subsidiary|noun|A company controlled by a larger one|sangay|Kumpanyang kontrolado ng mas malaki|The subsidiary trades separately.
franchise|noun|A licence to run a business under a brand|prangkisa|Lisensyang magpatakbo sa ilalim ng tatak|He bought a coffee franchise.
shareholder|noun|A person who owns part of a company|kasapi sa pagmamay-ari|Taong may bahagi sa kumpanya|Shareholders voted against it.
overhead|noun|The regular costs of running a business|gastos|Regular na gastusin ng negosyo|Rent is our largest overhead.
target|noun|A result you are aiming for|target|Layuning nais abutin|We met the quarterly target.
campaign|noun|A planned series of actions to achieve something|kampanya|Sunod-sunod na hakbang para sa layunin|The campaign ran for six weeks.
branding|noun|Creating a distinct identity for a product|pagtatatak|Paglikha ng natatanging pagkakakilanlan|Branding shapes how buyers feel.
logo|noun|A symbol representing a company|logo|Simbolong kumakatawan sa kumpanya|The new logo is simpler.
slogan|noun|A short memorable phrase used in advertising|islogan|Maikling parirala sa patalastas|Their slogan is known everywhere.
endorsement|noun|Public support for a product by a known person|pag-endorso|Pampublikong suporta sa produkto|The endorsement boosted sales.
outlet|noun|A shop selling a company's goods|tindahan|Tindahang nagbebenta ng produkto|They opened a new outlet.
supply chain|noun|The steps that bring a product to market|kadena ng suplay|Hakbang mula produksyon hanggang benta|The supply chain was disrupted.
inventory|noun|The goods a business holds|imbentaryo|Mga paninda ng negosyo|Check the inventory monthly.
contract|noun|A legal agreement between parties|kontrata|Legal na kasunduan sa pagitan ng panig|Read the contract before signing.
negotiation|noun|Discussion aimed at reaching agreement|negosasyon|Talakayan upang magkasundo|Negotiations lasted three days.
compromise|noun|An agreement where each side gives way|kompromiso|Kasunduang may pagbibigay ang bawat panig|They reached a fair compromise.

# ---------------- Useful Verbs ----------------
abandon|verb|To leave somebody or something behind for good|pabayaan|Iwanan nang tuluyan|They abandoned the plan in June.
absorb|verb|To take in liquid, heat or information|sumipsip|Kunin o tanggapin ang likido o kaalaman|Plants absorb water through their roots.
accelerate|verb|To go faster or make something faster|bumilis|Pabilisin o lumakas ang takbo|The car accelerated up the hill.
accompany|verb|To go somewhere with somebody|samahan|Sumama sa isang tao|She accompanied him to the station.
accumulate|verb|To gather more and more over time|maipon|Mangolekta nang paunti-unti|Dust accumulates on the shelves.
accuse|verb|To say that somebody has done wrong|akusahan|Sabihing may ginawang mali|They accused him of cheating.
achieve|verb|To succeed in doing something|makamit|Magtagumpay sa isang layunin|She achieved all her goals.
acknowledge|verb|To accept that something is true|kilalanin|Tanggaping totoo ang isang bagay|He acknowledged his mistake.
activate|verb|To make something start working|paganahin|Simulan ang paggana ng isang bagay|Activate the card online.
adhere|verb|To stick to something, or to follow a rule|sumunod|Dumikit o sumunod sa patakaran|We adhere to strict standards.
admire|verb|To respect and like somebody|humanga|Igalang at gustuhin ang isang tao|I admire her patience.
adopt|verb|To take on a method, or take a child as your own|ampunin|Gamitin ang paraan o kupkupin ang bata|The council adopted the proposal.
advance|verb|To move forward or make progress|sumulong|Gumalaw pasulong o umunlad|The troops advanced slowly.
affirm|verb|To state something firmly|patunayan|Sabihing may katiyakan|She affirmed her support.
afford|verb|To have enough money for something|kayanin|May sapat na pera para sa isang bagay|We cannot afford a new car.
aggravate|verb|To make a bad situation worse|palalain|Gawing mas malala ang sitwasyon|Stress aggravates the condition.
aim|verb|To try to achieve something|layunin|Subukang makamit ang isang bagay|We aim to finish by Friday.
align|verb|To arrange things in a line or in agreement|iayon|Ihanay o itugma|Align the text on the left.
allege|verb|To claim something without proof|ipagpalagay|Sabihin nang walang patunay|They allege that funds were misused.
alleviate|verb|To make pain or a problem less severe|magpagaan|Bawasan ang sakit o problema|The medicine alleviates the pain.
allow|verb|To let somebody do something|pahintulutan|Payagang gawin ang isang bagay|Smoking is not allowed here.
alter|verb|To change something slightly|baguhin|Bahagyang palitan|We altered the schedule.
amaze|verb|To surprise somebody greatly|mamangha|Bigla at labis na magulat|Her progress amazes me.
amend|verb|To make small changes to improve something|amyendahan|Gumawa ng maliit na pagbabago|The law was amended last year.
amuse|verb|To make somebody laugh or smile|aliwin|Pasayahin o patawanin|The story amused the children.
anticipate keenly|verb|To look forward to something with eagerness|hintayin|Asahan nang may pananabik|We anticipate the results keenly.
apply|verb|To make a formal request, or to put into use|mag-apply|Magsumite ng hiling o gamitin|She applied for the post.
appoint|verb|To choose somebody for a job|italaga|Piliin para sa isang tungkulin|They appointed a new director.
approve|verb|To agree officially to something|aprubahan|Sang-ayunan nang opisyal|The board approved the budget.
arrange|verb|To plan or organise something|ayusin|Planuhin o isaayos|I arranged the meeting.
arrest|verb|To take somebody into police custody|arestuhin|Dakpin ng pulis|Two men were arrested.
ascertain|verb|To find out something for certain|tiyakin|Alamin nang may katiyakan|We must ascertain the facts.
aspire|verb|To have a strong wish to achieve something|maghangad|Magkaroon ng matinding hangarin|She aspires to be a surgeon.
assist|verb|To help somebody|tumulong|Magbigay ng tulong|Volunteers assisted the nurses.
associate|verb|To connect one thing with another|iugnay|Ikonekta ang isang bagay sa iba|I associate that song with summer.
attach|verb|To fasten one thing to another|ikabit|Idugtong sa isa pang bagay|Attach the file to your email.
attempt|verb|To try to do something difficult|subukan|Sikaping gawin ang mahirap|He attempted the climb twice.
attend|verb|To be present at an event|dumalo|Naroroon sa isang pagtitipon|Fifty people attended the talk.
attract|verb|To make somebody interested|makaakit|Makuha ang interes ng iba|The offer attracted many buyers.
avoid|verb|To keep away from something|iwasan|Layuan ang isang bagay|Avoid the motorway at rush hour.
