remind|verb|To help somebody remember|paalalahanan|Tulungang maalala|Remind me to call her.
remove|verb|To take something away|alisin|Kunin at ilayo|Remove the lid carefully.
renew|verb|To make something valid again|i-renew|Gawing balido muli|Renew your licence online.
repair|verb|To fix something broken|kumpunihin|Ayusin ang sira|They repaired the roof.
replace|verb|To put something new in place of another|palitan|Maglagay ng bago kapalit|Replace the battery yearly.
represent|verb|To act or speak on behalf of others|kumatawan|Kumilos para sa iba|She represents the region.
require|verb|To need something|mangailangan|Kailanganin ang isang bagay|The job requires patience.
rescue|verb|To save somebody from danger|iligtas|Sagipin mula sa panganib|They rescued the crew.
reserve|verb|To arrange for something to be kept for you|magpareserba|Ipaglaan ang isang bagay|I reserved a table.
resist|verb|To fight against something|labanan|Tumutol sa isang bagay|She resisted the pressure.
respect|verb|To admire somebody for their qualities|igalang|Hangaan dahil sa katangian|I respect his judgement.
restrict|verb|To limit something|limitahan|Bawasan ang saklaw|They restricted access.
retire|verb|To stop working at the end of a career|magretiro|Tumigil sa trabaho|He retired at sixty-five.
reveal slowly|verb|To make known something hidden|ibunyag|Ipaalam ang nakatago|The film reveals the truth slowly.
revive|verb|To bring back to life or use|buhayin muli|Ibalik sa buhay o gamit|They revived the old custom.
reward|verb|To give something for good work|gantimpalaan|Bigyan ng gantimpala|Effort should be rewarded.
ruin|verb|To spoil something completely|sirain|Sirain nang lubusan|Rain ruined the picnic.
satisfy|verb|To make somebody pleased|masiyahan|Bigyang-kasiyahan|The answer satisfied the panel.
scatter|verb|To throw or spread in different directions|ikalat|Ipalaganap sa iba't ibang dako|The wind scattered the leaves.
secure|verb|To obtain something, or make it safe|makuha|Makamit o gawing ligtas|She secured a place at university.
select|verb|To choose carefully|piliin|Pumili nang mabuti|Select the best option.
separate|verb|To divide or keep apart|paghiwalayin|Ihiwalay sa isa't isa|Separate the whites from the yolks.
settle|verb|To resolve a matter, or to make a home|ayusin|Lutasin o manirahan|They settled the dispute.
shrink back|verb|To move away in fear|umatras|Umurong dahil sa takot|He shrank back from the dog.
solve|verb|To find an answer to a problem|lutasin|Hanapan ng sagot ang problema|She solved the puzzle quickly.
sort|verb|To arrange things into groups|pag-uriin|Ihanay ayon sa uri|Sort the letters by date.
specify|verb|To state something exactly|tukuyin|Sabihin nang eksakto|Specify the size you need.
spoil|verb|To damage the quality of something|sirain|Bawasan ang kalidad|Too much salt spoils the dish.
spread rapidly|verb|To reach a wider area quickly|kumalat|Sumakop nang mabilis|The fire spread rapidly.
squeeze|verb|To press something firmly|pisilin|Idiin nang mahigpit|Squeeze the lemon.
stabilise|verb|To make something steady|patatagin|Gawing matatag|Prices finally stabilised.
stimulate|verb|To encourage something to develop|pasiglahin|Hikayating umunlad|Grants stimulate research.
strengthen|verb|To make something stronger|palakasin|Gawing mas malakas|Exercise strengthens bones.
stretch|verb|To make something longer or wider|iunat|Gawing mas mahaba o malapad|Stretch before running.
struggle|verb|To try hard against difficulty|magpumilit|Magsikap laban sa hirap|She struggled with the language.
submit|verb|To give something for consideration|isumite|Ibigay para sa pagsusuri|Submit your form by Friday.
succeed|verb|To achieve what you wanted|magtagumpay|Makamit ang ninanais|She succeeded at the second attempt.
suffer|verb|To experience something unpleasant|magdusa|Danasin ang masakit|The region suffered badly.
suggest gently|verb|To put forward an idea|imungkahi|Maglahad ng ideya|May I gently suggest a break?
supply|verb|To provide goods or services|maglaan|Magbigay ng paninda o serbisyo|They supply local schools.
surrender|verb|To stop fighting and give in|sumuko|Tumigil sa laban at pumayag|The garrison surrendered.
surround|verb|To be all around something|palibutan|Nasa palibot ng isang bagay|Trees surround the house.
survive|verb|To continue to live after danger|makaligtas|Manatiling buhay matapos ang panganib|Only two survived.
suspect|verb|To think something is probably true|maghinala|Isiping malamang totoo|Police suspect arson.
suspend|verb|To stop something temporarily|suspindihin|Ihinto pansamantala|Play was suspended for rain.
swallow|verb|To make food go down your throat|lunukin|Ipababa ang pagkain sa lalamunan|Swallow the tablet whole.
tackle|verb|To deal with a difficult problem|harapin|Lutasin ang mahirap na problema|We must tackle waste.
tempt|verb|To make somebody want something|tuksuhin|Pukawin ang pagnanasa|The offer tempted him.
terminate|verb|To bring something to an end|wakasan|Tapusin ang isang bagay|They terminated the contract.
threaten|verb|To say you will cause harm|magbanta|Sabihing magdudulot ng masama|He threatened legal action.
tolerate|verb|To accept something you dislike|tiisin|Tanggapin ang ayaw mo|I cannot tolerate rudeness.
trace|verb|To find by following signs|tunton|Hanapin sa pagsunod sa bakas|They traced the call.
transfer|verb|To move from one place to another|ilipat|Ilipat mula sa isang lugar|Transfer the funds today.
transform|verb|To change completely|baguhin|Palitan nang lubusan|The city transformed in a decade.
treat|verb|To behave towards somebody, or to give medical care|pakitunguhan|Ituring o gamutin|Treat everyone fairly.
trust|verb|To believe somebody is honest|magtiwala|Maniwalang tapat ang iba|I trust her completely.
unite|verb|To join together for a common purpose|magkaisa|Magsama para sa iisang layunin|The crisis united the town.
urge|verb|To strongly advise somebody|hikayatin|Payuhan nang mariin|I urge you to reconsider.
vanish|verb|To disappear suddenly|maglaho|Mawala nang biglaan|The keys simply vanished.
vary|verb|To be different in different situations|magkaiba|Maging iba sa bawat sitwasyon|Prices vary by season.
violate|verb|To break a rule or law|labagin|Sumuway sa batas o patakaran|They violated the agreement.
volunteer|verb|To offer to do something without pay|magboluntaryo|Mag-alok nang walang bayad|She volunteered at the shelter.
wander|verb|To walk without a fixed purpose|maglibot|Maglakad nang walang tiyak na layon|We wandered through the market.
warn firmly|verb|To tell somebody about a danger|balaan|Sabihan tungkol sa panganib|I firmly warned them of the risk.
weaken|verb|To become less strong|humina|Maging mas mahina|The storm weakened overnight.
welcome|verb|To greet somebody in a friendly way|tanggapin|Batiin nang magiliw|We welcome new members.
widen|verb|To make something wider|palawakin|Gawing mas malapad|They widened the road.
withstand|verb|To resist damage or pressure|matiis|Labanan ang pinsala o presyon|The wall withstood the flood.
witness|verb|To see something happen|masaksihan|Makita ang pangyayari|She witnessed the accident.
worsen|verb|To become worse|lumala|Maging mas masama|Conditions worsened overnight.

# ---------------- Useful Adjectives ----------------
abrupt|adjective|Sudden and unexpected, or rude in manner|biglaan|Biglaan o magaspang ang pakikitungo|The road came to an abrupt end.

acceptable|adjective|Good enough to be approved|katanggap-tanggap|Sapat upang tanggapin|The work is acceptable.

sacred|adjective|Connected with religion and greatly respected|banal|May kaugnayan sa relihiyon at iginagalang|The site is sacred to them.
safe|adjective|Not in danger|ligtas|Walang panganib|The children are safe.
satisfactory|adjective|Good enough for a purpose|kasiya-siya|Sapat para sa layunin|Progress is satisfactory.
selfish|adjective|Caring only about yourself|makasarili|Sarili lamang ang iniisip|That was a selfish decision.
serious|adjective|Important and needing attention|seryoso|Mahalaga at kailangang pansinin|This is a serious problem.
shallow|adjective|Not deep|mababaw|Hindi malalim|The stream is shallow here.
sharp|adjective|Having a fine edge, or quick to notice|matalas|May matulis na gilid o mabilis makapansin|Use a sharp knife.
shy|adjective|Nervous about meeting people|mahiyain|Kinakabahan sa harap ng iba|He is shy with strangers.
significant change|adjective|Large enough to matter|makabuluhan|Sapat na malaki upang mahalaga|There was a significant change.
silent|adjective|Making no sound at all|tahimik|Walang anumang tunog|The room fell silent.
similar|adjective|Almost the same|magkatulad|Halos magkapareho|The two cases are similar.

smooth|adjective|Having an even surface|makinis|Pantay ang ibabaw|The stone felt smooth.
solid|adjective|Firm and not liquid, or dependable|matibay|Matigas o maaasahan|We need solid evidence.

steep|adjective|Rising sharply|matarik|Biglang paakyat|The path is very steep.
sticky|adjective|Tending to stick to things|malagkit|May hilig dumikit|The floor is sticky.
stiff|adjective|Not easy to bend or move|matigas|Mahirap ibaluktot o igalaw|My neck feels stiff.

suspicious|adjective|Feeling that something is wrong|kahina-hinala|May hinalang may mali|The guard grew suspicious.
tedious|adjective|Boring and lasting too long|nakakabagot|Nakakainip at masyadong mahaba|The meeting was tedious.
terrible|adjective|Extremely bad|kakila-kilabot|Napakasama|The traffic was terrible.
thick|adjective|Wide from one side to the other|makapal|Malapad mula sa isang gilid|Cut a thick slice.
thin|adjective|Narrow, or having little fat|manipis|Makitid o walang taba|The ice is too thin.
thoughtful|adjective|Considerate, or deep in thought|maalalahanin|Maalalahanin o malalim mag-isip|That was a thoughtful gift.
tolerant|adjective|Willing to accept different views|mapagparaya|Handang tanggapin ang ibang pananaw|We aim to be a tolerant society.
tough|adjective|Strong, or difficult to deal with|matigas|Malakas o mahirap harapin|It was a tough decision.
tragic|adjective|Extremely sad, often involving death|trahiko|Labis na malungkot|The accident was tragic.
typical|adjective|Showing the usual qualities|karaniwan|Ipinapakita ang pangkaraniwang katangian|That is typical of him.
ugly|adjective|Unpleasant to look at|pangit|Hindi kaaya-ayang tingnan|The building is rather ugly.
uncomfortable|adjective|Not physically or mentally at ease|hindi komportable|Hindi maginhawa|The chair is uncomfortable.
unfair|adjective|Not treating people equally|di-makatarungan|Hindi pantay ang pakikitungo|The rule seems unfair.
unfamiliar|adjective|Not known or recognised|hindi pamilyar|Hindi kilala o alam|The route was unfamiliar.
unfortunate|adjective|Unlucky|malas|Walang swerte|It was an unfortunate error.

unpleasant|adjective|Not enjoyable|di-kaaya-aya|Hindi kasiya-siya|There was an unpleasant smell.
unstable|adjective|Likely to change or fall|hindi matatag|Madaling magbago o bumagsak|The ladder is unstable.
upset|adjective|Unhappy or worried|nababahala|Malungkot o nag-aalala|She was upset by the news.
useful|adjective|Helping you do something|kapaki-pakinabang|Nakakatulong sa gawain|That is a useful tip.
useless|adjective|Not helping at all|walang silbi|Walang naitutulong|The old key is useless.
usual|adjective|Happening most of the time|karaniwan|Madalas mangyari|Take the usual route.
vacant|adjective|Empty and available|bakante|Walang laman at pwedeng gamitin|The post is vacant.
vain|adjective|Too proud of your appearance, or useless|mayabang|Sobrang mapagmalaki o walang saysay|It was a vain attempt.

violent|adjective|Using physical force to hurt|marahas|Gumagamit ng dahas|The protest turned violent.

wealthy|adjective|Having a lot of money|mayaman|Maraming pera|The family is wealthy.
weird|adjective|Very strange|kakatwa|Napakakakaiba|That was a weird dream.

young|adjective|Having lived only a short time|bata|Kaunti pa lamang ang edad|The team is very young.

# ---------------- Useful Adverbs ----------------
abruptly|adverb|Suddenly and unexpectedly|biglaan|Nang biglaan|The music stopped abruptly.
absolutely|adverb|Completely and totally|lubusan|Nang buo at lubos|You are absolutely right.
actually|adverb|In fact, or really|talaga|Sa totoo lang|It is actually quite simple.
badly|adverb|In a poor manner, or very much|masama|Sa mahinang paraan o labis|The team played badly.
barely|adverb|Only just, almost not|halos hindi|Halos hindi|We barely caught the train.
briefly|adverb|For a short time|sandali|Sa maikling panahon|He spoke briefly.
carefully|adverb|With attention and caution|maingat|Nang may pag-iingat|Read the label carefully.
certainly|adverb|Without any doubt|tiyak|Walang alinlangan|That is certainly true.
clearly|adverb|In a way that is easy to understand|malinaw|Sa paraang madaling maunawaan|Please speak clearly.
closely|adverb|In a careful and detailed way|mabuti|Sa maingat at masusing paraan|Watch closely.
completely|adverb|Totally and entirely|lubusan|Nang lubos at buo|I completely agree.
constantly|adverb|All the time|palagi|Sa lahat ng oras|He is constantly late.
deliberately|adverb|On purpose|sinadya|Nang may layunin|She deliberately ignored him.
directly|adverb|In a straight line, or without delay|tuwiran|Sa tuwid na daan o agad|Go directly home.
easily|adverb|Without difficulty|madali|Nang walang hirap|She easily won the race.
effectively|adverb|In a way that produces results|epektibo|Sa paraang may resulta|The drug works effectively.
entirely|adverb|Completely|buung-buo|Nang lubusan|That is entirely my fault.
equally|adverb|To the same degree|pantay|Sa parehong antas|Both are equally important.
especially|adverb|More than usually|lalo na|Higit sa karaniwan|I like fruit, especially mangoes.
exactly|adverb|Precisely and with no error|eksakto|Nang tumpak at walang mali|That is exactly what I meant.
extremely|adverb|To a very great degree|napaka|Sa napakataas na antas|It was extremely cold.
fairly|adverb|To a moderate degree, or justly|medyo|Sa katamtamang antas|The film was fairly good.
finally|adverb|After a long time, or lastly|sa wakas|Matapos ang mahabang panahon|We finally arrived at nine.
firmly|adverb|In a strong and definite way|matatag|Nang matatag at tiyak|She firmly refused.
frankly|adverb|In an honest and direct way|prangka|Nang tapat at tuwiran|Frankly, I disagree.
generally|adverb|In most cases|karaniwan|Sa karamihan ng pagkakataon|Generally, the service is good.
gently|adverb|In a soft and careful way|banayad|Sa malumanay na paraan|Stir the mixture gently.
hardly|adverb|Almost not at all|halos hindi|Halos wala|I hardly know her.
heavily|adverb|To a great degree|malakas|Sa malaking antas|It rained heavily.
honestly|adverb|In a truthful way|tapat|Sa totoong paraan|Honestly, I forgot.
immediately|adverb|At once, without delay|agad|Kaagad, walang antala|Call me immediately.
instantly|adverb|At once|kaagad|Sa isang iglap|She recognised him instantly.
largely|adverb|Mostly|halos lahat|Sa kalakhang bahagi|The plan was largely successful.
literally|adverb|In the exact sense of the words|literal|Sa tunay na kahulugan ng salita|He literally ran all the way.
merely|adverb|Only and nothing more|lamang|Wala nang iba|It was merely a suggestion.
mostly|adverb|In most cases or for the most part|kadalasan|Sa halos lahat ng pagkakataon|The audience was mostly students.
nearly|adverb|Almost|halos|Malapit nang mangyari|We are nearly finished.
obviously|adverb|In a way that is easy to see|halata|Sa paraang madaling makita|He was obviously tired.
particularly|adverb|Especially, more than others|lalo na|Higit sa iba|I particularly liked the ending.
perfectly|adverb|In a faultless way|perpekto|Nang walang kapintasan|The lid fits perfectly.
possibly|adverb|Perhaps, maybe|maaari|Marahil o baka|That could possibly work.
precisely|adverb|Exactly|tumpak|Nang eksakto|Arrive at precisely nine.
probably|adverb|Most likely|malamang|Malaki ang tsansa|She will probably call.
properly|adverb|In the correct way|maayos|Sa tamang paraan|Fit the part properly.
quickly|adverb|At a fast speed|mabilis|Sa mabilis na paraan|Answer quickly.
quietly|adverb|Without much noise|tahimik|Nang walang ingay|Close the door quietly.
rarely|adverb|Not often|bihira|Hindi madalas|She rarely complains.
really|adverb|Truly or very|talaga|Tunay o napaka|It was really helpful.
recently|adverb|A short time ago|kamakailan|Kani-kanina lamang|We recently moved house.
roughly|adverb|Approximately, or not gently|humigit-kumulang|Halos o hindi banayad|It costs roughly fifty pounds.
seriously|adverb|In a serious manner|seryoso|Sa seryosong paraan|Take the warning seriously.
sharply|adverb|Suddenly and severely|matalas|Nang bigla at matindi|Prices rose sharply.

strongly|adverb|With great force or conviction|mariin|Nang may lakas o paniniwala|I strongly recommend it.
suddenly|adverb|Quickly and without warning|bigla|Mabilis at walang babala|The lights suddenly went out.
surely|adverb|Certainly, without doubt|tiyak|Walang alinlangan|Surely that cannot be right.
totally|adverb|Completely|lubos|Nang buo|I totally forgot.
truly|adverb|Genuinely and sincerely|tunay|Tunay at taos-puso|I am truly sorry.
unfortunately|adverb|Sadly, by bad luck|sa kasamaang-palad|Sa kasawiang-palad|Unfortunately, the shop was closed.

willingly|adverb|Readily and without objection|kusang-loob|Nang walang pagtutol|She willingly helped.

guidance|noun|Advice and help|gabay|Payo at tulong|Students need guidance.
habitat loss|noun|The destruction of natural homes|pagkasira ng tirahan|Pagkawasak ng likas na tahanan|Habitat loss threatens many species.
harmony among neighbours|noun|Peaceful agreement between people|pagkakaisa|Mapayapang pagkakasundo|There is harmony among neighbours here.
health|noun|The condition of your body and mind|kalusugan|Kalagayan ng katawan at isip|Look after your health.
hope|noun|A feeling that something good may happen|pag-asa|Damdaming may mabuting mangyayari|There is still hope.
idea|noun|A thought or plan|ideya|Isipin o plano|That is a good idea.
image|noun|A picture, or how somebody is seen|imahe|Larawan o pagtingin sa isang tao|The company changed its image.
impact|noun|A strong effect on something|epekto|Malakas na bisa sa isang bagay|The law had a real impact.
importance|noun|The quality of being significant|kahalagahan|Katangian ng pagiging mahalaga|Do not underestimate its importance.
improvement|noun|A change that makes something better|pagbuti|Pagbabagong nagpapabuti|There is room for improvement.
incident|noun|An event, often unpleasant|insidente|Pangyayari, kadalasang hindi kaaya-aya|Report any incident at once.
industry|noun|The making of goods on a large scale|industriya|Malawakang paggawa ng produkto|The steel industry declined.
injury|noun|Physical harm to the body|pinsala|Pisikal na sakit sa katawan|He recovered from the injury.
instance|noun|A single example|pagkakataon|Isang halimbawa|In this instance, she was right.
instrument|noun|A tool, or a device for music|instrumento|Kasangkapan o panugtog|The violin is a fine instrument.
intention|noun|What you plan to do|intensyon|Ang balak mong gawin|My intention was to help.
interest|noun|A wish to know more, or money paid on a loan|interes|Nais malaman o tubo sa utang|She has an interest in history.
journey|noun|An act of travelling from one place to another|paglalakbay|Pagbiyahe mula sa isang lugar|The journey took six hours.
judgement|noun|The ability to make good decisions|pagpapasya|Kakayahang gumawa ng mabuting desisyon|Trust your judgement.
knowledge|noun|Information and understanding you have|kaalaman|Impormasyon at pag-unawang taglay|Knowledge grows with reading.
lack|noun|The state of not having enough|kakulangan|Kalagayan ng kawalan ng sapat|There is a lack of space.
level|noun|A position on a scale|antas|Posisyon sa isang sukatan|Water levels are rising.
limit|noun|The greatest amount allowed|hangganan|Pinakamataas na pinapayagan|There is a speed limit.
link|noun|A connection between things|kawing|Ugnayan sa pagitan ng bagay|There is a link between the two.
location|noun|A particular place|lokasyon|Tiyak na lugar|The location is ideal.
loss|noun|The state of no longer having something|pagkawala|Kalagayan ng kawalan|The loss was deeply felt.
majority|noun|The greater number of people or things|nakararami|Mas malaking bilang|The majority agreed.
manner|noun|The way something is done|paraan|Paraan ng paggawa|He answered in a calm manner.
material|noun|The substance something is made from|materyal|Sangkap na pinaggawaan|Choose a strong material.
matter|noun|A subject or situation to deal with|usapin|Paksa o sitwasyong dapat asikasuhin|It is a serious matter.
method|noun|A particular way of doing something|paraan|Tiyak na paraan ng paggawa|Try a different method.
mind|noun|The part of you that thinks and feels|isip|Bahaging nag-iisip at nakakadama|Keep an open mind.
mistake|noun|Something done wrongly|pagkakamali|Bagay na maling nagawa|Everyone makes mistakes.
moment|noun|A very short period of time|sandali|Napakaikling panahon|Wait a moment.
motion|noun|Movement, or a formal proposal|galaw|Paggalaw o pormal na mungkahi|The motion was carried.
nature|noun|The physical world, or a person's character|kalikasan|Pisikal na daigdig o ugali ng tao|It is not in his nature to lie.
need|noun|Something necessary|pangangailangan|Bagay na kailangan|There is a need for change.
occasion|noun|A particular time when something happens|okasyon|Tiyak na pagkakataon|It was a special occasion.

pain|noun|An unpleasant physical feeling|sakit|Di-kanais-nais na pisikal na pakiramdam|The pain has eased.

peace|noun|A state without war or trouble|kapayapaan|Kalagayang walang digmaan|The treaty brought peace.
percentage point|noun|A unit for comparing percentages|puntong porsyento|Yunit sa paghahambing ng porsyento|Rates rose by two percentage points.

phase|noun|A stage in a process|yugto|Bahagi ng isang proseso|We are in the final phase.
plan|noun|A set of intended actions|plano|Nakatakdang mga hakbang|The plan needs approval.
policy|noun|An official set of rules or aims|patakaran|Opisyal na tuntunin o layunin|The policy changed in April.
population|noun|All the people living in a place|populasyon|Lahat ng nakatira sa isang lugar|The population is growing.
position|noun|A place, or a job|posisyon|Lugar o trabaho|She applied for the position.
possibility|noun|Something that may happen|posibilidad|Bagay na maaaring mangyari|There is a possibility of rain.
practice|noun|Repeated activity to improve a skill|pagsasanay|Paulit-ulit na gawain para umunlad|Practice makes you faster.
pressure|noun|Force, or a demanding influence|presyon|Puwersa o mabigat na impluwensya|He works well under pressure.
priority|noun|Something treated as most important|prayoridad|Bagay na itinuturing na pinakamahalaga|Safety is our first priority.
problem|noun|A difficulty needing a solution|problema|Hirap na kailangang lutasin|We solved the problem quickly.
procedure|noun|An official way of doing something|pamamaraan|Opisyal na paraan ng paggawa|Follow the correct procedure.
progress|noun|Movement towards a goal|pag-unlad|Paggalaw patungo sa layunin|We are making progress.
project|noun|A planned piece of work|proyekto|Planadong gawain|The project ends in June.
property|noun|Something owned, especially land|ari-arian|Pag-aari lalo na ang lupa|Property prices have risen.
proportion of income|noun|A part in relation to the whole|bahagi|Bahagi kaugnay ng kabuuan|Rent takes a large proportion of income.
protection|noun|The act of keeping somebody safe|proteksyon|Pagpapanatiling ligtas|The law offers protection.
purpose|noun|The reason something is done|layunin|Dahilan ng paggawa|What is the purpose of this form?
quality|noun|How good something is|kalidad|Antas ng kabutihan|The quality is excellent.
range|noun|The extent between limits|saklaw|Lawak sa pagitan ng hangganan|We stock a wide range.
rate|noun|A speed, or a fixed charge|antas|Bilis o takdang singil|The rate has increased.
reaction|noun|A response to something|reaksyon|Tugon sa isang bagay|Her reaction surprised me.
reality|noun|Things as they actually are|katotohanan|Mga bagay sa tunay nilang anyo|Face reality.
record|noun|A written account, or a best performance|talaan|Nakasulat na tala o pinakamahusay na tala|Keep a record of expenses.
region|noun|A large area of a country|rehiyon|Malaking bahagi ng isang bansa|The region is mainly rural.
relationship|noun|The way people are connected|relasyon|Paraan ng pagkakaugnay ng tao|They have a close relationship.
requirement|noun|Something needed or demanded|kinakailangan|Bagay na kailangan|Meet every requirement.
resource|noun|Something useful that can be used|yaman|Bagay na kapaki-pakinabang|Water is a precious resource.
response|noun|An answer or reaction|tugon|Sagot o reaksyon|We await your response.
responsibility|noun|A duty to deal with something|responsibilidad|Tungkuling asikasuhin ang isang bagay|Take responsibility for it.
result|noun|What happens because of something|resulta|Bunga ng isang bagay|The results arrive tomorrow.
risk|noun|The chance that something bad happens|panganib|Posibilidad ng masamang mangyari|There is little risk.
role|noun|The part somebody plays|papel|Bahaging ginagampanan|She plays a key role.
rule|noun|An instruction about what is allowed|panuntunan|Tagubilin sa pinapayagan|Follow the rules.
safety|noun|The state of being free from danger|kaligtasan|Kalagayang walang panganib|Safety comes first.
scale|noun|The size or extent of something|sukat|Laki o lawak ng isang bagay|The scale of the task is huge.
scene|noun|A place where something happens|tagpo|Lugar ng pangyayari|Police reached the scene quickly.
section|noun|One part of something larger|seksyon|Isang bahagi ng mas malaki|Read the first section.
security|noun|Protection from danger or loss|seguridad|Proteksyon mula sa panganib|Airport security is tight.
sense|noun|A feeling, or the ability to understand|pandama|Pakiramdam o kakayahang umunawa|She has a good sense of humour.
series|noun|A number of similar things in order|serye|Sunod-sunod na magkakatulad na bagay|A series of talks began.
service|noun|Work done for others|serbisyo|Gawaing para sa iba|The service was excellent.
shape|noun|The outer form of something|hugis|Panlabas na anyo|Cut it into a round shape.
share|noun|A part belonging to one person|bahagi|Bahaging para sa isang tao|Take your fair share.
shortage|noun|A lack of something needed|kakulangan|Kawalan ng kailangan|There is a water shortage.
situation|noun|The circumstances at a particular time|sitwasyon|Kalagayan sa isang panahon|The situation has improved.
skill|noun|The ability to do something well|kasanayan|Kakayahang gumawa nang mahusay|Listening is a valuable skill.
solution|noun|A way of solving a problem|solusyon|Paraan ng paglutas ng problema|We found a simple solution.
source|noun|The place something comes from|pinagmulan|Lugar na pinanggalingan|Name your source.
space|noun|An empty area available for use|espasyo|Bakanteng lugar na magagamit|There is space for two more.
speed|noun|How fast something moves|bilis|Kabilisan ng paggalaw|Reduce your speed.
staff|noun|The people who work for an organisation|kawani|Mga taong nagtatrabaho sa samahan|The staff are very helpful.
standard|noun|A level of quality expected|pamantayan|Antas ng kalidad na inaasahan|The work meets the standard.
statement|noun|Something said or written formally|pahayag|Bagay na pormal na sinabi o isinulat|She issued a short statement.
status|noun|A person's position or the current state|katayuan|Posisyon o kasalukuyang kalagayan|Check the status of your order.
step|noun|One action in a process|hakbang|Isang kilos sa proseso|Take it one step at a time.
strength|noun|The quality of being strong|lakas|Katangian ng pagiging malakas|Honesty is her greatest strength.
success|noun|The achieving of a desired aim|tagumpay|Pagkamit ng ninanais na layunin|Her success was well earned.
surface|noun|The outside layer of something|ibabaw|Panlabas na bahagi|Wipe the surface clean.
system|noun|A set of connected parts working together|sistema|Magkakaugnay na bahaging nagsasama|The system works well.
task|noun|A piece of work to be done|gawain|Trabahong dapat gawin|Divide the task between you.
technique|noun|A particular way of doing something skilfully|teknik|Tiyak na paraan ng mahusay na paggawa|Her technique is flawless.
tendency|noun|A likelihood of behaving in a certain way|hilig|Posibilidad ng ganitong pagkilos|He has a tendency to worry.
tension|noun|A feeling of nervous strain|tensyon|Damdamin ng pagkabalisa|Tension filled the room.
threat|noun|A warning of harm, or a danger|banta|Babala ng pinsala o panganib|Pollution is a serious threat.
tool|noun|An object used to do work|kasangkapan|Bagay na ginagamit sa trabaho|Choose the right tool.
trade|noun|The buying and selling of goods|kalakalan|Pagbili at pagbebenta ng paninda|Trade between them grew.
treatment|noun|Medical care, or how you deal with somebody|paggamot|Pangangalagang medikal o pakikitungo|The treatment worked well.
trend|noun|A general direction of change|takbo|Pangkalahatang direksyon ng pagbabago|The trend is upwards.
trial|noun|A test, or a court hearing|pagsubok|Pagsusuri o paglilitis|The trial begins on Monday.
trouble|noun|Difficulty or problems|gulo|Hirap o suliranin|We had trouble starting the car.
truth|noun|What is real and correct|katotohanan|Ang tunay at tama|Tell me the truth.
type|noun|A category of things|uri|Kategorya ng mga bagay|What type of music is it?
understanding|noun|Knowledge of how something works|pag-unawa|Kaalaman sa paggana ng bagay|She has a deep understanding.
unit|noun|A single complete thing, or a measure|yunit|Isang buong bagay o panukat|The metre is a unit of length.
use|noun|The act of using something|paggamit|Ang paggamit ng isang bagay|The use of phones is banned.
value|noun|The worth or importance of something|halaga|Kahalagahan ng isang bagay|The value has doubled.
variety|noun|A number of different kinds|iba't ibang uri|Maraming magkakaibang uri|We stock a variety of sizes.
version|noun|A particular form of something|bersyon|Tiyak na anyo ng isang bagay|This is the latest version.
view|noun|An opinion, or what you can see|pananaw|Opinyon o natatanaw|In my view, that is wrong.
volume of work|noun|The amount of something|dami|Dami ng isang bagay|The volume of work has doubled.
warning|noun|A statement about possible danger|babala|Pahayag tungkol sa panganib|Ignore the warning at your peril.
waste|noun|Unwanted material, or careless use|basura|Di-kailangang materyal o maluhong paggamit|Reduce food waste.
way|noun|A method, or a route|paraan|Pamamaraan o daan|Is there another way?
weakness|noun|A lack of strength, or a fault|kahinaan|Kakulangan sa lakas o kapintasan|Impatience is his weakness.
wealth|noun|A large amount of money and possessions|kayamanan|Malaking halaga ng pera at ari-arian|The region has great mineral wealth.
work|noun|Activity done to achieve a purpose|trabaho|Gawaing may layunin|The work is nearly done.
worth|noun|The value of something|halaga|Halaga ng isang bagay|The painting's worth is unknown.

# ---------------- Phrasal Verbs ----------------
account for|phrasal verb|To explain or be the cause of something|ipaliwanag|Ipaliwanag o maging dahilan|Wages account for most of the cost.
add up|phrasal verb|To make sense, or to total|magkatuwiran|Magkaroon ng saysay o magsama-sama|His story does not add up.
allow for|phrasal verb|To take something into account|isaalang-alang|Isaalang-alang ang isang bagay|Allow for delays on the road.
answer back|phrasal verb|To reply rudely to somebody in authority|sumagot nang bastos|Sumagot nang walang galang|Do not answer back to your teacher.
ask after|phrasal verb|To enquire about somebody's health|magtanong tungkol sa iba|Itanong ang kalagayan ng iba|She asked after your mother.
back down|phrasal verb|To stop demanding something|umatras|Ihinto ang paghingi|Neither side would back down.
bank on|phrasal verb|To rely on something happening|umasa|Manalig na mangyayari|Do not bank on good weather.
bear out|phrasal verb|To support that something is true|patunayan|Suportahan ang katotohanan|The figures bear out her claim.
blow up|phrasal verb|To explode, or to become very angry|sumabog|Pumutok o magalit nang matindi|The boiler blew up.
break in|phrasal verb|To enter a building by force|pumasok nang sapilitan|Pumasok sa gusali sa pamamagitan ng lakas|Thieves broke in overnight.
break out|phrasal verb|To start suddenly, of war or disease|sumiklab|Biglang magsimula|Fighting broke out at dawn.
bring about|phrasal verb|To cause something to happen|maging sanhi|Maging dahilan ng pangyayari|The law brought about real change.
bring forward|phrasal verb|To move an event to an earlier time|iusog nang mas maaga|Ilipat sa mas maagang oras|They brought the meeting forward.
bring out|phrasal verb|To produce or publish something new|ilabas|Gumawa o maglathala ng bago|The band brought out an album.
brush up on|phrasal verb|To improve a skill you have not used|balikan|Pagbutihin ang kasanayang matagal nang hindi ginamit|I must brush up on my French.
build up|phrasal verb|To increase gradually|dumami|Unti-unting tumaas|Pressure built up slowly.
call for|phrasal verb|To demand or require something|humiling|Hingin o kailanganin|The situation calls for patience.
call on|phrasal verb|To visit briefly, or to ask somebody to act|dumalaw|Bisitahin o hilingang kumilos|I called on my aunt yesterday.
carry off|phrasal verb|To do something difficult successfully|magtagumpay|Gawin nang matagumpay ang mahirap|She carried off the speech well.
catch on|phrasal verb|To become popular, or to understand|umuso|Maging popular o maintindihan|The idea quickly caught on.
catch up|phrasal verb|To reach the same level as others|makahabol|Umabot sa antas ng iba|He worked hard to catch up.
clear up|phrasal verb|To tidy, or of weather to improve|maglinis|Mag-ayos o bumuti ang panahon|The weather cleared up by noon.
come along|phrasal verb|To make progress, or to accompany|umunlad|Umunlad o sumama|Her work is coming along nicely.
come down to|phrasal verb|To be essentially a matter of|bumagsak sa|Bumaba sa usapin ng|It comes down to money.
come round|phrasal verb|To regain consciousness, or to visit|magkamalay|Magkamalay o dumalaw|He came round after a minute.
count in|phrasal verb|To include somebody in an activity|isali|Isama sa gawain|Count me in for Saturday.
crack down on|phrasal verb|To take strong action against something|higpitan|Magsagawa ng mahigpit na aksyon|Police cracked down on speeding.
cut off|phrasal verb|To stop a supply, or to isolate|putulin|Ihinto ang suplay o ihiwalay|The village was cut off by snow.
deal with|phrasal verb|To handle a problem or person|harapin|Asikasuhin ang problema o tao|She deals with complaints.
draw up|phrasal verb|To prepare a document|ihanda|Gumawa ng dokumento|Lawyers drew up the contract.
drop in|phrasal verb|To visit without arranging it|dumaan|Dumalaw nang hindi nagpaalam|Drop in whenever you like.
drop out|phrasal verb|To leave a course before finishing|tumigil|Umalis bago matapos|He dropped out in his second year.
face up to|phrasal verb|To accept a difficult situation|harapin|Tanggapin ang mahirap na sitwasyon|You must face up to the truth.
fall apart|phrasal verb|To break into pieces, or to fail|mawasak|Magkapira-piraso o mabigo|The plan fell apart.
fall out|phrasal verb|To quarrel with somebody|mag-away|Makipag-away sa iba|The brothers fell out years ago.
fall through|phrasal verb|To fail to happen as planned|hindi matuloy|Mabigong maganap|The sale fell through.
fill out|phrasal verb|To complete a form|punan|Sagutan ang porma|Fill out both sides.
fit in|phrasal verb|To be accepted by a group|makibagay|Matanggap ng grupo|She fits in well at work.
get across|phrasal verb|To make an idea understood|maiparating|Maipaunawa ang ideya|He got his point across clearly.
get around|phrasal verb|To travel from place to place|makagalaw|Makapaglakbay|It is easy to get around by bus.
get at|phrasal verb|To mean or suggest something|ipahiwatig|Ibig sabihin o ipahiwatig|What are you getting at?
get away with|phrasal verb|To avoid punishment for something|makalusot|Makaiwas sa parusa|He got away with a warning.
get back to|phrasal verb|To reply to somebody later|bumalik sa|Sagutin sa ibang pagkakataon|I will get back to you tomorrow.
get down to|phrasal verb|To start doing something seriously|magsimula nang seryoso|Simulan nang taos-puso|Let us get down to business.
get on with|phrasal verb|To have a good relationship, or to continue|makisama|Magkasundo o magpatuloy|They get on with each other well.
get through|phrasal verb|To finish, or to make contact|matapos|Matapos o makausap|I could not get through to her.
give back|phrasal verb|To return something|isauli|Ibalik ang isang bagay|Give back the book when done.
give off|phrasal verb|To produce a smell, heat or light|maglabas|Magpalabas ng amoy o init|The lamp gives off little heat.
give out|phrasal verb|To distribute, or to stop working|mamigay|Ipamahagi o tumigil sa paggana|They gave out leaflets.
go about|phrasal verb|To start dealing with a task|simulan|Simulang harapin ang gawain|How should I go about this?
go ahead|phrasal verb|To proceed with something|magpatuloy|Ituloy ang isang bagay|Go ahead and start without me.
go along with|phrasal verb|To agree to a plan|sumang-ayon|Pumayag sa plano|I will go along with your idea.
go back on|phrasal verb|To fail to keep a promise|bawiin|Hindi tuparin ang pangako|He went back on his word.
go down with|phrasal verb|To become ill with something|magkasakit|Tamaan ng sakit|She went down with flu.
go off|phrasal verb|To explode or ring, or of food to spoil|tumunog|Pumutok, tumunog o mapanis|The alarm went off at six.
go out|phrasal verb|To leave home for entertainment|lumabas|Umalis para maglibang|We go out on Fridays.
go up|phrasal verb|To rise in price or level|tumaas|Tumaas ang presyo o antas|Fares go up in April.
grow into|phrasal verb|To develop into something|maging|Maging isang bagay|He grew into a fine musician.
hand out|phrasal verb|To give things to several people|mamigay|Magbigay sa maraming tao|Teachers handed out the papers.
hand over|phrasal verb|To give something to somebody in authority|isuko|Ibigay sa may kapangyarihan|He handed over the keys.
hang up|phrasal verb|To end a telephone call|ibaba ang telepono|Tapusin ang tawag|She hung up without a word.
head for|phrasal verb|To move towards a place|tumungo|Pumunta sa isang lugar|We headed for the coast.
hold back|phrasal verb|To prevent progress, or to hide feelings|pigilan|Hadlangan o itago ang damdamin|Fear held her back.
hold up|phrasal verb|To delay, or to rob|antalahin|Mag-antala o magnakaw|Traffic held us up.
keep up|phrasal verb|To move or progress at the same rate|makasabay|Makasabay sa bilis o antas|I cannot keep up with the class.
knock out|phrasal verb|To make somebody unconscious|matulog|Mawalan ng malay|The blow knocked him out.
lay off|phrasal verb|To dismiss workers because of low demand|tanggalin sa trabaho|Alisin sa trabaho dahil sa kakulangan|The firm laid off fifty staff.
lead to|phrasal verb|To result in something|magbunga|Magresulta sa isang bagay|Poor diet leads to illness.
leave out|phrasal verb|To omit something|iwanan|Huwag isama|You left out one name.
live up to|phrasal verb|To be as good as expected|abutin ang inaasahan|Maging kasinghusay ng inaasahan|The film lived up to its reviews.
look into|phrasal verb|To investigate something|imbestigahan|Suriin ang isang bagay|We will look into the matter.
look up to|phrasal verb|To admire and respect somebody|hangaan|Igalang at hangaan|Younger players look up to her.
make out|phrasal verb|To manage to see or understand|matukoy|Makita o maunawaan|I could just make out the sign.
make up for|phrasal verb|To compensate for something|bawiin|Bayaran ang pagkukulang|He tried to make up for his error.
miss out|phrasal verb|To lose a chance to do something|malampasan|Mawalan ng pagkakataon|Do not miss out on the offer.
narrow down|phrasal verb|To reduce the number of choices|paliitin|Bawasan ang pagpipilian|We narrowed it down to three.
note down|phrasal verb|To write something so you remember|isulat|Isulat upang maalala|Note down the reference number.
opt for|phrasal verb|To choose one thing rather than another|piliin|Piliin ang isa sa iba|She opted for the later flight.
own up|phrasal verb|To admit that you did something wrong|umamin|Aminin ang maling nagawa|Nobody owned up to it.
pass on|phrasal verb|To give information to somebody else|ipaabot|Ibigay ang impormasyon sa iba|Please pass on my thanks.
pass out|phrasal verb|To lose consciousness|himatayin|Mawalan ng malay|He passed out in the heat.
pay back|phrasal verb|To return money you borrowed|bayaran|Isauli ang hiniram na pera|I paid back the loan early.
pay off|phrasal verb|To settle a debt, or to succeed|magbunga|Bayaran ang utang o magtagumpay|The hard work paid off.
put across|phrasal verb|To communicate an idea clearly|iparating|Maipaliwanag nang malinaw|She puts her ideas across well.
put down|phrasal verb|To place on a surface, or to criticise|ibaba|Ilagay o punahin|Do not put yourself down.
put forward|phrasal verb|To suggest an idea for consideration|imungkahi|Ilahad ang ideya|He put forward a new plan.

rely on|phrasal verb|To depend on somebody|umasa|Manalig sa isang tao|You can rely on him.
result in|phrasal verb|To cause a particular outcome|magresulta|Maging sanhi ng kinalabasan|The delay resulted in extra costs.
rule out|phrasal verb|To decide something is not possible|hindi isaalang-alang|Alisin sa posibilidad|Police ruled out foul play.
run through|phrasal verb|To review something quickly|balikan|Suriin nang mabilis|Let us run through the plan.
see off|phrasal verb|To go with somebody to say goodbye|ihatid|Samahan upang magpaalam|We saw her off at the airport.
see through|phrasal verb|To recognise a lie, or to finish|makita ang totoo|Makilala ang kasinungalingan|I saw through his excuse.
send off|phrasal verb|To post something, or to order a player off|ipadala|Ipadala o palabasin sa laro|Send off the form today.
set aside|phrasal verb|To save something for later use|ilaan|Itabi para sa hinaharap|Set aside an hour for revision.
set out|phrasal verb|To begin a journey, or to explain|magsimula|Umalis o ipaliwanag|The report sets out the findings.
settle down|phrasal verb|To become calm, or to start a stable life|humupa|Kumalma o magtatag ng buhay|The children settled down quickly.
sign up|phrasal verb|To join a course or activity|magpalista|Sumali sa kurso o gawain|Sign up before Friday.
sort through|phrasal verb|To examine things to organise them|pagbukud-bukurin|Suriin upang ayusin|I sorted through the papers.
speak up|phrasal verb|To talk louder, or to say what you think|magsalita nang malakas|Magsalita nang mas malakas o magpahayag|Please speak up at the back.
stand by|phrasal verb|To support somebody, or to wait ready|tumabi|Suportahan o maghintay|She stood by him throughout.
stand out|phrasal verb|To be easily noticed as different|tumingkad|Maging kapansin-pansin|Her essay really stood out.
step down|phrasal verb|To resign from a position|magbitiw|Magbitiw sa posisyon|The chairman stepped down.
stick to|phrasal verb|To continue with something without changing|manatili|Magpatuloy nang hindi nagbabago|Stick to the plan.
sum up|phrasal verb|To give a brief summary|buodin|Magbigay ng maikling buod|Let me sum up the argument.
switch off|phrasal verb|To turn off a device, or to stop paying attention|patayin|Isara ang kagamitan|Switch off the lights.
take after|phrasal verb|To resemble an older relative|magmana|Katulad ng nakatatandang kamag-anak|She takes after her mother.
take apart|phrasal verb|To separate something into pieces|buwagin|Hiwalayin ang mga bahagi|He took the engine apart.
take back|phrasal verb|To return something, or to withdraw a statement|ibalik|Isauli o bawiin ang sinabi|I take back what I said.
take in|phrasal verb|To understand, or to give shelter|maunawaan|Maintindihan o magpatuloy|It was a lot to take in.
take on|phrasal verb|To accept work, or to employ|tanggapin|Tanggapin ang gawain o magtrabaho|We took on three new staff.
take out|phrasal verb|To remove, or to arrange a loan|ilabas|Alisin o kumuha ng utang|She took out insurance.
talk over|phrasal verb|To discuss something thoroughly|pag-usapan|Talakayin nang masinsinan|Let us talk it over tomorrow.
tell off|phrasal verb|To speak angrily to somebody who did wrong|pagalitan|Sawayin ang nagkamali|The coach told him off.
think over|phrasal verb|To consider something carefully|pag-isipan|Isaalang-alang nang mabuti|Think it over before deciding.
throw out|phrasal verb|To get rid of something, or to reject|itapon|Alisin o tanggihan|The court threw out the case.
tidy away|phrasal verb|To put things back in their place|iligpit|Ibalik sa tamang lugar|Please tidy away the tools.
track down|phrasal verb|To find after a long search|matunton|Matagpuan matapos maghanap|We tracked down the owner.
turn into|phrasal verb|To become something different|maging|Maging ibang bagay|The rain turned into snow.
turn out|phrasal verb|To happen in a particular way|maging|Maging ganito ang kalabasan|It turned out to be simple.
wear off|phrasal verb|To gradually stop having an effect|mawala|Unti-unting mawala ang bisa|The pain wore off by noon.
weigh up|phrasal verb|To consider the pros and cons|timbangin|Isaalang-alang ang mabuti at masama|Weigh up your options.
wipe out|phrasal verb|To destroy completely|puksain|Wasakin nang tuluyan|Disease wiped out the herd.
work through|phrasal verb|To deal with something step by step|harapin|Lutasin nang paisa-isa|We worked through the list.
write down|phrasal verb|To record something in writing|isulat|Itala sa papel|Write down the address.
write off|phrasal verb|To accept a loss, or to damage beyond repair|isulat na wala|Tanggapin ang pagkalugi|The car was written off.
zoom in|phrasal verb|To look at something more closely|palakihin|Tingnan nang mas malapit|Zoom in on the detail.

# ---------------- Emotions & Feelings ----------------
affection for family|noun|A warm feeling of caring|pagmamahal|Mainit na damdamin ng pagmamalasakit|He shows deep affection for family.

fury|noun|Extreme anger|matinding galit|Napakatinding galit|He shook with fury.
gladness|noun|A feeling of happiness|kagalakan|Damdamin ng kaligayahan|There was gladness in her voice.
gloom|noun|A feeling of sadness and hopelessness|lungkot|Damdamin ng lungkot at kawalang pag-asa|A sense of gloom hung over the town.
hatred|noun|A very strong dislike|poot|Napakatinding pagkasuklam|Hatred solves nothing.
hesitation|noun|A pause caused by uncertainty|pag-aalangan|Sandaling huminto dahil sa pagdududa|She agreed without hesitation.
homesickness|noun|Sadness caused by being away from home|pangungulila|Lungkot dahil malayo sa tahanan|Homesickness hit him hard.
hostility|noun|Strong unfriendly feeling|poot|Matinding di-magiliw na damdamin|He met open hostility.
impatience|noun|Unwillingness to wait calmly|pagkainip|Ayaw maghintay nang mahinahon|Her impatience showed.
indifference|noun|A lack of interest or concern|kawalang-interes|Kawalan ng interes o malasakit|Public indifference worried them.
insecurity|noun|A lack of confidence in yourself|kawalang-katiyakan|Kawalan ng tiwala sa sarili|Insecurity held her back.
irritation|noun|A feeling of mild anger|pagkairita|Damdamin ng bahagyang galit|There was irritation in his tone.
joy|noun|A feeling of great happiness|galak|Damdamin ng matinding kaligayahan|The news brought great joy.
loneliness|noun|Sadness from being alone|kalungkutan|Lungkot dahil nag-iisa|Loneliness is common in winter.
love|noun|A deep feeling of affection|pag-ibig|Malalim na damdamin ng pagmamahal|Love changes everything.
panic|noun|Sudden uncontrollable fear|pagkataranta|Biglaan at di-mapigilang takot|Panic spread through the crowd.
passion|noun|A very strong feeling or enthusiasm|silakbo|Napakatinding damdamin o sigla|She speaks with real passion.

# ---------------- Behaviour & Actions ----------------

boast|verb|To talk too proudly about yourself|magyabang|Magsalita nang mapagmalaki|He never boasts about his work.
bully|verb|To frighten or hurt a weaker person|apihin|Takutin o saktan ang mahina|Nobody should be bullied at school.

# ---------------- Health & Medicine ----------------
ache|noun|A continuous dull pain|kirot|Tuloy-tuloy na mahinang sakit|I have an ache in my back.
allergy|noun|A bad reaction to a normally harmless substance|allergy|Masamang reaksyon sa karaniwang bagay|She has an allergy to nuts.
ambulance|noun|A vehicle for taking sick people to hospital|ambulansya|Sasakyan para sa maysakit|Call an ambulance at once.
anaesthetic|noun|A drug that stops you feeling pain|pampamanhid|Gamot na nag-aalis ng pakiramdam ng sakit|The dentist used a local anaesthetic.
bandage|noun|A strip of cloth for covering a wound|benda|Telang pantakip sa sugat|Wrap a bandage round the ankle.
blister|noun|A swelling on the skin filled with liquid|paltos|Pamamaga sa balat na may likido|New shoes gave me a blister.
bruise|noun|A dark mark on the skin from a knock|pasa|Maitim na marka sa balat|He has a bruise on his arm.
checkup|noun|A general medical examination|tsek-ap|Pangkalahatang pagsusuri sa kalusugan|Book a yearly checkup.
clinic|noun|A place where people receive medical treatment|klinika|Lugar ng paggamot|The clinic opens at eight.
consultation|noun|A meeting with a doctor for advice|konsultasyon|Pagpupulong sa doktor para sa payo|The consultation lasted ten minutes.
contagion|noun|The spreading of disease by contact|hawa|Pagkalat ng sakit sa pagdampi|Contagion spread through the ward.
cough|noun|A sudden noisy expulsion of air from the lungs|ubo|Biglaang paglabas ng hangin sa baga|A dry cough kept him awake.
dentist|noun|A doctor who treats teeth|dentista|Doktor ng ngipin|I see the dentist twice a year.
diagnose|verb|To identify an illness|masuri|Tukuyin ang sakit|Doctors diagnosed pneumonia.

dizziness|noun|A feeling that everything is spinning|pagkahilo|Pakiramdam na umiikot ang paligid|Dizziness may follow the injection.
fever|noun|A body temperature above normal|lagnat|Mataas na temperatura ng katawan|The fever broke overnight.
first aid|noun|Basic treatment given before a doctor arrives|paunang lunas|Unang lunas bago dumating ang doktor|She trained in first aid.
fracture|noun|A break in a bone|bali|Pagkabali ng buto|The scan showed a small fracture.
germ|noun|A tiny organism that causes disease|mikrobyo|Maliit na organismong nagdudulot ng sakit|Wash your hands to remove germs.
hospital|noun|A place where sick people are treated|ospital|Lugar ng paggamot sa maysakit|She works at the local hospital.
inflammation of the joint|noun|Painful swelling in part of the body|pamamaga|Masakit na pamamaga sa katawan|Rest reduces inflammation of the joint.
injection|noun|Medicine put into the body with a needle|iniksyon|Gamot na ipinapasok gamit ang karayom|The injection stung a little.
insulin|noun|A hormone controlling sugar in the blood|insulin|Hormonang kumokontrol sa asukal sa dugo|He takes insulin daily.
nurse|noun|A person trained to care for the sick|nars|Taong sanay mag-alaga sa maysakit|The nurse checked his pulse.
nutritionist|noun|An expert in food and health|nutrisyonista|Dalubhasa sa pagkain at kalusugan|A nutritionist planned her diet.
obesity|noun|The condition of being very overweight|labis na katabaan|Kalagayan ng sobrang timbang|Obesity raises many health risks.
operation|noun|Medical treatment involving cutting the body|operasyon|Paggamot na may paghiwa sa katawan|The operation went well.
ointment|noun|A thick cream put on the skin|pamahid|Makapal na krema sa balat|Apply the ointment twice daily.
paramedic|noun|A person trained to give emergency care|paramediko|Taong sanay sa agarang lunas|Paramedics arrived within minutes.
pharmacy|noun|A shop where medicines are sold|parmasya|Tindahan ng gamot|The pharmacy closes at six.
physiotherapy|noun|Treatment using exercise and massage|physiotherapy|Paggamot gamit ang ehersisyo at masahe|Physiotherapy restored his movement.
rash|noun|An area of red spots on the skin|pantal|Bahagi ng pulang butlig sa balat|The rash spread to her neck.
recovery time|noun|The period of getting well again|panahon ng paggaling|Panahon ng pagbalik sa kalusugan|Recovery time varies by patient.
scar|noun|A mark left after a wound heals|peklat|Markang naiwan matapos gumaling ang sugat|The scar has faded.
sneeze|verb|To blow air suddenly out of the nose|bumahin|Biglang maglabas ng hangin sa ilong|Cover your mouth when you sneeze.

# ---------------- History & Geography ----------------
archaeology|noun|The study of the past through remains|arkeolohiya|Pag-aaral ng nakaraan sa pamamagitan ng labi|Archaeology revealed a Roman road.
artefact|noun|An object made by people long ago|artepakto|Bagay na gawa ng tao noon|The artefact dates from 800 BC.
chronology|noun|The order in which events happened|kronolohiya|Pagkakasunod ng pangyayari|Establish the chronology first.
colony|noun|A country controlled by another|kolonya|Bansang kontrolado ng iba|The island was once a colony.
conquest|noun|The taking of control by force|pananakop|Pagkontrol sa pamamagitan ng lakas|The conquest changed the language.
dynasty|noun|A series of rulers from one family|dinastiya|Sunod-sunod na pinuno mula sa isang angkan|The dynasty lasted 300 years.
empire|noun|A group of countries under one ruler|imperyo|Grupo ng bansang pinamumunuan ng isa|The empire stretched to the sea.
excavation|noun|The digging up of buried remains|paghuhukay|Paghukay ng nakalibing na labi|The excavation began in May.
independence|noun|Freedom from control by another country|kalayaan|Kalayaan mula sa ibang bansa|They won independence in 1946.
invasion|noun|Entering a country by force|pananalakay|Pagpasok sa bansa gamit ang lakas|The invasion lasted six weeks.
medieval|adjective|Relating to the Middle Ages|medyebal|May kinalaman sa Gitnang Panahon|The medieval walls still stand.
monarchy|noun|A country ruled by a king or queen|monarkiya|Bansang pinamumunuan ng hari o reyna|Britain is a monarchy.
prehistoric|adjective|From before written records|prehistoriko|Bago pa ang nakasulat na tala|Prehistoric tools were found here.
republic|noun|A country whose head is elected|republika|Bansang halal ang pinuno|France became a republic in 1792.
revolution|noun|A complete overthrow of a government|rebolusyon|Lubos na pagpapabagsak ng pamahalaan|The revolution began in the capital.
ruins|noun|The remains of destroyed buildings|guho|Labi ng nasirang gusali|The ruins attract many visitors.
treaty of peace|noun|A formal agreement between countries|kasunduan|Pormal na kasunduan ng mga bansa|They signed a treaty of peace.
altitude|noun|The height above sea level|altitud|Taas mula sa lebel ng dagat|The village sits at high altitude.
climate zone|noun|An area with a particular type of weather|sona ng klima|Lugar na may tiyak na uri ng panahon|Deserts form a distinct climate zone.
continent|noun|One of the earth's large land masses|kontinente|Isa sa malalaking lupain ng mundo|Africa is a vast continent.
delta|noun|Land where a river splits before the sea|delta|Lupang hinahati ng ilog bago ang dagat|The delta is very fertile.
equator|noun|The imaginary line round the middle of the earth|ekwador|Guhit na pumapalibot sa gitna ng mundo|Countries near the equator are hot.
glacier|noun|A slow moving mass of ice|glasyar|Mabagal na gumagalaw na yelo|The glacier has retreated.
hemisphere|noun|One half of the earth|hemispero|Kalahati ng mundo|Summer differs by hemisphere.
latitude|noun|The distance north or south of the equator|latitud|Layo pahilaga o patimog ng ekwador|Note the latitude and longitude.
longitude|noun|The distance east or west of a fixed line|longhitud|Layo pasilangan o pakanluran|Longitude is measured in degrees.
plateau|noun|A large area of high flat land|talampas|Malawak na mataas at patag na lupa|Sheep graze on the plateau.
tributary|noun|A river flowing into a larger one|sanga ng ilog|Ilog na dumadaloy sa mas malaki|The stream is a tributary of the Thames.
tropics|noun|The hot regions near the equator|tropiko|Mainit na rehiyon malapit sa ekwador|Bananas grow in the tropics.
volcano|noun|A mountain that can throw out lava|bulkan|Bundok na maaaring magbuga ng lava|The volcano last erupted in 1991.

# ---------------- Religion & Belief ----------------
altar|noun|A table used in religious ceremonies|altar|Mesa sa seremonyang panrelihiyon|Flowers stood on the altar.
blessing|noun|A prayer asking for protection or favour|basbas|Panalanging humihingi ng proteksyon|The priest gave a blessing.
chapel|noun|A small place of worship|kapilya|Maliit na lugar ng pagsamba|The chapel seats forty.
congregation|noun|The people attending a religious service|kongregasyon|Mga taong dumadalo sa pagsamba|The congregation sang together.
devotion|noun|Deep religious feeling or loyalty|debosyon|Malalim na damdaming panrelihiyon|Her devotion never wavered.
fasting|noun|Going without food for religious reasons|pag-aayuno|Hindi pagkain dahil sa relihiyon|Fasting lasts a month.
hymn|noun|A religious song of praise|himno|Awit ng papuri sa relihiyon|They sang an old hymn.
meditation|noun|Quiet thought for relaxation or religion|meditasyon|Tahimik na pagmumuni|Meditation calms the mind.
missionary|noun|A person who spreads a religion abroad|misyonero|Taong nagpapalaganap ng relihiyon|A missionary founded the school.
monk|noun|A man living in a religious community|monghe|Lalaking nakatira sa komunidad panrelihiyon|The monk kept a vow of silence.
mosque|noun|A place of Muslim worship|moske|Lugar ng pagsamba ng Muslim|The mosque was built in 1900.
pilgrim|noun|A person travelling to a holy place|peregrino|Taong naglalakbay sa banal na lugar|Pilgrims walk the route each year.
prayer|noun|Words spoken to a god|panalangin|Salitang binibigkas sa diyos|She said a quiet prayer.
preach|verb|To give a religious talk|mangaral|Magbigay ng sermon|The vicar preached for ten minutes.
ritual practice|noun|A set of actions performed regularly|ritwal|Hanay ng kilos na regular na ginagawa|The ritual practice dates back centuries.
sacrifice|noun|The giving up of something valued|sakripisyo|Pagbibigay ng mahalagang bagay|They made great sacrifices.
saint|noun|A person recognised as very holy|santo|Taong kinikilalang napakabanal|The church is named after a saint.
scripture|noun|The sacred writings of a religion|banal na kasulatan|Banal na sulatin ng relihiyon|He quoted from scripture.
sermon|noun|A religious talk given in a service|sermon|Pananalitang panrelihiyon|The sermon lasted twenty minutes.
shrine|noun|A holy place associated with a person|dambana|Banal na lugar na may kaugnayan sa isang tao|Pilgrims visit the shrine.
spiritual|adjective|Relating to the soul rather than the body|espiritwal|May kinalaman sa kaluluwa|She had a spiritual awakening.
temple|noun|A building used for worship|templo|Gusaling ginagamit sa pagsamba|The temple stands on a hill.
worship|verb|To show respect and love for a god|sumamba|Magpakita ng paggalang sa diyos|They worship every Friday.

# ---------------- Work & Career ----------------
apprentice|noun|A person learning a trade from a skilled worker|aprentis|Taong natututo ng hanapbuhay|He started as an apprentice.
appraisal meeting|noun|A review of an employee's performance|pagsusuri|Pagrepaso sa gawa ng empleyado|Her appraisal meeting is on Friday.
bonus|noun|Extra money paid for good work|bonus|Karagdagang pera para sa mahusay na gawa|Staff received a Christmas bonus.
briefing|noun|A meeting giving instructions or information|briefing|Pulong na nagbibigay ng tagubilin|Attend the morning briefing.
career break|noun|A period away from work|pahinga sa karera|Panahong lumalayo sa trabaho|She took a career break to study.
colleague at work|noun|A person you work with|katrabaho|Taong kasama sa trabaho|Ask a colleague at work for help.
contract of employment|noun|A legal agreement about a job|kontrata sa trabaho|Legal na kasunduan sa trabaho|Read your contract of employment.
CV|noun|A summary of your education and work history|CV|Buod ng pinag-aralan at karanasan|Update your CV before applying.
delegate tasks|verb|To give work to somebody else|magtalaga|Ibigay ang gawain sa iba|Good managers delegate tasks.
department head|noun|The person leading a section|pinuno ng departamento|Namumuno sa isang bahagi|Speak to the department head.
dismissal notice|noun|A statement ending somebody's employment|abiso ng pagkakatanggal|Pahayag ng pagtatapos ng trabaho|He received a dismissal notice.
employer|noun|A person or company that pays workers|amo|Tao o kumpanyang nagbabayad ng manggagawa|A good employer invests in training.
employee|noun|A person who works for somebody else|empleyado|Taong nagtatrabaho para sa iba|Every employee gets training.
expertise in finance|noun|Special skill or knowledge|kadalubhasaan|Natatanging kasanayan o kaalaman|She has expertise in finance.
flexitime|noun|A system letting staff choose their hours|flexitime|Sistemang pumipili ng oras ng trabaho|Flexitime suits working parents.
freelancer|noun|A person working for several clients|freelancer|Taong nagtatrabaho para sa iba't ibang kliyente|She works as a freelancer.
grievance|noun|A formal complaint by an employee|reklamo|Pormal na reklamo ng empleyado|He filed a grievance.
headhunter|noun|A person who finds staff for senior jobs|headhunter|Taong naghahanap ng tao sa mataas na posisyon|A headhunter contacted her.
induction|noun|The introduction of a new employee to a job|oryentasyon|Pagpapakilala sa bagong empleyado|Induction takes two days.
job description|noun|A written list of a role's duties|paglalarawan ng trabaho|Nakasulat na listahan ng tungkulin|Read the job description carefully.
line manager|noun|The person directly above you at work|direktang superbisor|Taong nasa itaas mo sa trabaho|Report to your line manager.
maternity leave|noun|Time off work after having a baby|maternity leave|Bakasyon matapos manganak|She is on maternity leave.
notice period|noun|The time you must work before leaving|panahon ng abiso|Panahong dapat pa ring magtrabaho|My notice period is one month.
occupation|noun|A person's job or profession|hanapbuhay|Trabaho o propesyon ng tao|State your occupation on the form.
overtime pay|noun|Money for hours worked beyond normal|bayad sa obertaym|Bayad sa sobrang oras|Overtime pay is time and a half.
probation|noun|A trial period at the start of a job|probasyon|Panahon ng pagsubok sa simula ng trabaho|Probation lasts three months.
profession|noun|A job needing special training|propesyon|Trabahong nangangailangan ng pagsasanay|Nursing is a demanding profession.
promotion prospects|noun|The chance of moving to a higher post|pagkakataon sa promosyon|Tsansang umakyat sa mas mataas|The promotion prospects are good.
qualification required|noun|The training a job demands|kwalipikasyong kailangan|Pagsasanay na hinihingi ng trabaho|List the qualifications required.
redundancy pay|noun|Money given when a job is cut|bayad sa pagkakatanggal|Perang ibinibigay kapag natanggal|He received redundancy pay.
resignation letter|noun|A written notice that you are leaving|liham ng pagbibitiw|Nakasulat na abiso ng pag-alis|She handed in her resignation letter.
resume duties|verb|To start doing something again|magpatuloy|Simulang muli ang gawain|He resumed duties in March.
retirement|noun|The state of having stopped working|pagreretiro|Kalagayan ng pagtigil sa trabaho|Retirement suits him well.
salary|noun|Fixed money paid monthly for work|sahod|Takdang buwanang bayad sa trabaho|The salary is paid on the 25th.
shift|noun|A period of work in a rota|shift|Panahon ng trabaho sa iskedyul|I work the night shift.
sick leave|noun|Time off work because of illness|sick leave|Bakasyon dahil sa sakit|He is on sick leave.
staff meeting|noun|A meeting of the people who work somewhere|pulong ng kawani|Pagtitipon ng mga nagtatrabaho|The staff meeting is at nine.
teamwork|noun|Working effectively with others|pagtutulungan|Mahusay na pagtutulungan|Teamwork made the difference.
trade union|noun|An organisation protecting workers' rights|unyon|Samahang nagtatanggol sa manggagawa|The trade union called a vote.
vacancy for a clerk|noun|A job that is available|bakante|Trabahong bukas para sa aplikante|There is a vacancy for a clerk.
workforce|noun|All the people who work for a company|lakas-paggawa|Lahat ng manggagawa ng kumpanya|The workforce has doubled.
workplace|noun|The place where you do your job|lugar ng trabaho|Lugar ng paggawa|The workplace must be safe.

# ---------------- Money & Finance ----------------
affordability|noun|The state of being cheap enough to buy|abot-kaya|Kalagayan ng pagiging kayang bilhin|Housing affordability has worsened.
allowance|noun|Money given regularly for a purpose|alawans|Perang ibinibigay nang regular|She gets a travel allowance.
arrears|noun|Money owed that should have been paid|atraso|Perang dapat nang nabayaran|The rent is in arrears.
asset value|noun|The worth of something owned|halaga ng ari-arian|Halaga ng pag-aari|Asset value fell last year.
balance of an account|noun|The amount of money in an account|balanse|Perang natitira sa akawnt|Check the balance of your account.
banknote|noun|A piece of paper money|papel de bangko|Papel na salapi|A twenty pound banknote.
borrowing|noun|The act of taking money as a loan|panghihiram|Pagkuha ng pera bilang utang|Borrowing costs have risen.
capital investment|noun|Money used to start or grow a business|puhunan|Perang ginagamit sa negosyo|Capital investment fell sharply.
cash flow|noun|The movement of money in and out|daloy ng salapi|Paggalaw ng pera papasok at palabas|Cash flow is the main worry.
cheque|noun|A written order to a bank to pay money|tseke|Nakasulat na utos sa bangko|Pay by cheque or card.
commission|noun|Money paid for making a sale|komisyon|Perang bayad sa nakabenta|Agents earn commission.
creditor|noun|A person or firm you owe money to|pinagkakautangan|Taong may utang ka|Creditors must be paid first.
debtor|noun|A person who owes money|may utang|Taong may pagkakautang|The debtor agreed a plan.
donation|noun|Money or goods given to help|donasyon|Pera o bagay na ibinigay bilang tulong|The donation funded a new roof.
earnings|noun|Money received from work or investment|kita|Perang natatanggap sa trabaho|Earnings rose by five percent.
economy|noun|The system of money and trade in a country|ekonomiya|Sistema ng pera at kalakalan|The economy is recovering.
exchange rate|noun|The value of one currency against another|palitan|Halaga ng isang pera kumpara sa iba|The exchange rate moved sharply.
expense claim|noun|A request to be repaid for work costs|singil sa gastos|Hiling na mabayaran ang gastos|Submit your expense claim monthly.
fee|noun|Money paid for a service|bayarin|Perang bayad sa serbisyo|The fee includes materials.
finance a purchase|verb|To provide money for something|pondohan|Magbigay ng pera para sa isang bagay|The bank will finance the purchase.
funding|noun|Money provided for a purpose|pondo|Perang ibinibigay para sa layunin|Funding runs out in June.
income tax|noun|Tax paid on money you earn|buwis sa kita|Buwis sa perang kinikita|Income tax is deducted monthly.
inheritance|noun|Money or property received from somebody who died|mana|Pera o ari-ariang minana|She used her inheritance to study.
insolvent|adjective|Unable to pay what you owe|walang kakayahang magbayad|Hindi kayang bayaran ang utang|The firm became insolvent.
interest rate|noun|The percentage charged on a loan|tantos ng interes|Bahagdang sinisingil sa utang|Interest rates have risen again.
investment portfolio|noun|The collection of investments somebody holds|portfolio|Koleksyon ng puhunan ng isang tao|Diversify your investment portfolio.
lender|noun|A person or bank that lends money|nagpapautang|Tao o bangkong nagpapautang|Compare lenders before borrowing.
liability for debt|noun|Legal responsibility for something|pananagutan|Legal na responsibilidad|Limit your liability for debt.
loan agreement|noun|A contract setting out loan terms|kasunduan sa utang|Kontratang naglalaman ng kondisyon|Read the loan agreement.
payment plan|noun|An arrangement to pay in stages|plano ng pagbabayad|Kasunduang bayaran nang paunti-unti|We agreed a payment plan.
pension scheme|noun|A plan providing money after retirement|plano sa pensiyon|Planong nagbibigay ng pera pagkaretiro|Join the pension scheme early.
receipt of payment|noun|Proof that money was paid|resibo|Patunay ng pagbabayad|Keep the receipt of payment.
recession risk|noun|A period when the economy shrinks|panganib ng resesyon|Panahon ng pagliit ng ekonomiya|Recession risk has increased.
refund request|noun|A demand for money to be returned|hiling ng refund|Hiling na maisauli ang pera|Submit a refund request online.
salary increase|noun|A rise in the money paid for work|dagdag sa sahod|Pagtaas ng bayad sa trabaho|She asked for a salary increase.
saving account|noun|A bank account for keeping money|ipunan|Akawnt sa bangko para sa ipon|Open a saving account today.
shares|noun|Units of ownership in a company|sapi|Bahagi ng pagmamay-ari sa kumpanya|He bought shares in the firm.
statement of account|noun|A record of money in and out|pahayag ng akawnt|Talaan ng pera papasok at palabas|Your statement of account arrives monthly.
transaction fee|noun|A charge for moving money|bayad sa transaksyon|Singil sa paglilipat ng pera|A small transaction fee applies.
wages|noun|Money paid weekly for work done|sahod|Lingguhang bayad sa trabaho|Wages are paid on Fridays.
withdrawal|noun|The taking of money out of an account|paglabas ng pera|Pagkuha ng pera sa akawnt|A cash withdrawal costs nothing.

# ---------------- Academic Vocabulary ----------------
abstract idea|noun|Something existing as a thought, not physical|abstraktong ideya|Bagay na nasa isip lamang|Justice is an abstract idea.
accumulate evidence|verb|To gather more and more over time|mangalap|Magtipon nang paunti-unti|Researchers accumulate evidence slowly.
adequate sample|noun|An amount sufficient for a purpose|sapat na sampol|Dami na sapat para sa layunin|Ensure an adequate sample size.
ambiguity|noun|The state of having more than one meaning|kalabuan|Kalagayan ng higit sa isang kahulugan|The wording creates ambiguity.
analogy|noun|A comparison showing similarity|analohiya|Paghahambing na nagpapakita ng pagkakatulad|He drew an analogy with farming.
attribute a cause|verb|To say what caused something|iugnay|Sabihin ang sanhi ng isang bagay|Do not attribute a cause too soon.
categorise|verb|To place things into groups|pag-uri-uriin|Ilagay sa mga pangkat|Categorise the responses first.
coherence|noun|The quality of fitting together logically|pagkakaugnay|Katangian ng lohikal na pagkakabuo|The essay lacks coherence.
concept map|noun|A diagram showing linked ideas|mapa ng konsepto|Diagram ng magkakaugnay na ideya|Draw a concept map to revise.
conclusion drawn|noun|A judgement reached after reasoning|konklusyon|Hatol matapos mangatwiran|State the conclusion drawn clearly.
constitute a majority|verb|To make up or form something|bumuo|Bumuo ng isang bagay|Women constitute a majority here.
correlate|verb|To have a mutual relationship|magkaugnay|Magkaroon ng ugnayan|Income correlates with education.
criterion|noun|A standard used for judging|pamantayan|Sukatang ginagamit sa paghatol|Cost is one criterion.
deduce|verb|To reach a conclusion by reasoning|ihinuha|Bumuo ng konklusyon sa katwiran|We deduce the cause from the data.
denote|verb|To be a sign of something|magpahiwatig|Maging tanda ng isang bagay|The symbol denotes danger.
discourse|noun|Serious discussion or writing on a topic|diskurso|Seryosong talakayan o sulatin|Academic discourse follows rules.
empirical evidence|noun|Knowledge based on observation|empirikal na ebidensya|Kaalamang batay sa obserbasyon|Provide empirical evidence.
framework|noun|A structure of ideas supporting something|balangkas|Istruktura ng ideyang sumusuporta|The theory offers a useful framework.
hierarchy of needs|noun|A system ranking things by importance|hayerarkiya|Sistemang naghahanay ayon sa halaga|Maslow described a hierarchy of needs.
implication of the result|noun|A likely consequence not stated directly|implikasyon|Malamang na bungang hindi tuwirang sinabi|Consider the implication of the result.
inference|noun|A conclusion drawn from evidence|hinuha|Konklusyon mula sa ebidensya|That inference is not supported.
interpret data|verb|To explain the meaning of something|bigyang-kahulugan|Ipaliwanag ang kahulugan|Interpret the data with care.
justify a claim|verb|To show that something is reasonable|patunayan|Ipakitang makatuwiran|You must justify a claim with evidence.
literature review|noun|A survey of existing writing on a topic|repaso ng literatura|Pagsusuri ng nailathala nang sulatin|Begin with a literature review.
methodology used|noun|The set of methods used in research|metodolohiya|Hanay ng paraang ginamit sa pananaliksik|Describe the methodology used.
paradigm shift|noun|A fundamental change in approach|pagbabago ng paradigm|Malaking pagbabago sa pananaw|The discovery caused a paradigm shift.
phenomenon|noun|A fact or event that can be observed|penomeno|Katotohanan o pangyayaring napapansin|The phenomenon is well documented.
qualitative data|noun|Information described in words not numbers|kwalitatibong datos|Impormasyong nakasaad sa salita|Interviews gather qualitative data.
quantitative data|noun|Information expressed in numbers|kwantitatibong datos|Impormasyong nakasaad sa bilang|Surveys produce quantitative data.
rationale|noun|The reasons behind a decision|katwiran|Dahilan sa likod ng desisyon|Explain the rationale for the change.
refute|verb|To prove that something is wrong|pabulaanan|Patunayang mali ang isang bagay|The study refutes that claim.
scope of the study|noun|The range of what is covered|saklaw|Lawak ng sinasaklaw|Define the scope of the study.
subsequent analysis|noun|Something that comes after|kasunod|Bagay na sumusunod|Subsequent analysis confirmed it.
substantiate|verb|To support with evidence|patunayan|Suportahan ng ebidensya|Substantiate every assertion.
synthesise|verb|To combine ideas into a whole|pagsamahin|Pagsamahin ang ideya sa isang kabuuan|Synthesise the main findings.

# ---------------- Common Idioms ----------------
a change of heart|idiom|A change in your opinion or feeling|pagbabago ng isip|Pagbabago ng damdamin o pasya|He had a change of heart at the last minute.
a drop in the ocean|idiom|A very small amount compared with what is needed|patak sa dagat|Napakaliit kumpara sa kailangan|The donation is a drop in the ocean.
a hard nut to crack|idiom|A difficult problem or person|mahirap lutasin|Mahirap na suliranin o tao|That case is a hard nut to crack.
a piece of the action|idiom|A share in something profitable|bahagi sa kita|Bahagi sa kumikitang bagay|Everyone wants a piece of the action.
a slap on the wrist|idiom|A very mild punishment|magaang parusa|Napakagaan na parusa|He got only a slap on the wrist.
against the clock|idiom|Hurrying because time is short|karera sa oras|Nagmamadali dahil kulang ang oras|We worked against the clock.
all ears|idiom|Listening with full attention|nakikinig na mabuti|Buong-atensyong nakikinig|Go on, I am all ears.
an arm and a leg|idiom|A very large amount of money|napakamahal|Napakalaking halaga ng pera|That watch cost an arm and a leg.
at face value|idiom|Accepted as it appears without question|sa unang tingin|Tinatanggap ayon sa nakikita|Do not take the offer at face value.
at loggerheads|idiom|In strong disagreement|hindi magkasundo|Matinding di-pagkakasundo|The two sides are at loggerheads.
back on your feet|idiom|Recovered after illness or trouble|nakabangon|Gumaling matapos ang sakit o gulo|She is back on her feet now.
beat about the bush|idiom|To avoid saying something directly|paligoy-ligoy|Iwasang sabihin nang tuwiran|Stop beating about the bush.
behind the scenes|idiom|Happening privately, not in public|sa likod ng eksena|Nangyayari nang palihim|Much work happens behind the scenes.
bend the rules|idiom|To allow an exception to a rule|lumihis sa patakaran|Payagan ang eksepsyon sa tuntunin|They bent the rules for her.
better off|idiom|In a more fortunate position|mas mabuti ang kalagayan|Nasa mas mabuting kalagayan|You would be better off waiting.
bite your tongue|idiom|To stop yourself from speaking|magpigil sa pagsasalita|Pigilan ang sarili sa pagsasalita|I had to bite my tongue.
blow your own trumpet|idiom|To praise yourself too much|magyabang|Purihin ang sarili nang labis|He never blows his own trumpet.
break new ground|idiom|To do something never done before|magbukas ng bagong landas|Gumawa ng hindi pa nagagawa|The study breaks new ground.
by and large|idiom|Generally speaking|sa pangkalahatan|Sa karamihan ng pagkakataon|By and large, the plan worked.
call the shots|idiom|To be the person in control|magdesisyon|Ang siyang nag-uutos|The finance team calls the shots.
carry weight|idiom|To be influential and taken seriously|may bigat|May impluwensya at pinapakinggan|Her opinion carries weight.
catch someone off guard|idiom|To surprise somebody who is not prepared|mahuli sa hindi handa|Gulatin ang hindi nakahanda|The question caught him off guard.
change your tune|idiom|To change your opinion suddenly|magbago ng tono|Biglang magbago ng opinyon|He soon changed his tune.
clear the air|idiom|To remove tension by discussing openly|linisin ang sitwasyon|Alisin ang tensyon sa bukas na usapan|A quick chat cleared the air.
come to terms with|idiom|To accept a difficult situation|tanggapin|Tanggapin ang mahirap na sitwasyon|She came to terms with the loss.
cut it fine|idiom|To leave yourself very little time|halos maubusan ng oras|Napakakaunting oras ang natira|We cut it fine catching the train.
down the line|idiom|At a later point in the future|sa hinaharap|Sa susunod na panahon|We will review it further down the line.
draw the line|idiom|To set a limit on what you accept|maglagay ng hangganan|Magtakda ng limitasyon|I draw the line at rudeness.
drive a hard bargain|idiom|To negotiate very firmly|magpursigi sa presyo|Makipagtawaran nang mariin|She drives a hard bargain.
fall on deaf ears|idiom|To be ignored completely|hindi pinakinggan|Hindi pinansin|The warnings fell on deaf ears.
find your feet|idiom|To become confident in a new situation|masanay|Maging kumpiyansa sa bagong kalagayan|Give her time to find her feet.
get a grip|idiom|To take control of your emotions|kumalma|Kontrolin ang damdamin|Get a grip and start again.
get your act together|idiom|To become organised and effective|ayusin ang sarili|Maging maayos at epektibo|He needs to get his act together.
give the green light|idiom|To give permission to proceed|magbigay ng pahintulot|Payagang magpatuloy|The board gave the green light.
go down well|idiom|To be received favourably|magustuhan|Maging kaaya-aya sa iba|The speech went down well.
hands are tied|idiom|Unable to act because of rules|walang magawa|Hindi makakilos dahil sa patakaran|My hands are tied on this.
have a soft spot for|idiom|To have special affection for somebody|may pagkagusto|May natatanging pagmamahal|She has a soft spot for stray cats.
hit the ground running|idiom|To start something with great energy|magsimula nang masigla|Simulan nang may lakas|The new manager hit the ground running.
in a nutshell briefly|idiom|Stated in very few words|sa madaling salita|Sa napakaikling pahayag|In a nutshell, we cannot afford it.
in the long run|idiom|Over an extended period of time|sa katagalan|Sa mahabang panahon|It saves money in the long run.
in the same vein|idiom|In a similar manner or style|sa katulad na paraan|Sa parehong estilo|In the same vein, costs must fall.
jump to conclusions|idiom|To decide too quickly without facts|magmadali sa paghusga|Magpasya nang walang sapat na batayan|Do not jump to conclusions.
