keep a low profile|idiom|To avoid attracting attention|magpakababa|Iwasang mapansin|He kept a low profile for months.
keep your options open|idiom|To avoid committing to one choice|magtira ng pagpipilian|Iwasang magpasya kaagad|Keep your options open for now.
know the ropes|idiom|To understand how something works|alam ang gawain|Alam ang paraan ng paggawa|She already knows the ropes.
leave no stone unturned|idiom|To search or try everything possible|maghanap nang lubusan|Subukan ang lahat ng paraan|Police left no stone unturned.
let off steam|idiom|To release anger or energy harmlessly|maglabas ng sama ng loob|Ilabas ang galit nang walang pinsala|Running lets him off steam.
like clockwork|idiom|Working smoothly without problems|walang aberya|Maayos na gumagana|Everything ran like clockwork.
long shot|idiom|Something with little chance of success|malabong tsansa|Bagay na maliit ang tsansa|It is a long shot, but try.
make headway|idiom|To make progress|umusad|Magkaroon ng pag-unlad|We are finally making headway.
miss the point|idiom|To fail to understand the main idea|hindi maintindihan ang punto|Hindi maunawaan ang pangunahing ideya|You have missed the point entirely.
off the top of my head|idiom|Without checking or thinking carefully|sa aking pagkakaalala|Nang hindi tinitingnan|Off the top of my head, about fifty.
on the cards|idiom|Likely to happen|malamang mangyari|Malaki ang tsansang mangyari|A pay rise is on the cards.
on the same wavelength|idiom|Understanding each other easily|magkaintindihan|Madaling magkaunawaan|We are on the same wavelength.
out of the question|idiom|Completely impossible|imposible|Ganap na hindi maaari|A refund is out of the question.
pay lip service|idiom|To say you support something without acting|salita lamang|Sinasabing sumusuporta ngunit walang gawa|They pay lip service to safety.
pick up the pieces|idiom|To restore order after trouble|ayusin ang gulo|Ibalik ang kaayusan matapos ang gulo|She picked up the pieces afterwards.
play devil's advocate|idiom|To argue against for the sake of debate|kontrahin para sa talakayan|Kontrahin upang mapag-usapan|Let me play devil's advocate.
put your foot in it|idiom|To say something embarrassing by accident|makapagsalita ng mali|Makapagsabi ng nakakahiya|I really put my foot in it.
rock the boat|idiom|To cause trouble in a settled situation|manggulo|Gumawa ng gulo sa payapang kalagayan|Do not rock the boat now.
rub salt in the wound|idiom|To make a bad situation feel worse|magdagdag ng sakit|Palalain ang masakit na sitwasyon|Mentioning it rubs salt in the wound.
run its course|idiom|To continue naturally until it ends|matapos nang kusa|Magpatuloy hanggang matapos|Let the illness run its course.
save face|idiom|To avoid public embarrassment|iligtas ang dangal|Iwasan ang kahihiyan sa publiko|He resigned to save face.
second nature|idiom|Something done easily from long practice|likas na|Madaling gawin dahil sa kasanayan|Driving is second nature to her.
set in stone|idiom|Fixed and impossible to change|hindi na mababago|Nakatakda at di-mababago|The date is not set in stone.
sit tight|idiom|To wait patiently without acting|maghintay|Maghintay nang matiyaga|Sit tight until we hear more.
steal the show|idiom|To attract the most attention|maagaw ang atensyon|Makuha ang pinakamaraming pansin|The youngest dancer stole the show.
take it in your stride|idiom|To deal with difficulty calmly|harapin nang kalmado|Harapin ang hirap nang mahinahon|She took the criticism in her stride.
the tip of the iceberg|idiom|A small visible part of a large problem|dulo lamang|Maliit na bahagi ng malaking suliranin|These cases are the tip of the iceberg.
think outside the box|idiom|To think in a new and creative way|mag-isip nang malikhain|Mag-isip sa bagong paraan|We need to think outside the box.
throw light on|idiom|To make something easier to understand|magbigay-linaw|Gawing mas madaling maunawaan|The report throws light on the delay.
turn over a new leaf|idiom|To change your behaviour for the better|magbagong-buhay|Baguhin ang ugali tungo sa mabuti|He turned over a new leaf.
under your belt|idiom|Already achieved and gained|nakamit na|Natamo at naipon na|She has ten years under her belt.
up to scratch|idiom|Of an acceptable standard|sapat ang kalidad|Umaabot sa pamantayan|The work is not up to scratch.
water under the bridge|idiom|Past trouble that no longer matters|lumipas na|Nakaraang gulo na hindi na mahalaga|That is water under the bridge.
weather the storm|idiom|To survive a difficult period|makatawid sa hirap|Makaligtas sa mahirap na panahon|The firm weathered the storm.
with flying colours|idiom|With great success|may tagumpay|Nagtagumpay nang husto|She passed with flying colours.

# ---------------- Proverbs & Sayings ----------------
a chain is only as strong as its weakest link|proverb|A group fails at its weakest point|kasinlakas lamang ng pinakamahina|Nabibigo ang grupo sa pinakamahinang bahagi|Train everyone: a chain is only as strong as its weakest link.
a fool and his money are soon parted|proverb|Careless people quickly lose their wealth|madaling maubos ang pera ng hangal|Mabilis maubos ang salapi ng padalos-dalos|He gambled it all away; a fool and his money are soon parted.
a friend in need is a friend indeed|proverb|True friends help in difficult times|kaibigang tunay ay nakikita sa hirap|Ang tunay na kaibigan ay tumutulong sa gipit|She stayed all night; a friend in need is a friend indeed.
a rolling stone gathers no moss|proverb|People who keep moving gain no attachments|walang naiipon ang laging gumagalaw|Walang naiipon ang taong laging lumilipat|He has lived in six cities; a rolling stone gathers no moss.
absence makes the heart grow fonder|proverb|Being apart increases affection|lalong tumitindi ang pagmamahal sa layo|Lumalalim ang pagmamahal kapag magkalayo|They write daily; absence makes the heart grow fonder.
all good things must come to an end|proverb|Nothing pleasant lasts forever|may hangganan ang lahat ng mabuti|Walang habambuhay na kasiyahan|The holiday is over; all good things must come to an end.
all work and no play makes Jack a dull boy|proverb|Rest is needed as well as work|kailangan ng pahinga at trabaho|Kailangan ang libangan hindi lamang gawain|Take a break; all work and no play makes Jack a dull boy.
an apple a day keeps the doctor away|proverb|Healthy eating prevents illness|malusog na pagkain ay panlaban sa sakit|Ang masustansyang pagkain ay nag-iwas sa sakit|Eat fruit daily; an apple a day keeps the doctor away.
appearances can be deceptive|proverb|Things are not always as they look|mapanlinlang ang anyo|Hindi laging totoo ang nakikita|Check the details; appearances can be deceptive.
beggars cannot be choosers|proverb|Take what you are given when you need help|walang pamimili ang nangangailangan|Hindi makapamili ang humihingi ng tulong|Accept the offer; beggars cannot be choosers.
birds of a feather|proverb|Similar people spend time together|magkakatulad ay nagsasama|Nagsasama ang magkakatulad na tao|They are always together, birds of a feather.
blood is thicker than water|proverb|Family ties are strongest|mas matimbang ang dugo|Mas matibay ang ugnayan ng pamilya|He helped his cousin; blood is thicker than water.
charity begins at home|proverb|Help your own family first|magsimula sa sariling tahanan|Unahin ang sariling pamilya sa pagtulong|Support your parents; charity begins at home.
do not put off till tomorrow what you can do today|proverb|Do things promptly rather than delaying|huwag ipagpaliban ang kayang gawin ngayon|Gawin agad ang kayang gawin|Start now; do not put off till tomorrow what you can do today.
empty vessels make the most noise|proverb|People who know least often talk most|maingay ang walang laman|Madalas maingay ang kakaunti ang alam|Ignore his boasting; empty vessels make the most noise.
familiarity breeds contempt|proverb|Knowing somebody too well reduces respect|nawawalan ng galang sa sobrang pagkakilala|Bumababa ang paggalang sa sobrang lapit|They argue constantly now; familiarity breeds contempt.
fortune favours the bold|proverb|Brave action is often rewarded|pinapaboran ng kapalaran ang matapang|Ginagantimpalaan ang matapang na kilos|Apply for it; fortune favours the bold.
if at first you do not succeed try try again|proverb|Keep trying after failure|subukan muli matapos mabigo|Magpatuloy kahit nabigo|Try once more; if at first you do not succeed, try, try again.
ignorance is bliss|proverb|Not knowing can be more comfortable|kaligayahan ang kamangmangan|Minsan mas payapa ang hindi alam|He never asked; ignorance is bliss.
it takes two to tango|proverb|Both people share responsibility|kailangan ng dalawa|Parehong may pananagutan ang dalawa|Do not blame only her; it takes two to tango.
let bygones be bygones|proverb|Forget past quarrels|kalimutan ang nakaraan|Kalimutan ang lumang alitan|Shake hands and let bygones be bygones.
look on the bright side|proverb|Focus on the positive part|tingnan ang magandang bahagi|Pagtuunan ang positibong bahagi|At least we learned something; look on the bright side.
make hay while the sun shines|proverb|Use a good opportunity while it lasts|samantalahin ang pagkakataon|Gamitin ang pagkakataon habang maaari|Sales are strong, so make hay while the sun shines.
more haste less speed|proverb|Rushing causes mistakes and delay|ang minamadali ay nauuwi sa kabagalan|Ang pagmamadali ay nagdudulot ng mali|Slow down: more haste, less speed.
prevention is better than cure|proverb|Stopping a problem beats fixing it|mas mabuti ang pag-iwas kaysa lunas|Mas mainam ang pag-iwas kaysa gamutin|Service the boiler; prevention is better than cure.
rome was not built in a day|proverb|Important work takes time|hindi natapos sa isang araw ang Roma|Ang mahalagang gawain ay matagal|Be patient; Rome was not built in a day.
the early bird catches the worm early|proverb|Acting first brings advantage|ang maagap ay nakikinabang|Ang unang kumilos ay may kalamangan|Arrive at eight; the early bird catches the worm early.
there is no time like the present|proverb|The best moment to act is now|walang mas mabuti kaysa ngayon|Ngayon ang pinakamainam na panahon|Begin today; there is no time like the present.
two wrongs do not make a right|proverb|Answering a wrong with a wrong helps nobody|hindi nagiging tama ang dalawang mali|Hindi nagiging tama ang paghihiganti|Do not retaliate; two wrongs do not make a right.
you cannot judge a book by its cover|proverb|Do not judge by appearance alone|huwag husgahan sa anyo|Huwag humatol sa panlabas na anyo|Give him a chance; you cannot judge a book by its cover.
you can lead a horse to water but you cannot make it drink|proverb|You cannot force somebody to accept help|hindi mo mapipilit ang ayaw|Hindi mapipilit ang taong ayaw|I offered lessons; you can lead a horse to water but you cannot make it drink.

# ---------------- British Slang ----------------
ace|adjective|Excellent or very good|magaling|Napakahusay|That film was ace.
bants|noun|Playful teasing between friends|biruan|Magaan na biruan ng magkakaibigan|It was just harmless bants.
bloke next door|noun|An ordinary man|lalaki|Karaniwang lalaki|The bloke next door fixed it.
bonkers|adjective|Completely mad or silly|baliw|Ganap na baliw o katawa-tawa|That price is bonkers.
brassed off|adjective|Annoyed and fed up|inis na inis|Sobrang inis|I am brassed off with the delays.
budge over|phrasal verb|To move to make space|umusog|Umusog upang magbigay ng puwang|Budge over and let me sit.
chuffed to bits|idiom|Extremely pleased|masayang-masaya|Sobrang tuwa|She was chuffed to bits with the news.
clanger|noun|An obvious embarrassing mistake|malaking pagkakamali|Halatang nakakahiyang pagkakamali|I dropped a real clanger.
cracking|adjective|Excellent|napakahusay|Napakagaling|We had a cracking time.
dead chuffed|idiom|Very pleased indeed|tuwang-tuwa|Labis na natutuwa|He was dead chuffed with the result.
dosh|noun|Money|pera|Salapi|I am short of dosh this week.
faff|noun|Unnecessary fuss and delay|abala|Di-kailangang abala at antala|Booking online avoids the faff.
gutted about it|idiom|Very disappointed|labis na nadismaya|Sobrang lungkot at dismaya|He was gutted about missing out.
hoover|verb|To clean with a vacuum cleaner|maglinis gamit ang vacuum|Linisin gamit ang vacuum|I need to hoover the lounge.
kerfuffle|noun|A noisy fuss about something small|kaguluhan|Ingay sa maliit na bagay|There was a kerfuffle at the door.
knees-up|noun|A lively party|salu-salo|Masiglang handaan|We had a proper knees-up.
mardy|adjective|Sulky and bad tempered|masungit|Nakasimangot at masama ang timpla|Do not be so mardy.
nick something|verb|To steal something small|magnakaw|Kumuha ng maliit na bagay|Somebody nicked my umbrella.
peckish before lunch|adjective|Slightly hungry|medyo gutom|Bahagyang nagugutom|I feel peckish before lunch.
proper job|idiom|Done well and correctly|maayos na gawa|Nagawa nang maayos|That is a proper job, well done.
pukka|adjective|Genuine and excellent|tunay|Tunay at mahusay|The curry was pukka.
scran|noun|Food|pagkain|Pagkain|Let us get some scran.
skiving off|idiom|Avoiding work or school|nangingikil sa gawain|Umiiwas sa trabaho o pasok|He is skiving off again.
snug|adjective|Warm and comfortable|maginhawa|Mainit at komportable|The cottage is lovely and snug.
sound bloke|idiom|A reliable decent person|mabuting tao|Maaasahan at disenteng tao|Ask Dave, he is a sound bloke.
telly tonight|noun|The television|telebisyon|Telebisyon|Anything good on the telly tonight?
wonky wheel|adjective|Not straight or not working properly|baluktot|Hindi tuwid o sira|The trolley has a wonky wheel.

# ---------------- Everyday Phrases ----------------
after you|idiom|A polite way of letting somebody go first|kayo po muna|Magalang na pagpapauna sa iba|After you, please.
as far as I know|idiom|Based on the information I have|sa pagkakaalam ko|Batay sa aking nalalaman|As far as I know, the shop is open.
as it happens|idiom|Used to introduce a surprising fact|sa pagkakataong ito|Ginagamit sa di-inaasahang katotohanan|As it happens, I was there too.
be my guest|idiom|A way of giving somebody permission|sige lang|Paraan ng pagbibigay ng pahintulot|Want to try? Be my guest.
better safe than sorry indeed|idiom|It is wiser to be careful|mas mabuting mag-ingat|Mas mainam ang pag-iingat|Take the umbrella, better safe than sorry indeed.
by all means|idiom|Certainly, please do|siyempre|Tiyak, gawin lamang|By all means, take a seat.
come to think of it|idiom|Used when you remember something|kung iisipin|Ginagamit kapag may naalala|Come to think of it, she did mention it.
do not mention it|idiom|A polite reply to thanks|walang anuman|Magalang na tugon sa pasasalamat|Do not mention it, I was glad to help.
excuse me|interjection|A polite way of getting attention|paumanhin|Magalang na paraan ng pagtawag-pansin|Excuse me, is this seat free?
fair enough|idiom|Used to accept what somebody says|tama na rin|Ginagamit sa pagtanggap ng sinabi|Fair enough, we will try it your way.
for the time being|idiom|For now, until things change|sa ngayon|Sa kasalukuyan hanggang magbago|Stay here for the time being.
give or take|idiom|Approximately, plus or minus|humigit-kumulang|Halos, may dagdag o bawas|It costs fifty pounds, give or take.
have a word|idiom|To speak briefly with somebody|makipag-usap sandali|Kausapin nang maikli|Can I have a word with you?
help yourself|idiom|An invitation to take what you want|kumuha ka lang|Paanyaya na kumuha ng nais|Help yourself to the biscuits.
how come|idiom|An informal way of asking why|bakit|Impormal na pagtatanong ng bakit|How come you left so early?
I am afraid not|idiom|A polite way of saying no|ikinalulungkot ko|Magalang na pagtanggi|I am afraid not, we are fully booked.
I see what you mean|idiom|Used to show you understand|naiintindihan ko|Ginagamit upang ipakitang naunawaan|I see what you mean about the cost.
if you do not mind|idiom|A polite way of making a request|kung hindi kayo tututol|Magalang na paghingi ng pabor|Could I sit here, if you do not mind?
in any case|idiom|Whatever happens, anyway|sa anumang kaso|Anuman ang mangyari|In any case, we should leave early.
in other words|idiom|Said in a different, clearer way|sa madaling salita|Sinabi sa ibang paraan|In other words, we cannot afford it.
it depends|idiom|The answer varies with circumstances|depende|Nagbabago ang sagot ayon sa sitwasyon|Will you come? It depends.
just in case|idiom|As a precaution|para sigurado|Bilang pag-iingat|Take a coat just in case.
let me know|idiom|Please tell me later|ipaalam mo sa akin|Sabihin mo sa akin mamaya|Let me know if you can make it.
make yourself at home|idiom|An invitation to feel comfortable|magpakalayaw ka|Paanyaya na maging komportable|Come in and make yourself at home.
mind you|idiom|Used to add a qualifying remark|pero|Ginagamit sa pagdaragdag ng paliwanag|It is expensive. Mind you, it lasts.
never mind|idiom|Do not worry about it|hayaan mo na|Huwag mo nang alalahanin|Never mind, we will try tomorrow.
no wonder|idiom|It is not surprising|kaya naman pala|Hindi nakapagtataka|No wonder you are tired.
not at all|idiom|A polite reply meaning you are welcome|walang anuman|Magalang na tugon sa pasasalamat|Thank you so much. Not at all.
on second thoughts|idiom|After reconsidering|kung susuriing muli|Matapos muling isipin|On second thoughts, let us walk.
same here|idiom|I feel or think the same|ako rin|Ganoon din ang nararamdaman ko|I am exhausted. Same here.
sooner or later|idiom|At some point in the future|maaga o huli|Sa hinaharap na panahon|Sooner or later he will find out.
suit yourself|idiom|Do what you want, said with mild annoyance|bahala ka|Gawin mo ang gusto mo|Fine, suit yourself.
take your time|idiom|Do not hurry|dahan-dahan lang|Huwag magmadali|Take your time reading it.
that is a shame|idiom|Used to express mild regret|sayang naman|Ginagamit sa bahagyang panghihinayang|You cannot come? That is a shame.
to be honest|idiom|Used before giving a frank opinion|sa totoo lang|Bago magbigay ng tapat na opinyon|To be honest, I did not enjoy it.
what is more|idiom|Used to add a further point|bukod pa rito|Ginagamit sa pagdaragdag ng punto|It is cheap; what is more, it works.
you are welcome|idiom|A polite reply to thank you|walang anuman|Magalang na tugon sa salamat|Thanks for the lift. You are welcome.

# ---------------- Questions & Responses ----------------
could you repeat that|idiom|A request to hear something again|paki-ulit po|Hiling na ulitin ang sinabi|Sorry, could you repeat that?
do you mind if|idiom|A polite way of asking permission|maaari po ba|Magalang na paghingi ng pahintulot|Do you mind if I open the window?
how do you mean|idiom|A request for clarification|ano po ang ibig sabihin|Hiling ng paglilinaw|How do you mean, exactly?
how does that sound|idiom|A request for an opinion on a plan|ano sa tingin mo|Paghingi ng opinyon sa plano|Friday at seven. How does that sound?
is that right|idiom|Used to check or show interest|ganoon ba|Ginagamit sa pagtiyak o pagpapakita ng interes|You lived in Spain? Is that right?
what do you reckon|idiom|An informal request for an opinion|ano sa palagay mo|Impormal na paghingi ng opinyon|What do you reckon about the price?
would you mind|idiom|A polite way of making a request|maaari po bang|Magalang na paghiling|Would you mind holding this?
absolutely not|idiom|A firm way of refusing|talagang hindi|Mariing pagtanggi|Absolutely not, it is far too risky.
by the way|idiom|Used to introduce a new topic|nga pala|Ginagamit sa pagpapasok ng bagong paksa|By the way, did you post the letter?
come again|idiom|An informal request to repeat|ano ulit|Impormal na hiling na ulitin|Come again? I did not catch that.
fingers crossed|idiom|Expressing hope for good luck|sana|Pag-asa sa magandang kapalaran|Fingers crossed for the results.
go ahead please|idiom|Permission to start or continue|sige po|Pahintulot na magsimula|Go ahead please, I am listening.
I could not agree more|idiom|A strong way of agreeing|lubos akong sang-ayon|Mariing pagsang-ayon|I could not agree more with that.
I doubt it|idiom|Expressing that something is unlikely|malabo|Nagpapahayag ng kawalang-tiwala|Will he come? I doubt it.
I would rather not|idiom|A polite way of declining|mas gusto kong hindi|Magalang na pagtanggi|I would rather not discuss it.
if you say so|idiom|Reluctant agreement|kung sabi mo|Pag-ayon nang may pag-aalinlangan|If you say so, but I am unsure.
let me think|idiom|Asking for a moment to consider|sandali lang|Paghingi ng sandali upang mag-isip|Let me think about it.
not really|idiom|A soft way of saying no|hindi masyado|Malumanay na pagtanggi|Did you like it? Not really.
of course|idiom|Certainly, naturally|siyempre|Tiyak at natural lamang|Of course you may borrow it.
that makes sense|idiom|Used to show something is logical|makatuwiran|Ginagamit sa pagpapakitang lohikal|That makes sense now.
you have got a point|idiom|Acknowledging a good argument|may punto ka|Pagkilala sa mabuting katwiran|You have got a point there.

# ---------------- Collocations ----------------
break a promise|idiom|To fail to do what you promised|sirain ang pangako|Hindi tuparin ang ipinangako|Never break a promise to a child.
break a record|idiom|To do better than any previous result|basagin ang rekord|Malagpasan ang dating tala|She broke the school record.
catch a cold|idiom|To become ill with a cold|magkasipon|Magkaroon ng sipon|I caught a cold on the train.
do business|idiom|To trade with somebody|makipagnegosyo|Makipagkalakalan|We do business across Asia.
do harm|idiom|To cause damage|makapinsala|Magdulot ng pinsala|The rumour did real harm.
do research|idiom|To study a subject carefully|magsaliksik|Mag-aral nang masinsinan|She does research on sleep.
draw a conclusion|idiom|To decide something after thinking|bumuo ng konklusyon|Magpasya matapos mag-isip|Do not draw a conclusion yet.
give an example|idiom|To mention something that shows a point|magbigay ng halimbawa|Banggitin ang nagpapakita ng punto|Can you give an example?
give priority|idiom|To treat something as most important|unahin|Ituring na pinakamahalaga|Give priority to safety checks.
have an effect|idiom|To cause a change|magkaroon ng epekto|Magdulot ng pagbabago|The law had an effect at once.
have an impact|idiom|To influence something strongly|magkaroon ng malaking epekto|Malakas na makaapekto|The article had a real impact.
keep a promise|idiom|To do what you said you would|tuparin ang pangako|Gawin ang ipinangako|He always keeps a promise.
keep a secret|idiom|Not to tell others something private|magtago ng lihim|Huwag ipaalam sa iba|Can you keep a secret?
make a decision|idiom|To choose after considering|magpasya|Pumili matapos isaalang-alang|We must make a decision today.
make a difference|idiom|To have a noticeable effect|magkaroon ng pagbabago|Magdulot ng kapansin-pansing epekto|Small changes make a difference.
make a mistake|idiom|To do something wrongly|magkamali|Gumawa ng mali|Anybody can make a mistake.
make an effort|idiom|To try hard|magsikap|Magpakahusay|Please make an effort to attend.
make progress|idiom|To move towards a goal|umunlad|Umusad patungo sa layunin|The class is making progress.
pay attention|idiom|To listen or watch carefully|magbigay-pansin|Makinig o tumingin nang mabuti|Pay attention to the details.
pay a compliment|idiom|To say something nice to somebody|magbigay ng papuri|Magsabi ng maganda sa iba|He paid her a compliment.
raise a question|idiom|To bring up a matter for discussion|magtanong|Ilahad ang usapin sa talakayan|That raises an important question.
reach an agreement|idiom|To settle a matter together|magkasundo|Magkaisa sa isang usapin|They reached an agreement quickly.
run a business|idiom|To manage a company|magpatakbo ng negosyo|Pamahalaan ang kumpanya|She runs a small business.
save time|idiom|To avoid spending time unnecessarily|makatipid ng oras|Iwasan ang aksayang oras|Booking online saves time.
set a goal|idiom|To decide what you want to achieve|magtakda ng layunin|Itakda ang nais makamit|Set a goal for the term.
take a break|idiom|To rest for a short time|magpahinga|Magpahinga sandali|Let us take a break now.
take a risk|idiom|To do something that may fail|magsapalaran|Gumawa ng bagay na maaaring mabigo|She took a risk and won.
take action|idiom|To do something to solve a problem|kumilos|Gumawa ng hakbang|The council must take action.
take advantage|idiom|To make good use of an opportunity|samantalahin|Gamitin ang pagkakataon|Take advantage of the discount.
take care|idiom|To be careful, or a friendly farewell|mag-ingat|Mag-ingat o pamamaalam|Take care on the icy path.
take part|idiom|To join in an activity|lumahok|Sumali sa gawain|Everyone can take part.
take place|idiom|To happen at a particular time|maganap|Mangyari sa takdang panahon|The wedding takes place in June.
take responsibility|idiom|To accept that something is your duty|managot|Tanggapin ang pananagutan|He took responsibility for the error.
tell the truth|idiom|To say what is real|magsabi ng totoo|Sabihin ang katotohanan|Always tell the truth.
waste time|idiom|To use time badly|mag-aksaya ng oras|Gamitin nang masama ang oras|Do not waste time arguing.

# ---------------- Formal & Informal ----------------
commence|verb|To begin, used formally|magsimula|Simulan, pormal na paggamit|The hearing will commence at ten.
purchase formally|verb|To buy, used formally|bumili|Bumili, pormal na paggamit|Customers may purchase online.
require assistance|verb|To need, used formally|mangailangan|Kailanganin, pormal na paggamit|Do you require assistance?
reside|verb|To live somewhere, used formally|manirahan|Tumira, pormal na paggamit|She resides in Manchester.
request politely|verb|To ask for, used formally|humiling|Humingi, pormal na paggamit|Guests are requested to be quiet.
obtain formally|verb|To get, used formally|makuha|Makuha, pormal na paggamit|Forms may be obtained at reception.
attempt formally|verb|To try, used formally|subukan|Subukan, pormal na paggamit|Do not attempt repairs yourself.
sufficient funds|adjective|Enough, used formally|sapat|Sapat, pormal na paggamit|Ensure sufficient funds are available.
additional charge|adjective|Extra, used formally|karagdagan|Dagdag, pormal na paggamit|An additional charge applies.
prior to departure|idiom|Before, used formally|bago|Bago, pormal na paggamit|Check in prior to departure.
subsequent to the meeting|idiom|After, used formally|matapos|Pagkatapos, pormal na paggamit|Subsequent to the meeting, we agreed.
in the event of|idiom|If something happens, used formally|sakaling|Kung sakaling mangyari|In the event of fire, use the stairs.
with regard to|idiom|About, used formally|hinggil sa|Tungkol sa, pormal na paggamit|With regard to your letter, we agree.
kindly note|idiom|Please be aware, used formally|mangyaring tandaan|Pakitandaan, pormal na paggamit|Kindly note the new opening hours.
we regret to advise|idiom|A formal way of giving bad news|ikinalulungkot naming ipaalam|Pormal na pagbibigay ng masamang balita|We regret to advise the class is full.
yours faithfully|idiom|A formal letter ending when the name is unknown|lubos na gumagalang|Pormal na pangwakas ng liham|Yours faithfully, A. Cruz.
yours sincerely|idiom|A formal letter ending when the name is known|lubos na gumagalang|Pormal na pangwakas kapag alam ang pangalan|Yours sincerely, A. Cruz.
kind regards|idiom|A friendly but professional letter ending|maligayang pagbati|Magiliw ngunit propesyonal na pangwakas|Kind regards, Maria.
get in touch|idiom|To contact somebody, used informally|makipag-ugnayan|Kontakin, impormal na paggamit|Get in touch when you arrive.
sort it out|idiom|To resolve something, used informally|ayusin|Lutasin, impormal na paggamit|We will sort it out tomorrow.
a bit of a problem|idiom|A small difficulty, used informally|kaunting problema|Maliit na suliranin|We have a bit of a problem.
loads of|idiom|A large amount, used informally|napakarami|Napakaraming dami|There are loads of options.

# ---------------- Speaking & Conversation ----------------
accent reduction|noun|Training to soften a regional pronunciation|pagbabawas ng punto|Pagsasanay upang pahinain ang punto|He took accent reduction classes.
banter|noun|Friendly playful conversation|biruan|Magiliw at mapaglarong usapan|The team enjoys light banter.
brainstorm|verb|To produce ideas quickly in a group|magbuo ng ideya|Bumuo ng ideya nang mabilis|Let us brainstorm before deciding.
clarification|noun|An explanation making something clearer|paglilinaw|Paliwanag na nagpapalinaw|May I ask for clarification?
concede a point|verb|To admit that an argument is fair|umamin|Aminin ang tamang katwiran|She conceded a point in the debate.
digress|verb|To move away from the main subject|lumihis|Lumayo sa pangunahing paksa|Sorry, I digress.
elaborate on|phrasal verb|To give more detail about something|magpaliwanag pa|Magbigay ng mas maraming detalye|Could you elaborate on that?
emphasise a point|verb|To give special importance to something|bigyang-diin|Bigyang-halaga ang isang bagay|Let me emphasise a point here.
eye contact matters|noun|Looking directly at somebody's eyes|pagtitinginan|Tuwirang pagtingin sa mata|Eye contact matters in interviews.
filler word|noun|A sound used while thinking what to say|salitang pandagdag|Tunog habang nag-iisip|"Um" is a common filler word.
follow-up question|noun|A question asked after an answer|karugtong na tanong|Tanong matapos ang sagot|She asked a good follow-up question.
icebreaker|noun|Something that starts a conversation easily|pampasimula|Bagay na nagpapasimula ng usapan|A simple icebreaker helps groups relax.
interject|verb|To say something suddenly during a talk|sumingit|Magsalita nang bigla sa usapan|He interjected with a question.
intonation pattern|noun|The rise and fall of the voice|intonasyon|Pagtaas at pagbaba ng tinig|Questions have a rising intonation pattern.
monologue|noun|A long speech by one person|monologo|Mahabang pananalita ng isang tao|The scene opens with a monologue.
mutter|verb|To speak quietly and unclearly|bumulong|Magsalita nang mahina at malabo|He muttered an apology.
persuasive argument|noun|Reasoning that convinces others|nakakakumbinsing katwiran|Katwirang nakakahikayat|She made a persuasive argument.
rapport|noun|A friendly understanding between people|magandang ugnayan|Magiliw na pagkakaunawaan|The teacher built rapport quickly.
rebut|verb|To argue that a claim is false|pabulaanan|Tutulan ang isang paratang|He rebutted the accusation calmly.
rephrase|verb|To say something again in different words|isaayos muli|Sabihing muli sa ibang salita|Let me rephrase the question.
small talk|noun|Polite conversation about unimportant things|maikling usapan|Magalang na usapan sa maliliit na bagay|We made small talk while waiting.
stammer|verb|To speak with involuntary pauses|mautal|Magsalita nang paputol-putol|He stammered slightly when nervous.
tone of voice|noun|The way your voice expresses feeling|tono ng boses|Paraan ng pagpapahayag ng damdamin|Her tone of voice softened.
turn-taking|noun|The pattern of speakers alternating|paghahalinhinan|Padron ng palitan ng nagsasalita|Turn-taking keeps discussion fair.
verbal|adjective|Using spoken words|berbal|Gumagamit ng salitang binibigkas|We had a verbal agreement.

# ---------------- Writing ----------------
adjective clause|noun|A clause that describes a noun|sugnay na panuring|Sugnay na naglalarawan ng pangngalan|An adjective clause adds detail.
byline|noun|The line naming the writer of an article|byline|Linyang nagsasaad ng may-akda|Her byline appeared on page three.
caption|noun|Text explaining a picture|kapsyon|Tekstong nagpapaliwanag ng larawan|Add a caption below each photo.
clarity of writing|noun|The quality of being easy to understand|kalinawan|Katangian ng pagiging malinaw|Clarity of writing wins readers.
cliché|noun|An overused phrase that has lost force|klise|Salitang labis nang nagamit|Avoid clichés in formal essays.
coherent paragraph|adjective|Logically connected and clear|magkaugnay|Lohikal na magkakaugnay|Write a coherent paragraph.
concluding sentence|noun|The final sentence summing up a paragraph|panghuling pangungusap|Huling pangungusap na bumubuod|End with a concluding sentence.
draft version|noun|An early version of a text|burador|Unang bersyon ng sulatin|This is only a draft version.
editorial|noun|An article giving the paper's opinion|editoryal|Artikulong naglalahad ng opinyon|The editorial criticised the plan.
formatting|noun|The way text is arranged on a page|pormat|Paraan ng pagkakaayos ng teksto|Consistent formatting looks professional.
headline|noun|The title of a newspaper article|ulo ng balita|Pamagat ng artikulo sa pahayagan|The headline was misleading.
indentation|noun|Space left at the start of a line|indentasyon|Puwang sa simula ng linya|Use indentation for new paragraphs.
introduction paragraph|noun|The opening part of a piece of writing|panimula|Panimulang bahagi ng sulatin|Keep the introduction paragraph short.
legible|adjective|Clear enough to read|nababasa|Malinaw upang mabasa|Please write legibly.
margin note|noun|A note written at the side of a page|nota sa gilid|Notang nasa gilid ng pahina|She left a margin note.
narrative voice|noun|The perspective a story is told from|tinig ng salaysay|Pananaw kung saan isinasalaysay|The narrative voice is first person.
op-ed|noun|An opinion article by an outside writer|op-ed|Artikulong opinyon ng panlabas na manunulat|He wrote an op-ed on housing.
prose|noun|Ordinary written language, not poetry|tuluyan|Karaniwang sulatin, hindi tula|Her prose is clear and simple.
punchline|noun|The final funny part of a joke|katapusan ng biro|Nakakatawang wakas ng biro|The punchline fell flat.
redraft|verb|To write something again in a new version|isulat muli|Muling isulat sa bagong bersyon|I redrafted the opening twice.
subheading|noun|A smaller heading within a text|subtitulo|Mas maliit na ulo sa loob ng teksto|Break long text with subheadings.
transition word|noun|A word linking ideas between sentences|salitang pang-ugnay|Salitang nag-uugnay ng ideya|"However" is a transition word.
typo|noun|A small typing mistake|tipograpikong mali|Maliit na maling pag-type|The email had one typo.
word count|noun|The number of words in a text|bilang ng salita|Bilang ng salita sa sulatin|Keep within the word count.

# ---------------- Commonly Confused Words ----------------
accept an offer|verb|To agree to receive something|tanggapin|Pumayag na tumanggap|She accepted an offer of help.
adapt to change|verb|To adjust to a new situation|makibagay|Umangkop sa bagong sitwasyon|Children adapt to change quickly.
adopt a method|verb|To take on a practice as your own|gamitin|Gawing sariling paraan|The school adopted a new method.
allude|verb|To refer to something indirectly|magpahiwatig|Banggitin nang hindi tuwiran|He alluded to earlier problems.
elude|verb|To escape from or avoid|makatakas|Makaiwas o makatakas|The answer eludes me.
amoral|adjective|Having no sense of right and wrong|walang moralidad|Walang pakiramdam ng tama at mali|Machines are amoral.
immoral|adjective|Going against accepted morals|imoral|Salungat sa tinatanggap na moralidad|Lying is immoral.
appraise|verb|To assess the value of something|tasahin|Suriin ang halaga|An expert appraised the painting.
apprise|verb|To inform somebody of something|ipaalam|Sabihan tungkol sa isang bagay|Please apprise me of the outcome.
biannual|adjective|Happening twice a year|dalawang beses sa isang taon|Nagaganap dalawang beses kada taon|The biannual review is due.
biennial|adjective|Happening every two years|tuwing dalawang taon|Nagaganap tuwing ikalawang taon|It is a biennial festival.
cite a source|verb|To quote a source as evidence|banggitin|Ibanggit ang pinagkunan|Cite a source for every claim.
site of the building|noun|The place where something is located|lugar|Lugar ng isang bagay|This is the site of the building.
sight of land|noun|The ability to see, or a view|paningin|Kakayahang makakita o tanawin|The sight of land cheered the crew.
complementary colours|adjective|Combining well to form a whole|magkatugma|Nagsasama upang mabuo|The colours are complementary.
complimentary drink|adjective|Given free, or expressing praise|libre|Ibinibigay nang libre|Guests get a complimentary drink.
conscience|noun|The sense of right and wrong|budhi|Pakiramdam ng tama at mali|His conscience troubled him.
conscious decision|adjective|Awake and aware of something|may kamalayan|Gising at may kamalayan|It was a conscious decision.
credible witness|adjective|Able to be believed|kapani-paniwala|Maaaring paniwalaan|She is a credible witness.
credulous buyer|adjective|Too ready to believe things|madaling maniwala|Mabilis maniwala sa lahat|A credulous buyer is easily fooled.
elicit a response|verb|To draw out a reply or reaction|makuha|Kunin ang tugon o reaksyon|The advert elicited a strong response.
illicit trade|adjective|Not allowed by law|labag sa batas|Ipinagbabawal ng batas|Illicit trade harms the economy.
eminent scholar|adjective|Famous and respected|kilala|Tanyag at iginagalang|She is an eminent scholar.
imminent danger|adjective|About to happen very soon|nalalapit|Malapit nang mangyari|They sensed imminent danger.
ensure success|verb|To make certain that something happens|tiyakin|Siguraduhing mangyari|Check twice to ensure success.
assure a client|verb|To tell somebody confidently|tiyakin sa iba|Sabihin nang may katiyakan|I assure you it is safe.
insure a car|verb|To arrange financial protection|magseguro|Ayusin ang proteksyong pinansyal|You must insure a car by law.
imply a meaning|verb|To suggest without saying directly|ipahiwatig|Ipahiwatig nang hindi tuwiran|Are you implying a different meaning?
infer a meaning|verb|To work out a meaning from evidence|hinuhain|Alamin mula sa ebidensya|Readers infer a meaning from context.
practise daily|verb|To do something repeatedly to improve|magsanay|Ulit-ulitin upang gumaling|Practise daily to improve.
principal reason|noun|The main one, or a head teacher|pangunahin|Pinakamahalaga o punong-guro|The principal reason is cost.
principle of fairness|noun|A basic rule or belief|prinsipyo|Batayang tuntunin o paniniwala|We follow the principle of fairness.
prescribe treatment|verb|To recommend a medicine or action|magreseta|Magrekomenda ng gamot o hakbang|Doctors prescribe treatment carefully.
proscribe an activity|verb|To forbid something officially|ipagbawal|Ipagbawal nang opisyal|The rules proscribe that activity.
respectful tone|adjective|Showing regard for others|magalang|Nagpapakita ng paggalang|Use a respectful tone.
respective owners|adjective|Belonging to each one separately|kanya-kanya|Para sa bawat isa|Return them to their respective owners.
sensual|adjective|Relating to physical pleasure|senswal|May kinalaman sa pisikal na kasiyahan|The music has a sensual quality.
sensuous|adjective|Appealing pleasantly to the senses|kaaya-aya sa pandama|Nakalulugod sa pandama|The fabric is sensuous to touch.
stationary vehicle|adjective|Not moving|nakatigil|Hindi gumagalaw|Do not overtake a stationary vehicle.
stationery supplies|noun|Paper and writing materials|panulat|Papel at gamit sa pagsulat|Order more stationery supplies.

# ---------------- Shades of Meaning ----------------
angry|adjective|Feeling strong displeasure|galit|May matinding pagkadismaya|He was angry about the delay.
annoyed|adjective|Mildly angry|inis|Bahagyang galit|She was annoyed by the noise.
enraged|adjective|Extremely angry|nagngingitngit|Sobrang galit|The crowd was enraged.
irate|adjective|Very angry, especially as a customer|galit na galit|Napakagalit lalo na bilang kliyente|An irate caller complained.
pleased|adjective|Feeling happy about something|natutuwa|Masaya sa isang bagay|We are pleased with the result.
thrilled|adjective|Extremely pleased and excited|tuwang-tuwa|Sobrang saya at sabik|He was thrilled by the offer.
overjoyed|adjective|Filled with great happiness|labis na masaya|Punong-puno ng kaligayahan|They were overjoyed at the news.
drained|adjective|Completely without energy|ubos ang lakas|Ganap na walang lakas|She felt drained by evening.
sleepy|adjective|Ready to fall asleep|inaantok|Handa nang makatulog|The child grew sleepy.
worn out|adjective|Extremely tired from effort|hapo|Sobrang pagod sa pagsisikap|He was worn out after the shift.
tiny house|adjective|Extremely small|napakaliit|Sobrang liit|They live in a tiny house.
compact car|adjective|Small and efficiently arranged|siksik|Maliit at maayos ang pagkakaayos|A compact car suits the city.
petite|adjective|Small and slim, of a person|maliit at balingkinitan|Maliit at payat na tao|She has a petite frame.
substantial|adjective|Large in amount or importance|malaki|Malaki ang dami o halaga|A substantial sum was raised.
considerable delay|adjective|Fairly large and noticeable|malaki|Sapat na laki upang mapansin|There was a considerable delay.
enormous cost|adjective|Extremely large|napakalaki|Sobrang laki|The enormous cost shocked us.
decent effort|adjective|Reasonably good|katamtaman|Katanggap-tanggap ang kabutihan|He made a decent effort.
excellent result|adjective|Extremely good|napakahusay|Napakagaling|She achieved an excellent result.
outstanding work|adjective|Clearly better than others|natatangi|Malinaw na nakahihigit|The work is outstanding.
poor quality|adjective|Below an acceptable standard|mahina|Mababa sa pamantayan|The poor quality showed.
awful noise|adjective|Very bad or unpleasant|nakakabinging|Napakasama o di-kaaya-aya|What an awful noise.
appalling conditions|adjective|Shockingly bad|kakila-kilabot|Nakakagulat sa sama|The conditions were appalling.
stroll along|verb|To walk slowly for pleasure|mamasyal|Maglakad nang mabagal|They stroll along the beach.
march|verb|To walk with regular steps|magmartsa|Maglakad nang may sukat na hakbang|The band marched past.
stride|verb|To walk with long confident steps|humakbang|Maglakad nang mahaba at tiwala|He strode into the room.
state clearly|verb|To say something formally and definitely|ipahayag|Sabihin nang pormal at tiyak|Please state clearly your name.
mention briefly|verb|To refer to something in passing|banggitin|Banggitin nang panandalian|She mentioned it briefly.
declare openly|verb|To announce something formally|ipahayag|Ianunsyo nang pormal|He declared openly his support.
glance quickly|verb|To look very briefly|sulyapan|Tingnan nang napakabilis|She glanced quickly at her watch.
gaze|verb|To look steadily for a long time|tumitig|Tumingin nang matagal|He gazed at the horizon.
happy|adjective|Feeling pleasure and contentment|masaya|Nakadarama ng kasiyahan|The children look happy.
cheerful mood|adjective|Noticeably positive and bright|masayahin|Malinaw na positibo|He is in a cheerful mood.
delighted guest|adjective|Very pleased|nagagalak|Labis na natutuwa|The delighted guest thanked us.
elated|adjective|Extremely happy and proud|nagtatagumpay|Sobrang saya at pagmamalaki|She felt elated after winning.

# ---------------- Word Relationships ----------------
antonym pairing|noun|A pair of words with opposite meanings|magkasalungat|Magkasalungat na salita|Hot and cold form an antonym pairing.
compound adjective|noun|An adjective made of two or more words|tambalang pang-uri|Pang-uring binubuo ng dalawang salita|"Well known" is a compound adjective.
connotation of a word|noun|The feeling a word suggests|pahiwatig|Damdaming ipinapahiwatig ng salita|"Cheap" has a negative connotation.
denotation of a word|noun|The literal meaning of a word|tuwirang kahulugan|Literal na kahulugan ng salita|The denotation of a word is in the dictionary.
false cognate|noun|A word that looks similar but differs in meaning|maling katulad|Salitang magkamukha ngunit magkaiba|"Actual" is a false cognate in Spanish.
homograph|noun|A word spelt the same but with another meaning|magkatulad na baybay|Salitang magkatulad ang baybay|"Lead" is a homograph.
idiomatic expression|noun|A phrase whose meaning is not literal|idyomatikong pahayag|Pariralang hindi literal ang kahulugan|Learn idiomatic expressions in context.
polyseme|noun|A word with several related meanings|maraming kahulugan|Salitang maraming kaugnay na kahulugan|"Head" is a polyseme.
register of language|noun|The level of formality in language|antas ng pananalita|Antas ng pormalidad sa wika|Match the register of language to the reader.
root and stem|noun|The base part of a word|ugat at tangkay|Batayang bahagi ng salita|Identify the root and stem first.
semantic field|noun|A group of words about one topic|larangang semantiko|Grupo ng salita sa isang paksa|Colours form a semantic field.
word formation|noun|The way new words are created|pagbuo ng salita|Paraan ng paglikha ng bagong salita|Word formation uses prefixes and suffixes.

# ---------------- Vocabulary & Diction ----------------
archaic term|adjective|No longer used in modern language|makaluma|Hindi na ginagamit ngayon|"Thou" is an archaic term.
bombastic|adjective|Using long words to sound impressive|mabulaklak|Gumagamit ng mabibigat na salita|His speech was bombastic.
concise wording|adjective|Short and clear|maikli at malinaw|Maikli ngunit malinaw|Aim for concise wording.
diction choice|noun|The choice of words in speech or writing|piniling salita|Pagpili ng salita sa pananalita|Her diction choice suits the audience.
euphemistic|adjective|Using mild words for something unpleasant|malumanay|Gumagamit ng malumanay na salita|"Passed away" is euphemistic.
florid|adjective|Excessively decorated in style|mabulaklak|Labis na palamuti sa istilo|The prose is rather florid.
formal register|noun|Language suited to serious situations|pormal na antas|Wikang angkop sa seryosong sitwasyon|Use a formal register in reports.
informal register|noun|Language suited to friends and family|impormal na antas|Wikang angkop sa kaibigan at pamilya|Emails to friends use an informal register.
jargon-free|adjective|Without specialist terms|walang jargon|Walang teknikal na salita|Write jargon-free guidance.
plain English|noun|Clear simple language for everyone|simpleng Ingles|Malinaw at simpleng wika|Government forms use plain English.
redundant phrase|noun|A phrase repeating what is already said|paulit-ulit|Pariralang inuulit ang nasabi na|"Free gift" is a redundant phrase.
tautology|noun|Saying the same thing twice in different words|tautolohiya|Pag-uulit ng ideya sa ibang salita|"A new innovation" is a tautology.
verbosity|noun|The use of more words than needed|kadaldalan|Paggamit ng sobrang salita|Edit out the verbosity.

# ---------------- Communication Qualities ----------------
approachability|noun|The quality of being easy to talk to|pagiging madaling lapitan|Katangian ng pagiging madaling kausapin|Approachability builds trust.
assertiveness|noun|Confidence in stating your needs|pagiging matatag|Tiwala sa paghahayag ng pangangailangan|Assertiveness is not aggression.
attentiveness|noun|The quality of listening carefully|pagiging matulungin|Katangian ng maingat na pakikinig|Attentiveness makes people feel heard.
authenticity|noun|The quality of being genuine|pagiging tunay|Katangian ng pagiging totoo|Audiences value authenticity.
charisma|noun|A personal quality that attracts others|karisma|Katangiang umaakit sa iba|She has natural charisma.
civility|noun|Politeness in speech and behaviour|kagandahang-asal|Kagalangan sa salita at kilos|Debate requires basic civility.
conciseness|noun|The quality of using few words|kaiklian|Katangian ng paggamit ng kakaunting salita|Conciseness respects your reader.
consideration for others|noun|Thoughtfulness about people's feelings|pagsasaalang-alang|Pag-iisip sa damdamin ng iba|Show consideration for others.
directness|noun|The quality of speaking plainly|katapatan|Katangian ng tuwirang pananalita|Her directness is refreshing.
discretion|noun|Care in keeping information private|pag-iingat|Ingat sa pagtatago ng impormasyon|Handle the matter with discretion.
frankness|noun|Honesty in expressing opinions|pagiging prangka|Katapatan sa paghahayag ng opinyon|I appreciate your frankness.
graciousness|noun|Kindness and courtesy towards others|kagandahang-loob|Kabaitan at kagalangan sa iba|She accepted defeat with graciousness.
humility|noun|A modest view of your own importance|kababaang-loob|Mababang pagtingin sa sariling halaga|True experts often show humility.
open-mindedness|noun|Willingness to consider new ideas|pagiging bukas ang isip|Handang tumanggap ng bagong ideya|Open-mindedness aids learning.
patience with learners|noun|The ability to wait calmly|pagtitiyaga|Kakayahang maghintay nang mahinahon|Teaching needs patience with learners.
persuasiveness|noun|The ability to convince others|kakayahang manghikayat|Kakayahang kumbinsihin ang iba|His persuasiveness won the vote.
professionalism|noun|The standard expected in a job|propesyonalismo|Pamantayang inaasahan sa trabaho|She handled it with professionalism.
receptiveness|noun|Willingness to listen and accept ideas|pagiging bukas|Handang makinig at tumanggap|Receptiveness improves teamwork.
responsiveness|noun|The speed of reacting to others|pagiging maagap|Bilis ng pagtugon sa iba|Customers value responsiveness.
sensitivity to tone|noun|Awareness of others' feelings|pagiging maramdamin|Kamalayan sa damdamin ng iba|Sensitivity to tone matters online.
tact|noun|Skill in avoiding giving offence|taktika|Kasanayang iwasang makasakit|He raised it with great tact.
transparency|noun|Openness about actions and reasons|kalinawan|Pagiging bukas sa kilos at dahilan|The public expects transparency.
warmth|noun|Friendly and kind feeling|init ng pakikitungo|Magiliw at mabait na damdamin|She greets everyone with warmth.

# ---------------- Listening & Understanding ----------------
active listening|noun|Listening with full attention and feedback|aktibong pakikinig|Pakikinig nang buong pansin|Active listening reduces conflict.
comprehend fully|verb|To understand something completely|maunawaan|Lubos na maintindihan|I could not comprehend fully at first.
context clue|noun|Information around a word that hints at meaning|palatandaan sa konteksto|Impormasyong nagpapahiwatig ng kahulugan|Use context clues to guess meaning.
decode meaning|verb|To work out what something means|unawain|Alamin ang kahulugan|Readers decode meaning as they go.
gist|noun|The general meaning of something|diwa|Pangkalahatang kahulugan|I got the gist of the talk.
grasp the concept|verb|To understand an idea firmly|maunawaan|Lubos na maintindihan ang ideya|She quickly grasped the concept.
inference from tone|noun|A conclusion drawn from how something is said|hinuha mula sa tono|Konklusyon mula sa paraan ng pagsasalita|Make an inference from tone.
interpretation of meaning|noun|An explanation of what something means|interpretasyon|Paliwanag sa kahulugan|Your interpretation of meaning differs.
misinterpret|verb|To understand something wrongly|maling maunawaan|Mali ang pagkakaintindi|Do not misinterpret my silence.
paraphrasing|noun|Restating something in your own words|paglalahad muli|Muling pagsasabi sa sariling salita|Paraphrasing checks understanding.
perception of the speaker|noun|The way you notice and understand things|pananaw|Paraan ng pagkilala at pag-unawa|Perception of the speaker shapes trust.
recap|verb|To repeat the main points briefly|balikan|Ulitin nang maikli ang mahalagang punto|Let me recap what we agreed.
skim|verb|To read quickly for the main ideas|balasahin|Basahin nang mabilis para sa pangunahing ideya|Skim the article first.
scan for detail|verb|To read quickly looking for specific information|hanapin|Basahin nang mabilis para sa tiyak na bagay|Scan for detail in the table.

# ---------------- Rhetoric & Argumentation ----------------
anecdote|noun|A short personal story used as evidence|anekdota|Maikling kuwentong ginagamit bilang patunay|He opened with an anecdote.
appeal to emotion|noun|Persuasion based on feelings|apela sa damdamin|Panghihikayat gamit ang damdamin|Beware an appeal to emotion.
appeal to authority|noun|Using an expert's word as proof|apela sa awtoridad|Paggamit ng salita ng eksperto|An appeal to authority is not proof.
assertion|noun|A confident statement without proof|paninindigan|Matapang na pahayag na walang patunay|That assertion needs evidence.
bias in reporting|noun|An unfair preference for one side|pagkiling|Di-patas na pagkiling sa isang panig|Watch for bias in reporting.
circular reasoning|noun|Using the conclusion as the reason|paikot na katwiran|Ginagamit ang konklusyon bilang dahilan|That is circular reasoning.
concession in debate|noun|Admitting part of the opposing view|pagsang-ayon|Pagtanggap sa bahagi ng kalaban|Begin with a concession in debate.
counterexample|noun|An example that disproves a claim|kontrang halimbawa|Halimbawang sumasalungat sa paratang|One counterexample defeats the rule.
credibility|noun|The quality of being believed|kredibilidad|Katangian ng pagiging kapani-paniwala|Evidence builds credibility.
false dilemma|noun|Presenting only two options unfairly|maling dilema|Pagbibigay ng dalawang pagpipilian lamang|That is a false dilemma.
generalisation|noun|A broad statement from limited cases|paglalahat|Malawak na pahayag mula sa kakaunting kaso|Avoid sweeping generalisations.
logical fallacy|noun|An error in reasoning|maling katwiran|Kamalian sa pangangatwiran|Spot the logical fallacy.
premise of the argument|noun|A statement an argument is built on|premisa|Pahayag na batayan ng katwiran|Question the premise of the argument.
rhetorical question|noun|A question asked for effect, not an answer|retorikal na tanong|Tanong na hindi naghihintay ng sagot|"Who would not want that?" is rhetorical.
straw man|noun|Misrepresenting an argument to attack it|pekeng argumento|Maling paglalahad upang atakihin|That is a straw man version of my view.
substantiated claim|adjective|Supported by evidence|napatunayan|Suportado ng ebidensya|Only substantiated claims belong here.
valid argument|noun|Reasoning where the conclusion follows|balidong katwiran|Katwirang lohikal ang konklusyon|That is a valid argument.

# ---------------- Pronunciation & Speech ----------------
articulate clearly|verb|To pronounce words distinctly|bigkasin nang malinaw|Bigkasin ang salita nang malinaw|Articulate clearly when presenting.
consonant cluster|noun|Two or more consonants together|kumpol ng katinig|Dalawa o higit pang katinig na magkadikit|"Str" is a consonant cluster.
diphthong|noun|A vowel sound gliding into another|diptonggo|Tunog ng patinig na dumudulas sa iba|"Oi" in "coin" is a diphthong.
elision|noun|Leaving out a sound when speaking|pagkakaltas|Pag-alis ng tunog sa pagsasalita|Elision makes speech faster.
enunciation|noun|The clear pronunciation of words|malinaw na pagbigkas|Malinaw na pagbigkas ng salita|Her enunciation is excellent.
fluency in speech|noun|The ability to speak smoothly|katatasan|Kakayahang magsalita nang maayos|Practice builds fluency in speech.
homophone pair|noun|Words that sound the same but differ in spelling|magkatunog|Salitang magkatunog ngunit magkaiba ang baybay|"Their" and "there" are a homophone pair.
minimal pair|noun|Two words differing in only one sound|minimal na pares|Dalawang salitang isang tunog lang ang kaiba|"Ship" and "sheep" are a minimal pair.
mispronounce|verb|To say a word incorrectly|maling bigkas|Maling pagbigkas ng salita|Many mispronounce that surname.
rhotic accent|noun|An accent where the r is always pronounced|rotikong punto|Puntong palaging binibigkas ang r|American English is largely rhotic.
schwa|noun|The neutral vowel sound in unstressed syllables|schwa|Neutral na tunog ng patinig|The first sound in "about" is a schwa.
silent letter|noun|A letter that is written but not pronounced|tahimik na titik|Titik na nakasulat ngunit hindi binibigkas|The k in "knee" is a silent letter.
stress pattern|noun|Which syllable is said most strongly|diin|Kung aling pantig ang binibigyang-diin|Note the stress pattern in "record".
tongue twister|noun|A phrase that is hard to say quickly|tongue twister|Pariralang mahirap bigkasin nang mabilis|Try that tongue twister slowly.

# ---------------- Useful Verbs ----------------

conceive|verb|To form an idea in the mind|isipin|Bumuo ng ideya sa isip|He conceived the plan overnight.

confer|verb|To discuss, or to give an honour|magtalakayan|Mag-usap o magbigay ng parangal|The judges conferred briefly.
consolidate|verb|To combine things to make them stronger|pagsamahin|Pagsamahin upang lumakas|We consolidated the two teams.

contradict|verb|To say the opposite of what was said|salungatin|Sabihin ang kabaligtaran|The witness contradicted himself.
converge|verb|To come together from different directions|magtagpo|Magsalubong mula sa iba't ibang dako|The roads converge at the bridge.
convict of fraud|verb|To find guilty of a crime|hatulan|Ipahayag na may sala|He was convicted of fraud.
culminate|verb|To reach a final climax|magtapos sa|Umabot sa rurok|The festival culminates in a parade.
curb|verb|To control or limit something|pigilan|Kontrolin o limitahan|Measures to curb spending began.
curtail|verb|To reduce or cut short|paikliin|Bawasan o putulin|The trip was curtailed by illness.
decipher|verb|To work out the meaning of something unclear|unawain|Alamin ang kahulugan ng malabo|I cannot decipher his writing.
delineate|verb|To describe something precisely|ilarawan|Ilarawan nang tumpak|The policy delineates each role.
denounce|verb|To criticise somebody publicly|tuligsain|Punahin nang pampubliko|Leaders denounced the violence.
deplete|verb|To reduce a supply greatly|ubusin|Bawasan nang malaki ang suplay|Fishing has depleted stocks.
deploy|verb|To move resources into position for use|ipakalat|Ilagay sa posisyon para gamitin|Troops were deployed at dawn.
depict|verb|To show or describe something|ilarawan|Ipakita o ilarawan|The painting depicts a harvest.
derive from|verb|To obtain something from a source|magmula|Makuha mula sa pinagmulan|The word derives from Latin.
designate|verb|To choose somebody or something officially|italaga|Piliin nang opisyal|The area is designated a park.
diminish returns|verb|To become less in size or importance|bumaba|Maging mas maliit o mahalaga|Extra effort diminishes returns.
discard|verb|To throw something away|itapon|Alisin ang hindi kailangan|Discard the packaging safely.
disclose information|verb|To make information known|ibunyag|Ipaalam ang impormasyon|Firms must disclose information.
disperse|verb|To spread out over a wide area|kumalat|Magkahiwa-hiwalay sa malawak|The crowd dispersed quietly.
disregard|verb|To ignore something deliberately|balewalain|Sadyang huwag pansinin|He disregarded the advice.
disrupt|verb|To interrupt the normal course of something|guluhin|Antalahin ang normal na takbo|Strikes disrupted the service.
divert|verb|To change the direction of something|ilihis|Palitan ang direksyon|Traffic was diverted round the town.
elevate|verb|To raise something to a higher level|itaas|Iangat sa mas mataas na antas|The role elevates her profile.
embark|verb|To begin something new, or board a ship|magsimula|Simulan ang bago o sumakay|They embarked on a new project.
emerge|verb|To come out or become known|lumitaw|Lumabas o maging kilala|New facts have emerged.
encompass|verb|To include a wide range of things|saklawin|Isama ang malawak na saklaw|The role encompasses training.
endorse a plan|verb|To publicly declare support|suportahan|Ipahayag ang suporta|The board endorsed the plan.
enrich|verb|To improve the quality of something|pagyamanin|Pabutihin ang kalidad|Reading enriches vocabulary.
entrust|verb|To give somebody responsibility for something|ipagkatiwala|Ibigay ang pananagutan sa iba|She entrusted him with the keys.
erode|verb|To wear away gradually|maagnas|Unti-unting masira|Waves erode the cliff.
evoke|verb|To bring a feeling or memory to mind|pukawin|Pukawin ang damdamin o alaala|The song evokes childhood.
exemplify|verb|To be a typical example of something|maglarawan|Maging tipikal na halimbawa|Her career exemplifies persistence.
exert influence|verb|To apply force or influence|gumamit|Gamitin ang lakas o impluwensya|He exerts influence quietly.
facilitate discussion|verb|To make something easier to happen|padaliin|Gawing mas madali|A neutral chair facilitates discussion.
foresee|verb|To predict what will happen|hulaan|Makita ang mangyayari|Nobody could foresee the crash.
forfeit|verb|To lose something as a penalty|mapawalang-bisa|Mawala bilang parusa|They forfeited the match.
formulate a plan|verb|To create something carefully|bumalangkas|Bumuo nang maingat|Formulate a plan before acting.
fortify|verb|To strengthen against attack|patibayin|Palakasin laban sa atake|They fortified the walls.
gauge|verb|To judge or measure something|tantiyahin|Sukatin o hatulan|It is hard to gauge his mood.
harness|verb|To control and use a resource|gamitin|Kontrolin at gamitin ang yaman|We must harness solar power.
heighten|verb|To increase a feeling or effect|patindihin|Palakasin ang damdamin o epekto|Silence heightened the tension.
hinder progress|verb|To make progress difficult|hadlangan|Pahirapan ang pag-unlad|Bad weather hindered progress.
illuminate|verb|To light up, or to make clear|liwanagan|Bigyan ng liwanag o linaw|The study illuminates the issue.
impede|verb|To slow down or block something|hadlangan|Pabagalin o harangan|Debris impeded the rescue.
impart|verb|To give knowledge or a quality|ibahagi|Magbigay ng kaalaman|Teachers impart more than facts.
implicate|verb|To show that somebody is involved|isangkot|Ipakitang may kinalaman|The email implicated two managers.
incur|verb|To bring something unpleasant on yourself|magkaroon|Magdulot sa sarili ng masama|You may incur extra charges.
induce|verb|To persuade or cause something|udyukan|Hikayatin o maging sanhi|Nothing could induce him to speak.
infringe|verb|To break a rule or limit a right|labagin|Sumuway sa tuntunin o karapatan|The design infringes a patent.
inhabit|verb|To live in a place|manirahan|Tumira sa isang lugar|Few people inhabit the island.
inhibit|verb|To prevent something from developing|pigilan|Hadlangan ang paglago|Fear inhibits learning.
instil|verb|To gradually establish an idea in somebody|itanim|Unti-unting itanim ang ideya|Parents instil good habits.
integrate systems|verb|To combine parts into a whole|pagsamahin|Buuin ang mga bahagi|We integrate systems each year.
interrogate a witness|verb|To question somebody thoroughly|usisain|Tanungin nang masinsinan|Police interrogated the witness.
intersect|verb|To cross at a point|magsalubong|Magkrus sa isang punto|The lines intersect here.
invoke|verb|To refer to a rule or right for support|manawagan|Banggitin ang tuntunin bilang suporta|She invoked her right to silence.
lament|verb|To express sadness about something|magdalamhati|Ipahayag ang kalungkutan|He lamented the lost years.
magnify|verb|To make something look larger|palakihin|Gawing mas malaki sa tingin|The lens magnifies the text.
mediate a dispute|verb|To help two sides reach agreement|mamagitan|Tulungang magkasundo ang dalawa|A judge mediated the dispute.
mitigate risk|verb|To make something less severe|pagaanin|Bawasan ang bigat|Insurance mitigates risk.
navigate a system|verb|To find your way through something|tahakin|Hanapin ang daan sa isang bagay|Users must navigate the system.
negate|verb|To make something ineffective|pawalang-saysay|Gawing walang bisa|Delays negate the savings.
nurture|verb|To care for and encourage growth|alagaan|Arugain at palaguin|Good schools nurture curiosity.
obscure|verb|To make something difficult to see|itago|Gawing mahirap makita|Clouds obscured the summit.
optimise|verb|To make something as good as possible|pagbutihin|Gawing pinakamainam|Optimise the layout for phones.
orchestrate|verb|To organise something carefully|isaayos|Ayusin nang maingat|She orchestrated the whole campaign.
outweigh|verb|To be more important than something else|higitan|Maging mas mahalaga|The benefits outweigh the risks.
overshadow|verb|To make something seem less important|malampasan|Gawing mas maliit ang halaga|The scandal overshadowed the win.
penetrate|verb|To go into or through something|tumagos|Pumasok o tumagos|Light penetrates the water.
perpetuate|verb|To make something continue indefinitely|panatilihin|Gawing tuloy-tuloy|Silence perpetuates the problem.
pertain|verb|To be relevant to something|may kinalaman|May kaugnayan sa isang bagay|These rules pertain to staff only.
ponder|verb|To think about something carefully|pagbulayan|Isipin nang mabuti|She pondered the offer for days.
precipitate|verb|To cause something to happen suddenly|magpasimula|Maging sanhi ng biglaang pangyayari|The news precipitated a sell-off.
preclude|verb|To make something impossible|hadlangan|Gawing imposible|Illness precluded her attendance.
presume|verb|To suppose something is true|ipagpalagay|Isiping totoo|I presume you have read it.
proliferate|verb|To increase rapidly in number|dumami|Dumami nang mabilis|Cafés have proliferated here.
propagate|verb|To spread an idea or a plant|palaganapin|Ikalat ang ideya o halaman|The society propagates rare plants.
rectify|verb|To correct something that is wrong|iwasto|Itama ang mali|We will rectify the error today.
reconcile differences|verb|To make two things agree|pagkasunduin|Pagtugmain ang dalawang bagay|We must reconcile the differences.
reiterate|verb|To say something again for emphasis|ulitin|Sabihing muli para sa diin|Let me reiterate the deadline.
relinquish|verb|To give up something willingly|isuko|Bitawan nang kusa|He relinquished the title.
render|verb|To cause something to be in a state|gawing|Gawing nasa isang kalagayan|The blow rendered him unconscious.
replicate|verb|To copy something exactly|gayahin|Kopyahin nang eksakto|Others could not replicate the result.
resent|verb|To feel bitter about something|magsama ng loob|Magdamdam sa isang bagay|He resents the extra work.
reside in the city|verb|To live in a particular place|manirahan|Tumira sa tiyak na lugar|They reside in the city now.
resonate|verb|To have a strong emotional effect|umalingawngaw|Magkaroon ng malalim na epekto|The message resonated widely.
restrain|verb|To hold somebody or something back|pigilan|Hawakan o pigilan|Officers restrained the man.
revoke|verb|To cancel a right or licence|bawiin|Kanselahin ang karapatan o lisensya|His licence was revoked.
scrutinise accounts|verb|To examine something very carefully|suriin|Suriin nang napakaingat|Auditors scrutinise accounts.
signify|verb|To be a sign of something|magpahiwatig|Maging tanda ng isang bagay|A nod signifies agreement.
stipulate|verb|To state a requirement in an agreement|itakda|Tukuyin ang kailangan sa kasunduan|The contract stipulates a notice period.
streamline a process|verb|To make a process more efficient|payabungin|Gawing mas mabisa ang proseso|We streamlined the process.
supersede|verb|To replace something older|palitan|Palitan ang mas luma|This form supersedes the old one.
surpass|verb|To be better than something else|malampasan|Maging mas mahusay|Results surpassed expectations.
suspend judgement|verb|To delay forming an opinion|ipagpaliban|Iurong ang paghatol|Suspend judgement until the facts arrive.
transcend|verb|To go beyond normal limits|malampasan|Lumagpas sa karaniwang hangganan|Music transcends language.
undertake research|verb|To commit to doing a task|magsagawa|Gawin ang isang gawain|The team undertook research.
unveil|verb|To show something new to the public|ilantad|Ipakita ang bago sa publiko|They unveiled the design in May.
uphold standards|verb|To maintain or support something|panatilihin|Panatilihin o suportahan|We uphold high standards.
