wield|verb|To hold and use power or a tool|gamitin|Hawakan at gamitin ang kapangyarihan|She wields real influence.

# ---------------- Work & Career ----------------
accountant|noun|A person who keeps financial records|akawntant|Taong nag-iingat ng talaan ng pera|An accountant prepares the returns.
architect|noun|A person who designs buildings|arkitekto|Taong nagdidisenyo ng gusali|The architect showed us the plans.
baker|noun|A person who bakes bread and cakes|panadero|Taong gumagawa ng tinapay|The baker starts at four.
barber|noun|A person who cuts men's hair|barbero|Taong gumugupit ng buhok ng lalaki|The barber opens at nine.
biologist|noun|A scientist who studies living things|biyologo|Siyentipikong nag-aaral ng buhay|A biologist led the survey.
blacksmith|noun|A person who makes things from iron|panday|Taong gumagawa mula sa bakal|The blacksmith forged the gate.
butcher|noun|A person who sells meat|karnisero|Taong nagbebenta ng karne|Ask the butcher for advice.
carpenter|noun|A person who makes things from wood|karpintero|Taong gumagawa mula sa kahoy|A carpenter built the shelves.
cashier|noun|A person who takes payments in a shop|kahera|Taong tumatanggap ng bayad|The cashier gave me change.
chef|noun|A skilled professional cook|chef|Bihasang propesyonal na kusinero|The chef trained in Paris.
clerk|noun|A person who does office work|klerk|Taong gumagawa ng gawaing opisina|A clerk filed the documents.
consultant|noun|An expert who gives professional advice|konsultant|Ekspertong nagbibigay ng payo|They hired a consultant.
counsellor|noun|A person trained to give guidance|tagapayo|Taong sanay magbigay ng gabay|The counsellor listened carefully.
craftsman|noun|A skilled worker who makes things by hand|manggagawa|Bihasang gumagawa gamit ang kamay|A craftsman repaired the chair.
economist|noun|An expert in how money and trade work|ekonomista|Dalubhasa sa pera at kalakalan|The economist predicted growth.
electrician|noun|A person who installs and repairs wiring|elektrisyan|Taong nag-aayos ng kuryente|Call an electrician for that.
engineer|noun|A person who designs machines or structures|inhinyero|Taong nagdidisenyo ng makina o gusali|The engineer checked the bridge.
farmer|noun|A person who grows crops or keeps animals|magsasaka|Taong nagtatanim o nag-aalaga|The farmer sold at the market.
firefighter|noun|A person trained to put out fires|bumbero|Taong sanay pumatay ng apoy|Firefighters reached the scene fast.
gardener|noun|A person who looks after a garden|hardinero|Taong nag-aalaga ng halamanan|The gardener planted new roses.
geologist|noun|A scientist who studies rocks and the earth|heologo|Siyentipikong nag-aaral ng bato at lupa|A geologist surveyed the site.
journalist|noun|A person who writes news reports|mamamahayag|Taong sumusulat ng balita|The journalist verified the facts.
labourer|noun|A person who does physical work|manggagawa|Taong gumagawa ng pisikal na trabaho|Labourers cleared the site.
lawyer|noun|A person trained in the law|abogado|Taong sanay sa batas|Consult a lawyer first.
lecturer|noun|A teacher at a university|lektyurer|Guro sa unibersidad|The lecturer set weekly essays.
librarian|noun|A person in charge of a library|librarian|Taong namamahala sa aklatan|The librarian found the book.
mechanic|noun|A person who repairs machines and vehicles|mekaniko|Taong nag-aayos ng makina|The mechanic fixed the brakes.
midwife|noun|A person trained to help at births|komadrona|Taong sanay tumulong sa panganganak|The midwife arrived quickly.
optician|noun|A person who tests eyes and sells glasses|optiko|Taong sumusuri ng mata|The optician prescribed new lenses.
paramedic on duty|noun|A person giving emergency medical care|paramediko|Taong nagbibigay ng agarang lunas|A paramedic on duty treated him.
pharmacist|noun|A person qualified to prepare medicines|parmasyutiko|Taong kwalipikadong maghanda ng gamot|The pharmacist explained the dose.
photographer|noun|A person who takes photographs|potograpo|Taong kumukuha ng larawan|A photographer covered the event.
plumber|noun|A person who fits and repairs water pipes|tubero|Taong nag-aayos ng tubo ng tubig|The plumber stopped the leak.
psychologist|noun|An expert in the mind and behaviour|sikologo|Dalubhasa sa isip at asal|A psychologist assessed the case.
receptionist|noun|A person who greets visitors at a desk|resepsyonista|Taong sumasalubong sa bisita|The receptionist took my name.
researcher|noun|A person who studies a subject in depth|mananaliksik|Taong nag-aaral nang malalim|Researchers published the findings.
scientist|noun|A person trained in a science|siyentipiko|Taong sanay sa agham|The scientist repeated the test.
sculptor|noun|An artist who shapes stone or metal|iskultor|Artistang humuhubog ng bato o metal|The sculptor worked in bronze.
secretary|noun|A person who does administrative work|sekretarya|Taong gumagawa ng gawaing administratibo|The secretary arranged the meeting.
surveyor|noun|A person who measures and examines land|surbeyor|Taong sumusukat at sumusuri ng lupa|A surveyor inspected the house.
technician|noun|A person skilled in practical technical work|teknisyan|Taong bihasa sa teknikal na gawain|A technician calibrated the machine.
therapist|noun|A person trained to treat a condition|terapista|Taong sanay gumamot ng kondisyon|The therapist set weekly goals.
translator at the summit|noun|A person who converts writing between languages|tagasalin|Taong nagsasalin ng sulatin|A translator at the summit worked all day.
veterinarian|noun|A doctor who treats animals|beterinaryo|Doktor ng hayop|The veterinarian examined the dog.
waiter|noun|A person who serves food in a restaurant|serbidor|Taong naghahain ng pagkain|The waiter took our order.
welder|noun|A person who joins metal using heat|welder|Taong nagdurugtong ng metal|A welder repaired the frame.

# ---------------- Home & Household ----------------
adhesive|noun|A substance used to stick things together|pandikit|Sangkap na ginagamit sa pagdikit|Use a strong adhesive.
alloy|noun|A metal made by mixing two or more metals|haluang metal|Metal na hinaluan ng ibang metal|Brass is an alloy.
bolt|noun|A metal pin used to fasten things|bolt|Metal na pansara o pandugtong|Tighten every bolt.
brick|noun|A block of baked clay used in building|laryo|Blokeng luwad na ginagamit sa gusali|The wall is solid brick.
bronze|noun|A brown metal made of copper and tin|tanso|Kayumangging metal mula sa tanso at lata|The statue is cast in bronze.
cement|noun|A grey powder that sets hard when wet|semento|Abuhing pulbos na tumitigas kapag basa|Mix the cement thoroughly.
ceramic|noun|A hard material made by baking clay|seramiko|Matigas na materyal mula sa lutong luwad|Ceramic tiles suit bathrooms.
clay|noun|Heavy sticky earth used for pottery|luwad|Mabigat at malagkit na lupa|The pot is shaped from clay.
concrete|noun|A hard building material of cement and stone|kongkreto|Matigas na materyal sa gusali|The floor is concrete.
copper|noun|A soft reddish metal that conducts well|tanso|Malambot na pulang metal|Copper pipes carry the water.
cord|noun|A thick string or electrical wire|lubid|Makapal na tali o kable|Coil the cord neatly.
fibre optic|noun|Thin glass used to carry signals|fiber optic|Manipis na salamin na nagdadala ng senyales|Fibre optic gives faster internet.
foam|noun|A light material full of tiny bubbles|foam|Magaang materyal na puno ng bula|The seat is filled with foam.
glue|noun|A sticky substance for joining things|pandikit|Malagkit na pandugtong|Let the glue dry overnight.
granite|noun|A very hard grey rock|granito|Napakatigas na abuhing bato|The worktop is granite.
hinge|noun|A joint allowing a door to swing|bisagra|Kasukasuang nagbubukas ng pinto|The hinge needs oiling.
insulation|noun|Material that keeps heat or sound in|insulasyon|Materyal na pumipigil sa init o tunog|Loft insulation cuts bills.
lever|noun|A bar used to lift or move something|panungkit|Barang ginagamit sa pag-angat|Use a lever to shift it.
mould|noun|A hollow shape for forming material|molde|Guwang na hugis para sa paghubog|Pour the mix into the mould.
nail|noun|A thin pointed metal pin|pako|Manipis at matulis na metal|Hammer the nail straight.
plaster|noun|A soft mixture that hardens on walls|palitada|Malambot na halong tumitigas sa dingding|The plaster is still wet.
plastic|noun|A light artificial material|plastik|Magaang artipisyal na materyal|Reduce single-use plastic.
plywood|noun|Board made of thin layers of wood glued together|playwud|Tablang gawa sa manipis na patong ng kahoy|Cut the plywood to size.
rope|noun|Thick strong cord|lubid|Makapal at matibay na tali|Tie the rope securely.
rust|noun|The reddish coating formed on wet iron|kalawang|Pulang balot sa basang bakal|Rust has weakened the gate.
screw|noun|A metal pin with a spiral groove|turnilyo|Metal na may paikot na uka|Tighten the screw firmly.
steel|noun|A strong metal made from iron|asero|Matibay na metal mula sa bakal|The frame is steel.
timber|noun|Wood prepared for building|kahoy|Kahoy na inihanda sa pagtatayo|They ordered fresh timber.
tile|noun|A flat piece used to cover a surface|tisa|Patag na piraso na pantakip|Replace the broken tile.
varnish|noun|A clear liquid giving wood a hard shiny finish|barnis|Malinaw na likidong pangkintab sa kahoy|Apply two coats of varnish.
wire|noun|A thin flexible metal thread|kawad|Manipis at nababaluktot na metal|Strip the wire carefully.

# ---------------- Useful Adjectives ----------------
abundant harvest|adjective|Existing in large quantities|masagana|Napakarami ang dami|They had an abundant harvest.
accessible to all|adjective|Able to be reached or used|naaabot|Maaaring puntahan o gamitin|The building is accessible to all.
adaptable approach|adjective|Able to change to suit conditions|nababagay|Kayang baguhin ayon sa kalagayan|Take an adaptable approach.
admirable|adjective|Deserving respect and approval|kahanga-hanga|Karapat-dapat sa paggalang|Her honesty is admirable.
affluent area|adjective|Having plenty of money|mayaman|Maraming pera|They live in an affluent area.
alarming|adjective|Causing worry or fear|nakakaalarma|Nagdudulot ng pag-aalala|The figures are alarming.
ambiguous wording|adjective|Having more than one possible meaning|malabo|May higit sa isang kahulugan|Avoid ambiguous wording.
analogous|adjective|Similar in a way that allows comparison|katulad|Magkatulad upang maihambing|The cases are analogous.
astonishing|adjective|Extremely surprising|kagulat-gulat|Napakanakakagulat|The speed was astonishing.
authoritative|adjective|Reliable because of expertise|makapangyarihan|Maaasahan dahil sa kadalubhasaan|It is the authoritative text.
beneficial effect|adjective|Producing a good result|kapaki-pakinabang|Nagbubunga ng mabuti|Exercise has a beneficial effect.
bewildering|adjective|Very confusing|nakakalito|Napakalito|The choice is bewildering.
brisk|adjective|Quick and energetic|mabilis|Mabilis at masigla|We took a brisk walk.
candid answer|adjective|Honest and direct|prangka|Tapat at tuwiran|I want a candid answer.
coherent argument|adjective|Clear and logically connected|magkaugnay|Malinaw at lohikal|Present a coherent argument.
commendable|adjective|Deserving praise|kapuri-puri|Karapat-dapat purihin|Her effort is commendable.
compelling case|adjective|Very convincing and interesting|nakakaakit|Napakakumbinsi at kawili-wili|They made a compelling case.
compulsive|adjective|Very hard to stop doing|di-mapigilan|Napakahirap ihinto|Checking email became compulsive.
concise summary|adjective|Short and to the point|maikli|Maikli at tuwiran|Write a concise summary.
conducive|adjective|Helping something to happen|nakatutulong|Tumutulong upang mangyari|Quiet is conducive to study.
consistent results|adjective|Always behaving in the same way|pare-pareho|Laging pareho ang kilos|We need consistent results.
convincing evidence|adjective|Able to make somebody believe|nakakakumbinsi|Nakapagpapaniwala|Provide convincing evidence.
cumbersome|adjective|Large and difficult to handle|mabigat|Malaki at mahirap dalhin|The process is cumbersome.
decisive action|adjective|Settling an issue firmly|mapagpasya|Tiyak na nagpapasya|The board took decisive action.
detrimental|adjective|Causing harm|nakapipinsala|Nagdudulot ng pinsala|Stress is detrimental to health.
diligent study|adjective|Showing careful and persistent effort|masipag|Maingat at matiyagang pagsisikap|Diligent study pays off.
disproportionate|adjective|Too large or small in comparison|hindi katimbang|Sobra o kulang kumpara sa iba|The penalty is disproportionate.
elusive|adjective|Difficult to find or achieve|mailap|Mahirap makita o makamit|Success proved elusive.
exhaustive list|adjective|Complete and covering everything|masusi|Kumpleto at saklaw ang lahat|This is not an exhaustive list.
formidable|adjective|Impressive and rather frightening|kakila-kilabot|Kahanga-hanga at nakakatakot|She is a formidable opponent.
futile|adjective|Having no useful result|walang saysay|Walang kapaki-pakinabang na bunga|Further argument is futile.
groundbreaking|adjective|Introducing something completely new|makabago|Nagpapakilala ng ganap na bago|It was groundbreaking research.
holistic|adjective|Considering the whole rather than parts|holistiko|Isinasaalang-alang ang kabuuan|Take a holistic approach.
inclusive|adjective|Including everybody, or covering everything|inklusibo|Kasama ang lahat|The price is fully inclusive.
inconsistent|adjective|Not staying the same|pabagu-bago|Hindi nananatiling pareho|His answers were inconsistent.
ingenious|adjective|Clever and original|matalino|Matalino at orihinal|That is an ingenious solution.
insightful|adjective|Showing deep understanding|may malalim na pag-unawa|Nagpapakita ng malalim na pag-unawa|The comment was insightful.
intriguing|adjective|Very interesting and mysterious|kawili-wili|Napakainteresante at misteryoso|It is an intriguing question.
irreversible|adjective|Impossible to change back|hindi na mababawi|Hindi na maibabalik|The damage is irreversible.
laborious|adjective|Requiring a lot of hard work|nakakapagod|Nangangailangan ng maraming trabaho|Checking each entry is laborious.
lucrative|adjective|Producing a lot of money|kumikita|Nagdudulot ng malaking kita|It is a lucrative contract.
meticulous work|adjective|Very careful about small details|maingat|Napakaingat sa maliliit na detalye|Her meticulous work impressed us.
negligible|adjective|So small as to be unimportant|napakaliit|Napakaliit kaya walang halaga|The difference is negligible.
notable achievement|adjective|Worth paying attention to|kapansin-pansin|Karapat-dapat pansinin|It is a notable achievement.
obsolete equipment|adjective|No longer used or needed|luma na|Hindi na ginagamit|The equipment is obsolete.
paramount|adjective|More important than anything else|pinakamahalaga|Higit sa lahat ang halaga|Safety is paramount.
pivotal|adjective|Of central importance|mahalaga|Sentro ng kahalagahan|She played a pivotal role.
pragmatic approach|adjective|Dealing with things practically|praktikal|Praktikal na pagharap sa bagay|Take a pragmatic approach.
prevalent view|adjective|Common in a particular area or time|laganap|Karaniwan sa isang lugar o panahon|That is the prevalent view.
proficient in Spanish|adjective|Skilled and competent|bihasa|Bihasa at may kakayahan|She is proficient in Spanish.
prohibitive|adjective|So expensive that it prevents action|napakamahal|Napakamahal kaya hindi kayang gawin|The cost is prohibitive.
prudent decision|adjective|Sensible and careful|maingat|Maalam at maingat|That was a prudent decision.
redundant role|adjective|No longer needed|hindi na kailangan|Wala nang silbi|The role became redundant.
relentless|adjective|Never stopping or weakening|walang humpay|Hindi humihinto o humihina|The pressure was relentless.
resilient community|adjective|Able to recover quickly|matatag|Mabilis makabangon|It is a resilient community.
rigorous testing|adjective|Very thorough and strict|masusi|Napakasinsin at mahigpit|The device underwent rigorous testing.
seamless|adjective|Working smoothly with no gaps|walang putol|Maayos at walang agwat|The transfer was seamless.
sceptical|adjective|Doubting that something is true|nag-aalinlangan|Nagdududa sa katotohanan|I remain sceptical about the claim.
stringent rules|adjective|Strict and demanding|mahigpit|Mahigpit at mabigat|The rules are stringent.
tangible benefit|adjective|Real and able to be felt|nadarama|Totoo at nadarama|There is a tangible benefit.
transferable skill|adjective|Able to be used in another situation|nailipat|Magagamit sa ibang sitwasyon|Communication is a transferable skill.
unanimous|adjective|Agreed by everybody|nagkakaisa|Sang-ayon ang lahat|The vote was unanimous.
unprecedented|adjective|Never having happened before|walang kaparis|Hindi pa nangyayari noon|The demand is unprecedented.
viable option|adjective|Able to work successfully|maaaring gawin|Kayang magtagumpay|That is a viable option.
warranted|adjective|Justified by the circumstances|nararapat|May sapat na dahilan|The caution was warranted.

# ---------------- Grammar Rules ----------------
antecedent|noun|The noun that a pronoun refers back to|antesedente|Pangngalang tinutukoy ng panghalip|Every pronoun needs a clear antecedent.
apposition|noun|Two noun phrases placed side by side|apposition|Dalawang pariralang magkatabi|"My brother, a doctor" shows apposition.
article usage|noun|The rules for using a, an and the|paggamit ng pantukoy|Tuntunin sa paggamit ng pantukoy|Article usage troubles many learners.
collective noun|noun|A word for a group of people or things|pangngalang pangkalahatan|Salita para sa isang grupo|"Team" is a collective noun.
comparative form|noun|The form used to compare two things|anyong paghahambing|Anyong ginagamit sa paghahambing ng dalawa|"Bigger" is a comparative form.
complement of the verb|noun|A word completing the sense of a verb|kaganapan|Salitang bumubuo sa diwa ng pandiwa|Identify the complement of the verb.
conditional clause|noun|A clause beginning with if|sugnay na pasakali|Sugnay na nagsisimula sa kung|Underline the conditional clause.
coordinating conjunction|noun|A word joining equal parts of a sentence|pang-ugnay|Salitang nag-uugnay ng pantay na bahagi|"And" is a coordinating conjunction.
countable noun|noun|A noun that can be counted|mabilang na pangngalan|Pangngalang mabibilang|"Chair" is a countable noun.
uncountable noun|noun|A noun that cannot be counted separately|di-mabilang na pangngalan|Pangngalang hindi mabibilang|"Water" is an uncountable noun.
dangling modifier|noun|A phrase that describes the wrong thing|nakalawit na panuring|Pariralang maling bagay ang inilalarawan|Fix that dangling modifier.
definite reference|noun|A reference to something already known|tiyak na pagtukoy|Pagtukoy sa alam nang bagay|"The book" makes a definite reference.
gerund phrase|noun|An -ing form used as a noun|pariralang gerund|Anyong -ing na ginagamit na pangngalan|"Swimming daily" is a gerund phrase.
indirect object|noun|The person who receives the direct object|di-tuwirang layon|Taong tumatanggap ng tuwirang layon|"Her" is the indirect object.
main clause|noun|A clause that can stand alone|punong sugnay|Sugnay na makatatayong mag-isa|Every sentence needs a main clause.
subordinate clause|noun|A clause that depends on another|sugnay na pantulong|Sugnay na nakadepende sa iba|Mark the subordinate clause.
modal auxiliary|noun|A helping verb showing mood or possibility|modal na pantulong|Pandiwang pantulong ng modo|"Should" is a modal auxiliary.
parallel structure|noun|Using the same pattern for related ideas|magkatulad na balangkas|Paggamit ng parehong anyo|Keep parallel structure in lists.
plural form|noun|The form used for more than one|anyong maramihan|Anyong ginagamit sa higit sa isa|The plural form of "child" is "children".
possessive apostrophe|noun|The mark showing ownership|kudlit ng paari|Bantas na nagpapakita ng pagmamay-ari|Use a possessive apostrophe correctly.
prepositional phrase|noun|A phrase beginning with a preposition|pariralang pang-ukol|Pariralang nagsisimula sa pang-ukol|"In the garden" is a prepositional phrase.
pronoun agreement|noun|Matching a pronoun to its noun|pagkakatugma ng panghalip|Pagtutugma ng panghalip sa pangngalan|Check pronoun agreement carefully.
relative pronoun|noun|A pronoun such as who or which|panghalip na pananong|Panghalip tulad ng sino o alin|"Which" is a relative pronoun.
run-on sentence|noun|Two sentences joined without proper punctuation|magkadikit na pangungusap|Dalawang pangungusap na walang tamang bantas|Correct the run-on sentence.
sentence fragment|noun|An incomplete sentence|di-buong pangungusap|Pangungusap na hindi kumpleto|Avoid sentence fragments in essays.
subject-verb agreement|noun|Matching the verb to its subject|pagkakatugma ng simuno at pandiwa|Pagtutugma ng pandiwa sa simuno|Subject-verb agreement is essential.
superlative form|noun|The form showing the highest degree|anyong pasukdol|Anyong nagpapakita ng pinakamataas|"Biggest" is a superlative form.
word order|noun|The arrangement of words in a sentence|ayos ng salita|Pagkakasunod-sunod ng salita|English word order is fairly fixed.

laundry day|noun|The day set aside for washing clothes|araw ng labada|Araw na nakalaan sa paglalaba|Sunday is laundry day.
lie in|noun|A longer sleep than usual in the morning|mahabang tulog|Mas mahabang tulog sa umaga|I had a lie in on Saturday.
meal prep|noun|Preparing food in advance for the week|paghahanda ng pagkain|Paghahanda ng pagkain nang maaga|Meal prep saves time midweek.
morning ritual|noun|A fixed set of actions after waking|gawain sa umaga|Nakatakdang gawain pagkagising|Coffee is part of my morning ritual.
school run|noun|The daily trip taking children to school|paghahatid sa paaralan|Araw-araw na paghahatid sa eskwela|The school run starts at eight.

# ---------------- Numbers & Measurement ----------------
approximation|noun|A figure close to the exact one|tantiya|Bilang na malapit sa eksakto|Give me a rough approximation.
benchmark figure|noun|A standard used for comparison|panukat|Pamantayan sa paghahambing|Use last year as a benchmark figure.
capacity limit|noun|The largest amount something can hold|hangganan ng kapasidad|Pinakamalaking dami na kayang lamanin|The hall has a capacity limit.
census count|noun|An official population count|bilang ng senso|Opisyal na bilang ng populasyon|The census count runs every decade.
circumference|noun|The distance round a circle|sirkumperensya|Layo sa palibot ng bilog|Measure the circumference of the pipe.
consumption rate|noun|The speed at which something is used|antas ng paggamit|Bilis ng paggamit ng isang bagay|The consumption rate has fallen.
conversion factor|noun|A number used to change units|paktor ng pagbabago|Bilang na ginagamit sa pagpapalit ng yunit|Apply the correct conversion factor.
density of population|noun|The amount of something in a space|densidad|Dami sa isang espasyo|The density of population is high.
diameter|noun|A straight line through the centre of a circle|diyametro|Tuwid na linya sa gitna ng bilog|The diameter is ten centimetres.
gradient|noun|The steepness of a slope|dalisdis|Tarik ng isang libis|The gradient is one in five.
increment|noun|A small regular increase|dagdag|Maliit at regular na pagtaas|Pay rises in yearly increments.
interval of five metres|noun|A space between two points|agwat|Puwang sa pagitan ng dalawang punto|Plant them at intervals of five metres.
magnitude of the quake|noun|The measured size of something|lakas|Sukat ng lakas ng isang bagay|The magnitude of the quake was six.
median|noun|The middle value in a set of numbers|median|Gitnang halaga sa hanay ng bilang|The median income rose slightly.
perimeter|noun|The distance round the edge of a shape|perimetro|Layo sa palibot ng isang hugis|Calculate the perimeter first.
radius|noun|The distance from the centre to the edge|radyus|Layo mula gitna hanggang gilid|The radius is half the diameter.
threshold value|noun|The point at which something changes|hangganang halaga|Puntong nagsisimula ang pagbabago|Set a clear threshold value.
tolerance|noun|The allowed variation in a measurement|toleransya|Pinapayagang pagkakaiba sa sukat|The tolerance is one millimetre.

# ---------------- Phrasal Verbs ----------------

dish out|phrasal verb|To give things to many people|mamigay|Mamahagi sa maraming tao|He dishes out advice freely.

factor in|phrasal verb|To include something in a calculation|isama sa kwenta|Isama sa pagkuwenta|Factor in the travel time.
fall back on|phrasal verb|To use as an alternative when needed|umasa sa|Gamitin bilang kapalit|We can fall back on savings.

hinge on|phrasal verb|To depend entirely on something|nakasalalay sa|Ganap na nakadepende|Everything hinges on funding.
hold off|phrasal verb|To delay doing something|ipagpaliban|Iurong ang paggawa|Hold off until we hear more.

lash out|phrasal verb|To attack suddenly with words or force|sumabog|Biglang umatake sa salita o lakas|He lashed out at critics.
level off|phrasal verb|To stop rising or falling|tumigil sa pagbabago|Huminto sa pagtaas o pagbaba|Prices levelled off in May.
map out|phrasal verb|To plan something in detail|balangkasin|Planuhin nang detalyado|Map out your revision.

plough through|phrasal verb|To work through something slowly|magpursigi|Ipagpatuloy kahit mabagal|I ploughed through the reports.

round off|phrasal verb|To finish something pleasantly|tapusin|Tapusin nang kaaya-aya|We rounded off the day with a meal.
scrape through|phrasal verb|To pass with great difficulty|makapasa nang bahagya|Makapasa nang halos hindi|He scraped through the exam.

stumble across|phrasal verb|To find something by chance|makatuklas|Matagpuan nang di-sinasadya|I stumbled across an old letter.

thrash out|phrasal verb|To discuss until a decision is reached|talakayin|Pag-usapan hanggang magpasya|They thrashed out the terms.

# ---------------- Animals & Plants ----------------

locals|noun|The people who live in a place|taumbayan|Mga taong nakatira sa lugar|Ask the locals for directions.
lodging|noun|A place to stay temporarily|tuluyan|Pansamantalang tirahan|Lodging is included in the price.
nature reserve|noun|Protected land for wildlife|reserba ng kalikasan|Protektadong lupa para sa hayop|The nature reserve shelters rare birds.
off-peak|adjective|At a time that is less busy|hindi abala|Sa panahong hindi abala|Off-peak fares are cheaper.
package holiday|noun|A trip with travel and hotel included|pakete ng bakasyon|Biyaheng kasama ang hotel|They booked a package holiday.
passport control|noun|The place where documents are checked|kontrol sa pasaporte|Lugar ng pagsusuri ng dokumento|Passport control was quick.
scenic route|noun|A road with beautiful views|magandang daan|Daang may magandang tanawin|Take the scenic route home.
transit visa|noun|Permission to pass through a country|transit visa|Pahintulot na dumaan sa bansa|You may need a transit visa.
travel insurance|noun|Cover against problems while travelling|seguro sa biyahe|Proteksyon sa suliranin sa biyahe|Buy travel insurance early.
vacancy at the inn|noun|A room that is available|bakanteng silid|Silid na maaaring tuluyan|There is a vacancy at the inn.
wanderlust|noun|A strong desire to travel|hilig maglakbay|Matinding hangaring maglakbay|Wanderlust took her to Peru.

# ---------------- Useful Verbs ----------------

# ---------------- Useful Nouns ----------------
abatement|noun|A reduction in something unpleasant|pagbaba|Pagbawas ng masamang bagay|Noise abatement measures began.
accolade|noun|An award or praise for achievement|parangal|Gantimpala o papuri sa tagumpay|The film received many accolades.
affinity|noun|A natural liking or connection|hilig|Likas na pagkagusto o ugnayan|She has an affinity for languages.
aftermath|noun|The period following a bad event|kahihinatnan|Panahon matapos ang masamang pangyayari|The aftermath lasted years.
ambivalence|noun|Having mixed feelings about something|pag-aalinlangan|Magkahalong damdamin|His ambivalence was obvious.
antidote|noun|Something that counteracts a problem|panlaban|Bagay na sumasalungat sa problema|Exercise is an antidote to stress.
apex|noun|The highest point of something|tuktok|Pinakamataas na bahagi|The apex of the roof leaks.
arbiter|noun|A person with power to settle a dispute|tagapamagitan|Taong may kapangyarihang magpasya|The court is the final arbiter.
attrition|noun|A gradual reduction in numbers|unti-unting pagbaba|Dahan-dahang pagbawas ng bilang|Staff numbers fell by attrition.
autonomy of the region|noun|The right to govern yourself|awtonomiya|Karapatang mamahala sa sarili|The region gained autonomy.
caveat|noun|A warning about a limitation|babala|Babala tungkol sa limitasyon|One caveat applies to the offer.
conjecture|noun|An opinion formed without full evidence|haka-haka|Palagay na walang buong patunay|That is pure conjecture.
conundrum|noun|A confusing and difficult problem|palaisipan|Nakalilitong suliranin|The scheduling is a conundrum.
corollary|noun|A natural result of something else|kaugnay na bunga|Likas na bunga ng ibang bagay|Higher rents are a corollary of demand.
crux|noun|The most important point of a problem|ubod|Pinakamahalagang punto ng suliranin|That is the crux of the matter.
demise|noun|The end or failure of something|pagbagsak|Pagtatapos o pagkabigo ng isang bagay|The demise of the paper shocked staff.
dichotomy|noun|A division between two opposed things|dikotomiya|Paghahati sa dalawang magkasalungat|There is a false dichotomy here.
disparity|noun|A great difference between things|pagkakaiba|Malaking pagkakaiba ng bagay|The pay disparity is stark.
duress|noun|Force or threats used to make somebody act|pamimilit|Pwersa o banta upang mapakilos|The confession was made under duress.
emergence|noun|The process of coming into view|paglitaw|Proseso ng paglabas o paglitaw|The emergence of new evidence changed things.
enigma|noun|Somebody or something mysterious|palaisipan|Taong o bagay na misteryoso|He remains an enigma.
equity in schooling|noun|Fairness in how people are treated|pagkakapantay|Katarungan sa pakikitungo|We work for equity in schooling.
euphemism for dying|noun|A mild word for something unpleasant|malumanay na salita|Malumanay na salita para sa di-kaaya-aya|"Passing" is a euphemism for dying.
fluctuation|noun|An irregular rise and fall|pagbabago-bago|Hindi regular na pagtaas at pagbaba|Price fluctuation is normal.
grievance procedure|noun|A formal way to raise a complaint|paraan ng pagrereklamo|Pormal na paraan ng pagreklamo|Follow the grievance procedure.
impasse|noun|A situation where no progress is possible|impas|Sitwasyong walang pag-unlad|The talks reached an impasse.
imperative to act|noun|Something that must be done|kailangang gawin|Bagay na dapat gawin|There is an imperative to act now.
incentive scheme|noun|Something that encourages an action|insentibo|Bagay na naghihikayat kumilos|The incentive scheme raised output.
inclination|noun|A tendency to do something|hilig|Hilig na gumawa ng isang bagay|I have no inclination to argue.
increment of pay|noun|A regular increase in amount|dagdag|Regular na pagtaas ng dami|Salaries rise by yearly increments of pay.
indicator|noun|A sign showing the state of something|palatandaan|Tandang nagpapakita ng kalagayan|Literacy is a key indicator.
initiative to reduce waste|noun|A new plan to solve a problem|inisyatiba|Bagong planong lutasin ang suliranin|They launched an initiative to reduce waste.
interlude|noun|A short period between two events|pahinga|Maikling panahon sa pagitan ng dalawa|A brief interlude followed.
intricacy|noun|A complicated detail|kaguluhan|Masalimuot na detalye|The intricacies of the law confuse people.
juxtaposition|noun|The placing of two things side by side|pagtatabi|Paglalagay ng dalawang bagay na magkatabi|The juxtaposition is striking.
lapse|noun|A brief failure of memory or standards|pagkukulang|Maikling pagkakamali o pagkalimot|It was a momentary lapse.
mainstay|noun|The most important part of something|haligi|Pinakamahalagang bahagi|Tourism is the town's mainstay.
mandate for change|noun|Authority given by voters|mandato|Kapangyarihang ibinigay ng botante|They claimed a mandate for change.
milestone in her career|noun|An important stage in progress|palatandaan|Mahalagang yugto ng pag-unlad|It was a milestone in her career.
nucleus of the team|noun|The central and most important part|ubod|Sentro at pinakamahalagang bahagi|Three players form the nucleus of the team.
outset|noun|The very beginning of something|simula|Pinakaunang bahagi|Say so at the outset.
periphery|noun|The outer edge of an area|gilid|Panlabas na hangganan ng lugar|Growth is on the periphery.
predicament|noun|A difficult and unpleasant situation|kagipitan|Mahirap at di-kaaya-ayang sitwasyon|We are in a real predicament.
premise for the plan|noun|A statement assumed to be true|premisa|Pahayag na ipinapalagay na totoo|The premise for the plan is flawed.
prerequisite|noun|Something required before something else|kinakailangan|Bagay na kailangan muna|Maths is a prerequisite for the course.
recourse|noun|A source of help in a difficulty|paraan|Pinagkukunan ng tulong|Legal recourse remains open.
repertoire|noun|The range of things somebody can do|repertoryo|Saklaw ng kayang gawin|The band has a wide repertoire.
respite|noun|A short period of rest from something|pahinga|Maikling pahinga mula sa hirap|The rain gave brief respite.
rhetoric of the campaign|noun|Language used to persuade|retorika|Wikang ginagamit sa panghihikayat|The rhetoric of the campaign hardened.
rift|noun|A serious break in a relationship|hidwaan|Malubhang pagkakasira ng ugnayan|A rift divided the family.
sanction against the state|noun|A penalty imposed to force compliance|parusa|Parusang ipinapataw upang mapasunod|Sanctions against the state continue.
scrutiny of the accounts|noun|Careful and critical examination|masusing pagsusuri|Maingat at kritikal na pagsusuri|The accounts face close scrutiny.
semblance|noun|An appearance of something|anyo|Panlabas na hitsura ng isang bagay|Order was restored to some semblance.
stalemate|noun|A situation where neither side can win|patas|Sitwasyong walang makakapanalo|The dispute ended in stalemate.
stimulus|noun|Something causing a reaction or growth|pampasigla|Bagay na nagdudulot ng reaksyon|The grant acted as a stimulus.
subsidy for farmers|noun|Money given to reduce costs|subsidiya|Perang ibinibigay upang bumaba ang gastos|The subsidy for farmers was cut.
synopsis|noun|A brief summary of a story|buod|Maikling buod ng kuwento|Read the synopsis first.
tenet|noun|A principle held to be true|prinsipyo|Prinsipyong itinuturing na totoo|Equality is a central tenet.
transition period|noun|A change from one state to another|panahon ng paglipat|Pagbabago mula sa isang kalagayan|The transition period lasts a year.
upheaval|noun|A great and often violent change|kaguluhan|Malaki at madalas marahas na pagbabago|The country faced political upheaval.
vested interest|noun|A personal reason for wanting an outcome|personal na interes|Personal na dahilan sa isang kinalabasan|He has a vested interest here.
vestige|noun|A small remaining trace of something|bakas|Maliit na natirang bakas|Not a vestige of the wall remains.
watershed moment|noun|A turning point in a situation|mahalagang sandali|Punto ng pagbabago sa sitwasyon|It was a watershed moment.

# ---------------- Common Idioms ----------------

move the goalposts|idiom|To unfairly change the rules|baguhin ang tuntunin|Baguhin ang tuntunin nang di-patas|They keep moving the goalposts.
no strings attached|idiom|With no hidden conditions|walang kondisyon|Walang nakatagong kondisyon|The offer has no strings attached.

# ---------------- Useful Adverbs ----------------

initially planned|adverb|At the beginning|sa simula|Sa unang bahagi|It was initially planned for June.

meticulously|adverb|With great attention to detail|maingat|Nang may masusing pansin|The model is meticulously built.

# ---------------- Meaning & Vocabulary ----------------
acronym formation|noun|Making a word from initial letters|pagbuo ng akronim|Paggawa ng salita mula sa unang titik|NATO shows acronym formation.
back-formation|noun|A word made by removing part of another|likha sa pagbawas|Salitang gawa sa pag-alis ng bahagi|"Edit" is a back-formation from "editor".
blend word|noun|A word made by combining two others|halong salita|Salitang pinagsama ang dalawa|"Brunch" is a blend word.
borrowing from Spanish|noun|A word taken from another language|hiram na salita|Salitang hiniram sa ibang wika|"Mesa" is a borrowing from Spanish.
calque|noun|A phrase translated word for word|salin sa bawat salita|Pariralang isinalin nang tuwiran|"Skyscraper" became a calque in many languages.
clipping|noun|A shortened form of a longer word|pinaikling salita|Pinaikling anyo ng mahabang salita|"Phone" is a clipping of "telephone".
coinage|noun|A newly invented word|bagong salita|Bagong likhang salita|"Selfie" was a recent coinage.
collocate|verb|To occur together often with another word|magsama|Madalas magkasama ang dalawang salita|"Heavy" collocates with "rain".
connotative meaning|noun|The feeling a word carries beyond its definition|pahiwatig na kahulugan|Damdaming dala ng salita|Note the connotative meaning of "cheap".
etymological root|noun|The historical origin of a word|ugat ng salita|Makasaysayang pinagmulan ng salita|Trace the etymological root.
figurative language|noun|Language used in a non-literal way|matalinghagang wika|Wikang hindi literal ang gamit|Poetry relies on figurative language.
frequency list|noun|A ranking of the most used words|listahan ng dalas|Talaan ng pinakamadalas na salita|Study from a frequency list.
gloss|noun|A short explanation of a difficult word|paliwanag|Maikling paliwanag ng mahirap na salita|A gloss appears in the margin.
headword|noun|The word that begins a dictionary entry|pangunahing salita|Salitang nagsisimula ng lahok|Each headword is in bold.
idiolect|noun|The speech habits of one individual|idyolek|Paraan ng pagsasalita ng isang tao|Everyone has an idiolect.
lexical item|noun|A single unit of vocabulary|yunit ng talasalitaan|Isang yunit ng bokabularyo|Treat the phrase as one lexical item.
literal meaning|noun|The exact meaning of the words|literal na kahulugan|Eksaktong kahulugan ng salita|The literal meaning differs from the idiom.
loan translation|noun|A phrase borrowed and translated directly|hiram na salin|Pariralang hiniram at tuwirang isinalin|"Flea market" is a loan translation.
morpheme|noun|The smallest unit of meaning in a word|morpema|Pinakamaliit na yunit ng kahulugan|"Un-" is a morpheme.
neologism in use|noun|A newly created word or phrase|bagong salita|Bagong likhang salita o parirala|"Doomscrolling" is a neologism in use.
pragmatic meaning|noun|Meaning that depends on the situation|kahulugang pragmatiko|Kahulugang nakadepende sa sitwasyon|Consider the pragmatic meaning.
prefixation|noun|The adding of a prefix to a word|paglalagay ng unlapi|Pagdagdag ng unlapi sa salita|Prefixation forms "unhappy".
register shift|noun|A change in level of formality|pagbabago ng antas|Pagbabago sa pormalidad ng wika|Notice the register shift.
semantic change|noun|A shift in a word's meaning over time|pagbabago ng kahulugan|Pagbabago ng kahulugan sa paglipas ng panahon|"Nice" underwent semantic change.
suffixation|noun|The adding of a suffix to a word|paglalagay ng hulapi|Pagdagdag ng hulapi sa salita|Suffixation forms "kindness".
synonymy|noun|The relationship between words of similar meaning|pagkakasingkahulugan|Ugnayan ng magkasingkahulugan|Perfect synonymy is rare.
usage note|noun|A comment on how a word is properly used|paalala sa gamit|Paliwanag sa tamang paggamit|Read the usage note carefully.
word class|noun|The grammatical category of a word|uri ng salita|Kategoryang gramatikal ng salita|Identify the word class first.

# ---------------- Study Skills ----------------
active recall|noun|Testing yourself to strengthen memory|aktibong pag-alala|Pagsubok sa sarili upang tumatag ang alaala|Active recall beats rereading.
cornell notes|noun|A note system splitting the page into sections|Cornell notes|Sistema ng pagsulat na hinahati ang pahina|Cornell notes suit lectures.
cram|verb|To study intensively at the last minute|magpuyat sa pag-aaral|Mag-aral nang matindi sa huling sandali|Do not cram the night before.
distributed practice|noun|Spreading study over many sessions|hati-hating pag-aaral|Paghahati ng pag-aaral sa maraming sesyon|Distributed practice aids retention.
elaboration|noun|Explaining ideas in your own words|pagpapaliwanag|Pagpapaliwanag sa sariling salita|Elaboration deepens understanding.
interleaving|noun|Mixing different topics while studying|paghahalo ng paksa|Paghahalo ng iba't ibang paksa|Interleaving improves transfer.
mind map|noun|A diagram organising ideas around a centre|mind map|Diagram na nag-aayos ng ideya|Draw a mind map for the chapter.
note-taking|noun|The recording of key points while listening|pagsusulat ng tala|Pagtala ng mahahalagang punto|Good note-taking saves revision time.
retrieval practice|noun|Recalling information without looking|pagsasanay sa pag-alala|Pag-alala nang hindi tumitingin|Retrieval practice builds memory.
spaced repetition|noun|Reviewing material at increasing intervals|puwang na pag-uulit|Pag-uulit sa lumalayong agwat|Spaced repetition suits vocabulary.
study group|noun|A small group meeting to learn together|pangkat ng pag-aaral|Maliit na grupong nag-aaral|Join a study group.
time blocking|noun|Setting aside fixed periods for tasks|paghahati ng oras|Paglalaan ng takdang oras sa gawain|Time blocking reduces distraction.

ongoing|adjective|Continuing at the present time|nagpapatuloy|Patuloy sa kasalukuyan|The work is ongoing.

perpetuity|noun|A state that lasts forever|walang hanggan|Kalagayang habambuhay|The land is protected in perpetuity.

transitional|adjective|Relating to a period of change|panlipat|May kinalaman sa panahon ng pagbabago|These are transitional arrangements.

# ---------------- Word Parts & Spelling ----------------
apostrophe misuse|noun|Wrong use of the apostrophe mark|maling gamit ng kudlit|Maling paggamit ng kudlit|Apostrophe misuse is very common.
capitalisation|noun|The use of capital letters|paggamit ng malaking titik|Paggamit ng malalaking titik|Check your capitalisation.
diacritic|noun|A mark added to a letter to change its sound|tuldik|Markang idinaragdag sa titik|Spanish uses a diacritic on "ñ".
digraph|noun|Two letters representing one sound|digrapo|Dalawang titik na iisang tunog|"Sh" is a digraph.
hyphenation|noun|The use of hyphens to join words|paggamit ng gitling|Paggamit ng gitling sa salita|Hyphenation varies between styles.
initialism|noun|An abbreviation said letter by letter|inisyalismo|Daglat na binibigkas isa-isa|BBC is an initialism.
ligature|noun|Two letters joined into one character|ligatura|Dalawang titik na pinagdikit|The "æ" is a ligature.
palindrome|noun|A word reading the same backwards|palindrom|Salitang pareho kung babasahin pabaliktad|"Level" is a palindrome.
phonetic spelling|noun|Spelling that matches pronunciation|ponetikong baybay|Baybay na tugma sa bigkas|Give the phonetic spelling.
punctuation mark|noun|A symbol used to organise writing|bantas|Simbolong nag-aayos ng sulatin|The comma is a punctuation mark.
silent e|noun|A final e that is not pronounced|tahimik na e|Hulihang e na hindi binibigkas|The silent e lengthens the vowel.
spelling rule|noun|A guideline for how words are written|tuntunin sa baybay|Panuntunan sa pagsulat ng salita|Learn the i before e spelling rule.
syllabification|noun|The dividing of a word into syllables|pagpapantig|Paghahati ng salita sa pantig|Syllabification helps pronunciation.
transliteration|noun|Writing a word in another alphabet|transliterasyon|Pagsulat ng salita sa ibang alpabeto|The transliteration varies.

# ---------------- Emotions & Feelings ----------------
apprehensive|adjective|Worried about something that may happen|nag-aalala|Nababahala sa maaaring mangyari|She felt apprehensive before the test.
contented|adjective|Quietly happy with what you have|kontento|Tahimik na masaya sa mayroon|He looked contented in retirement.
crestfallen|adjective|Sad and disappointed|nanlulumo|Malungkot at nadismaya|He looked crestfallen at the news.
disheartened|adjective|Having lost hope or confidence|panghinaan ng loob|Nawalan ng pag-asa o tiwala|Do not be disheartened by one refusal.
exasperated|adjective|Very annoyed after repeated trouble|inis na inis|Sobrang inis matapos ang paulit-ulit|She was exasperated by the delays.
flustered|adjective|Confused and nervous|naguguluhan|Nalilito at kinakabahan|He got flustered under questioning.
indignant|adjective|Angry at something unfair|nagagalit|Galit sa hindi makatarungan|She was indignant at the accusation.
jubilant|adjective|Extremely happy after success|nagagalak|Sobrang saya matapos ang tagumpay|The crowd was jubilant.
melancholic|adjective|Feeling a gentle lasting sadness|malungkutin|May banayad at matagal na lungkot|The music sounds melancholic.
perplexed|adjective|Confused and unable to understand|naguguluhan|Nalilito at hindi makaunawa|I am perplexed by the result.
relieved|adjective|Glad that a worry has ended|nakahinga|Masaya na natapos ang alalahanin|I was relieved to hear it.
resentful|adjective|Feeling bitter about unfair treatment|may hinanakit|May sama ng loob sa di-patas|He remains resentful about the decision.
smug|adjective|Too pleased with yourself|mayabang|Sobrang kuntento sa sarili|Do not look so smug.
wistful|adjective|Sadly thinking about something lost|nangungulila|Malungkot na naaalala ang nawala|She gave a wistful smile.

# ---------------- Speaking & Conversation ----------------
anecdotal evidence|noun|Evidence based on personal stories|patotoong personal|Patunay batay sa personal na kuwento|Anecdotal evidence is not proof.
casual conversation|noun|Relaxed informal talking|kaswal na usapan|Maluwag at impormal na usapan|We had a casual conversation.
closing remark|noun|The final thing said in a talk|panghuling pahayag|Huling sinabi sa isang talumpati|Her closing remark drew applause.
common courtesy|noun|Basic politeness expected of everybody|karaniwang kagandahang-asal|Batayang kagalangan|Saying thank you is common courtesy.
constructive feedback|noun|Criticism intended to help somebody improve|nakabubuting puna|Punang naglalayong makatulong|Offer constructive feedback.
discussion point|noun|A topic raised for people to consider|puntong tatalakayin|Paksang inilalahad para pag-usapan|Add that as a discussion point.
opening line|noun|The first thing said or written|panimulang linya|Unang sinabi o isinulat|The opening line matters most.
public speaking|noun|The skill of talking to an audience|pagsasalita sa publiko|Kasanayan sa pagsasalita sa madla|Public speaking improves with practice.
side note|noun|An extra comment away from the main point|paalala sa gilid|Karagdagang punang hiwalay sa paksa|As a side note, the venue changed.
tactful reply|noun|An answer that avoids causing offence|magalang na sagot|Sagot na umiiwas makasakit|She gave a tactful reply.
verbal agreement|noun|An agreement made in speech, not writing|pasalitang kasunduan|Kasunduang binigkas, hindi isinulat|A verbal agreement is still binding.
